### [1] [Semantic Lenia: Emergence of Homeostatic Solitons within the Semantic Space of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11657)
*Yoshihiko Kayama*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, nlin.CG

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 值得关注 (71.18) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Semantic Lenia 将 LLM 推理从静态优化问题转化为宏观 logit 空间中的连续动力学系统，通过非线性稳态反馈回路平衡语义吸引与句法排斥，涌现出"自主语义孤子"——避免重复结晶化的宏观耗散结构，使生成轨迹保持在混沌边缘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 LLM 推理被视为静态的 token 优化问题，缺乏连续动力学视角。作者希望将人工生命框架引入语言模型，使生成过程成为连续动力学系统，从而在混沌边缘触发深刻的溯因跳跃而不发生结构崩溃。

Method: 建立非线性稳态反馈回路，动态平衡语义吸引与句法排斥；在宏观 logit 空间中定义连续动力学系统；通过穷举参数扫描绘制"宜居脊"（Habitable Ridge），其中施加的引导力与模型内在句法惯性完美平衡。

Result: 成功维持生成轨迹于混沌边缘，涌现出自主语义孤子等宏观耗散结构，避免重复结晶化，并建立了机器认知的物理标度律。

Conclusion: 将 LLM 推理视为连续动力学系统能够触发深刻的溯因跳跃而不导致结构崩溃，为机器认知建立了物理标度律，开辟了连续语言建模与人工生命交叉的新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文将 LLM 推理转化为 logit 空间中的连续动力学系统，属于连续语言建模思想，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"连续语言模型与语言流"高度相关。

Importance reason: Directly targets continuous language model dynamics and flow-based reasoning, aligning strongly with the top-priority subtopic despite being a conceptual framework rather than a benchmark result. Signals: continuous language model, logit-space dynamics, flow matching, edge of chaos, LLM inference

Abstract: We introduce Semantic Lenia, an artificial life framework that transforms Large Language Model (LLM) inference from a static optimization problem into a continuous dynamical system within the macroscopic logit space. By establishing a non-linear homeostatic feedback loop to dynamically balance semantic attraction and syntactic repulsion, we demonstrate the emergence of "Autonomous Semantic Solitons" -- macroscopic dissipative structures that avoid repetitive crystallization. Our exhaustive parameter sweeps map a critical "Habitable Ridge" where applied steering forces perfectly balance the model's intrinsic syntactic inertia. This approach successfully maintains generative trajectories at the edge of chaos, triggering profound abductive leaps without structural collapse and establishing a physical scaling law for machine cognition.

</details>


### [2] [Continuous-Latent Predictive Modeling with Semantic Alignment for EEG-Language Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11656)
*Myeong-Ju Cho, Hye-Bin Shin, Seo-Hyun Lee, Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 普通相关 (61.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 BLPM（Brain Latent Predictive Model），一个 EEG-语言基础模型，将异构 EEG 解码任务重构为连续语义 embedding 预测问题。通过 CELP 编码器进行 latent 目标预测学习可迁移表征，再经 MQSD 模块在共享潜空间中对齐 EEG 表征与文本语义，在多个基准上展现跨任务泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 EEG 基础模型的预训练范式存在关键缺陷：掩码自编码倾向于重建低层信号而非任务相关语义；自回归建模则在连续神经动态与离散 token 空间之间存在不匹配。因此需要新策略来有效对齐连续 EEG 表征与自然语言语义，并使其能与大语言模型集成。

Method: 提出 BLPM 框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) Continuous EEG Latent Predictive (CELP) 编码器，通过 latent 目标预测学习可迁移的连续表征；(2) Multi-Query Semantic Decomposition (MQSD) 模块，提取任务相关信息并根据语义关系在共享潜空间中对齐连续 EEG 表征与文本语义。

Result: 在多个 EEG 基准上验证，BLPM 在多样任务上展现一致的泛化性能，证明连续 latent 语义预测是 EEG-语言基础模型的有效范式。

Conclusion: 连续 latent 语义预测可作为 EEG-语言基础模型的有效预训练范式，能够弥合连续神经信号与语言语义之间的鸿沟，实现跨任务、跨主体的泛化解码。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Brain Latent Predictive Model (BLPM)，将 EEG 解码任务重构为连续语义 embedding 预测问题，在连续 latent 空间中对齐 EEG 表征与自然语言语义。这属于连续 latent 空间中的语言建模与语义预测范式，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"连续语言模型与语言流"（continuous_language_flow）高度相关，故复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Though it applies continuous latent prediction, its EEG-brain-signal domain falls outside your configured research directions and is penalized by negative keywords. Signals: continuous latent prediction, EEG-language foundation model, semantic alignment, latent target prediction, cross-task generalization

Abstract: Recent advances in EEG foundation models have demonstrated the potential of large-scale pretraining to enable generalizable neural decoding across subjects, recording environments, and datasets. However, dominant pretraining paradigms face key challenges: masked autoencoding tends to prioritize low-level signal reconstruction over task-relevant semantics, while autoregressive modeling creates a mismatch between continuous neural dynamics and discrete token spaces. To address these challenges, new strategies are needed to effectively align continuous EEG representations with natural-language semantics and enable their integration with large language models. Accordingly, we propose Brain Latent Predictive Model (BLPM), an EEG-language foundation model that reformulates heterogeneous EEG decoding tasks as a continuous semantic embedding prediction problem. BLPM introduces a Continuous EEG Latent Predictive (CELP) encoder that learns transferable representations through latent target prediction. Building on these representations, a Multi-Query Semantic Decomposition (MQSD) module extracts task-relevant information and aligns continuous EEG representations with textual semantics within a shared latent space according to their semantic relationships. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate consistent generalization performance across diverse tasks, establishing continuous latent semantic prediction as an effective paradigm for EEG-language foundation models.

</details>

### [3] [Self-Evolving Embodied Agents via Skill-Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11350)
*Peidong Wang, Zhiming Ma, Ying Chang, Xufang Luo, Xiaocui Yang, Shi Feng, Yuqing Yang, Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CL

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (81.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SHAPER 是一个免训练的具身智能体自进化框架，冻结模型参数，通过目标环境 rollout 演化可复用技能与上下文代码 harness，使同一冻结模型同时充当规划器与优化器，在 VLABench 和 ESI-Bench 上验证了技能与 harness 优化是具身智能体自进化的实用路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体性能不仅取决于模型权重，还取决于技能、上下文、动作接口与执行 harness。监督微调和强化学习需要额外数据、奖励与训练，而免训练的代码中心方法依赖可编程机器人 API，在固定接口场景下不可用。因此需要一种免训练、不依赖可编程 API 的具身自适应方法。

Method: SHAPER 框架保持模型参数冻结，通过目标环境 rollout 不断演化可复用技能库和上下文代码 harness。同一冻结模型既作为规划器执行任务，又作为优化器反思并改进外部技能与 harness，全程无需参数更新。

Result: 在 VLABench 和 ESI-Bench 上覆盖不同低层动作接口的具身智能体，与纯执行、监督微调及测试时扩展基线（如无验证器选择、投票）对比，SHAPER 取得更优表现。

Conclusion: 技能与 harness 优化是训练昂贵、不可用或不理想时实现具身智能体自进化的实用路线，为免训练具身自适应提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 论文提出 SHAPER 框架，面向具身智能体在固定接口环境下的免训练自适应进化，通过演化技能与上下文代码 harness 提升具身智能体性能，属于具身智能体规划与控制范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Embodied agent self-evolution via skill/harness optimization is tangential to the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: embodied agents, train-free adaptation, skill evolution, harness optimization, VLABench

Abstract: Embodied agents are increasingly built as systems around foundation models, where performance depends not only on model weights but also on the skills, context, action interfaces, and execution harness surrounding the model. While supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning can adapt agents to new environments, they require additional data, rewards, and training runs; meanwhile, many train-free code-centric approaches rely on programmable robot APIs that may be unavailable in fixed-interface settings. We propose SHAPER, a self-evolving framework for train-free embodied adaptation that keeps model parameters frozen and improves the non-parametric agent system by evolving reusable skills and a context-code harness through target-environment rollouts. In SHAPER, the same frozen model can serve as both planner and optimizer, refining its external skills and context-code harness without parameter updates. We evaluate SHAPER on VLABench and ESI-Bench, covering embodied agents with different low-level action interfaces, and compare against pure execution, supervised fine-tuning, and test-time-scaling baselines such as verifier-free selection and voting. Our results suggest that skill-and-harness optimization is a practical route to self-evolving embodied agents when model training is expensive, unavailable, or undesirable.

</details>


### [4] [From Self-Normal-Positioning to Omni-Directional Tracking: Real-Time Surface Modeling Enabled Probe Tilt Control for Robotic Ultrasound Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11409)
*Xihan Ma, Haichong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (67.9) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出一种全方向探头姿态控制框架，集成RGB-D感知、局部表面建模与任务空间方向控制，使机器人超声探头能够跟踪任意非垂直角度，平均角度跟踪误差1.06±0.66度，可恢复高达44.39度的非垂直倾斜角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人超声系统大多只支持探头垂直于局部表面的正常定位，无法满足超声心动图等需要非垂直探头角度的临床检查需求，因此需要实时感知局部表面并保持期望探头方向的控制系统。

Method: 提出全方向探头方向控制框架：融合多视角点云构建局部表面二次模型，将期望成像方向相对于表面法线编码，实现探头对任意角度的跟踪，并通过任务空间方向控制执行。

Result: 在平面跟踪、体模目标角度恢复和专家选定视图的体内跟踪实验中，平均角度跟踪误差为1.06±0.66度，可恢复相对表面法线高达44.39±2.59度的非垂直倾斜角，并在体模和体内实验中成功获取期望心腔视图。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了机器人超声系统能够实时感知局部表面并跟踪任意探头方向，为超声心动图等需要非垂直视角的临床检查提供了可行的自动化方案。

Classification reason: 论文面向机器人超声成像的探头姿态控制，涉及RGB-D感知、局部表面建模与任务空间方向控制，属于具身机器人感知与规划控制范畴，映射到[具身世界模型与规划控制]。

Importance reason: Robotics ultrasound control is outside the user's core focus on continuous/multimodal models and video generation. Signals: robotic ultrasound, probe tilt control, surface modeling, RGB-D perception, world model (weak match)

Abstract: Ultrasound (US) provides real-time, radiation-free imaging, but the image quality depends strongly on how the probe is oriented against the patient body. Robotic US can reduce operator workload and improve acquisition consistency; however, most existing systems focus on normal positioning, where the probe is maintained perpendicular to the local surface. This constraint is inadequate for examinations like echocardiography, where obtaining a diagnostic view requires a non-normal probe angle. Consequently, a clinically useful robotic system must sense the local surface in real-time and preserve the desired probe orientation. Here, we propose an omni-directional probe-orientation control framework that integrates RGB-D perception, local-surface modeling, and task-space orientation control. The surface model fuses multi-view point clouds and provides a quadratic estimate of the local surface. A desired imaging direction is then encoded relative to the normal, enabling the probe to track arbitrary angles. The framework was evaluated through flat-surface tracking, phantom target-angle recovery, and in-vivo tracking of an expert selected view. Results show that the mean angular tracking error was 1.06 +- 0.66 deg. The system recovered a non-normal tilt angle of up to 44.39 +- 2.59 deg relative to the surface normal, and acquired the desired heart chamber view in the phantom and in-vivo experiments.

</details>


### [5] [Keep the Future, Drop the Rollout: RIFT for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11521)
*Chushan Zhang, Jinguang Tong, Xuesong Li, Yikai Wang, Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (90.8) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究世界动作模型（WAM）中动作生成是否依赖迭代视频rollout轨迹还是仅需其未来表征。通过LIBERO上的配对闭环干预实验发现，固定最终K/V缓存可近乎保持执行效果，据此提出RIFT方法，用学习到的预期token在一次骨干网络前向中构建完整未来K/V缓存，实现免rollout的未来条件动作生成，在LIBERO上达到98.8%成功率并大幅降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）通过预测未来视频来条件化机器人动作，但迭代式视频rollout在部署时引入显著延迟。作者质疑动作生成是否真的需要逐步演化的rollout轨迹，还是仅需其未来表征即可，旨在消除部署时的迭代视频生成开销。

Method: 首先通过配对闭环干预实验分析四种WAM在LIBERO全部40个任务上的行为，验证未来缓存值及其位置对执行的影响。发现Joint和Cosmos-2可复用固定最终K/V缓存。据此提出RIFT（Rollout-free Imagination via Future Tokens），使用学习到的预期token在一次骨干网络前向中构建完整未来K/V缓存，同时保留原始未来读取接口。

Result: 在LIBERO上RIFT达到98.8%成功率，接近基于rollout的Joint、IDM和LingBot-VA（98.4%-98.6%），同时将动作块延迟降低68.2%-89.1%。在RoboTwin 2.0上，RIFT在干净/随机场景分别达到92.9%/92.6%成功率，为评估方法中最高。

Conclusion: 研究表明未来条件动作生成可以在部署时无需迭代视频生成，通过一次前向构建未来K/V缓存即可实现。这支持了免rollout的未来条件范式，为世界动作模型的高效部署提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 论文研究世界动作模型（WAM）中动作生成与未来预测的关系，提出免rollout的未来条件机制，属于面向机器人动作规划与控制的世界模型范畴，映射到具身世界模型与规划控制这一CANONICAL子主题。

Importance reason: Directly advances world-model-based action generation with a rollout-free future-conditioning method, aligning with the user's world model and efficiency priorities. Signals: world model, neural simulator, efficient inference, video generation, autoregressive

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) condition robot actions on predicted futures, but iterative video rollout increases deployment latency. We ask whether action generation requires the evolving rollout trajectory or only its future representation. Across four WAMs on all 40 LIBERO tasks, paired closed-loop interventions show that masking or reassigning future-cache values changes execution and reduces success, indicating sensitivity to future values and their assigned positions. For Joint and Cosmos-2, however, replaying one fixed final-clean key/value (K/V) cache nearly preserves unmodified execution, with $1.7$ to $1.9$~cm end-effector average displacement error and $97.9\%$ to $98.2\%$ success. This separates cache consumption from production: these models can reuse a fixed cache but still require iterative rollout to construct it. We therefore propose RIFT (\emph{Rollout-free Imagination via Future Tokens}), which uses learned anticipation tokens to construct a complete future K/V cache in one backbone pass while retaining the original future-read interface. On LIBERO, RIFT achieves $98.8\%$ success, close to rollout-based Joint, IDM, and LingBot-VA at $98.4\%$ to $98.6\%$, while reducing action-chunk latency by $68.2\%$ to $89.1\%$. On RoboTwin~2.0, RIFT reaches $92.9/92.6\%$ on clean/randomized scenes, the highest observed among the evaluated methods. These results support rollout-free future conditioning without iterative video generation at deployment.

</details>


### [6] [Video2Track: From Real-World Interaction Videos to Steerable Adversarial Closed-Track Testing for Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11592)
*Mengjie Tian, Xinrui Zhang, Tianyu Li, Peizhi Zhang, Guirong Zhou, Haojie Feng, Junpeng Huang, Qixiang Zhang, Lu Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (60.95) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Video2Track 提出一个将真实道路交互视频转化为可操控的对抗性封闭赛道测试框架，用于自动驾驶系统验证。框架包含场景语义映射模块（用VLM提取视频语义并通过RAG映射到赛道拓扑库）和动态交互测试模块（用条件扩散模型生成多智能体轨迹，并通过Stackelberg博弈调节交互强度）。实验表明能忠实复现真实交互场景并生成可控风险等级与交互风格的变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶封闭赛道测试大多依赖标准化协议或预定义轨迹，导致交互过于脚本化，难以复现公共道路交通的自然复杂性，缺乏可扩展的真实场景验证手段。

Method: 提出 Video2Track 框架，包含两个模块：(1) 场景语义映射模块，使用视觉语言模型从驾驶视频提取结构化语义，并通过检索增强生成（RAG）将其映射到封闭赛道拓扑库，识别兼容地图段与交互锚点；(2) 动态交互测试模块，基于映射的拓扑与锚点，通过条件扩散模型生成多样化的多智能体轨迹，并利用带参数化对抗目标的 Stackelberg 博弈调节交互强度。

Result: 封闭赛道实验表明，该框架能忠实复现代表性真实世界交互场景，并生成具有可控风险等级与交互风格的可执行场景变体，为真实且可操控的自动驾驶验证提供了可扩展方案。

Conclusion: Video2Track 通过将真实视频场景转化为可操控的对抗性封闭赛道测试，弥合了真实道路复杂性与封闭测试可控性之间的鸿沟，为自动驾驶系统的安全验证提供了可扩展且可调节的测试方法。

Classification reason: 论文面向自动驾驶系统（ADS）的封闭赛道测试，通过视频提取真实交互场景并生成多智能体对抗轨迹，属于具身智能/自动驾驶场景下的世界建模与测试验证，映射到具身世界模型与规划控制这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on ADS closed-track testing via VLM and diffusion, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: world model, diffusion model, VLM, RAG, Stackelberg game

Abstract: Closed-track testing plays a fundamental role in the verification and validation of automated driving systems (ADS), particularly for safety-critical scenarios, by enabling reproducible evaluation under controlled conditions. However, most existing approaches still rely on standardized protocols or predefined trajectories, leading to overly scripted interactions and limited ability to reproduce the natural complexity of public-road traffic. To address this limitation, we propose Video2Track, a framework that transfers real-world interactive driving scenarios from videos into steerable adversarial closed-track testing. The framework consists of two tightly coupled modules. The first is a scenario semantic mapping module, which extracts structured semantics from driving videos using a vision-language model and grounds them onto a closed-track topology library via retrieval-augmented generation, thereby identifying compatible map segments and interaction anchors. The second is a dynamic interactive testing module, which conditions on the grounded topology and anchors to generate diverse multi-agent trajectories through a conditional diffusion model, while regulating interaction intensity via a Stackelberg game with a parameterized adversarial objective. Closed-track experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework can faithfully reproduce representative real-world interaction scenarios and generate executable scenario variants with controllable risk levels and interaction styles, providing a scalable approach for realistic and steerable ADS validation.

</details>

Abstract: As smart port infrastructures increasingly rely on autonomous maritime devices enabled by the Internet of Things (IoT), ensuring reliable onboard navigation intelligence has become a critical challenge for safe and scalable operations in congested waterways. This paper investigates onboard autonomous navigation for such IoT devices under partial observability and dense traffic conditions. A curriculum-guided reinforcement learning framework with a shared recurrent policy is developed to enhance temporal reasoning, deployment scalability, and robustness of edge-level decision-making. Centralized training is adopted as an offline design-time strategy, while all navigation actions are executed fully onboard, consistent with IoT edge intelligence paradigms. Extensive simulations in multiple realistic port environments demonstrate that the proposed approach improves navigation reliability, collision avoidance, and training stability compared with standard baseline methods, and generalizes effectively to previously unseen high-density scenarios. The results indicate that curriculum-guided shared learning provides a practical solution for scalable deployment of IoT-enabled autonomous maritime devices in smart port operations.

</details>

TL;DR: StellaVLA 提出一种通过上下文结构化演示（任务计划、子目标描述、3D 运动语言化）实现 VLA 模型测试时自适应的框架，无需微调即可泛化到分布外场景，并在 VLA-Arena、LIBERO 等基准上取得领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在分布外（OOD）场景下性能会显著下降，传统做法需要收集新数据并微调，成本高昂且难以泛化。作者希望让模型在测试时通过单个检索演示即可自适应，且不增加推理延迟。

Method: StellaVLA 采用自动化离线管线将原始轨迹转换为结构化演示（任务计划、子目标描述、语言化的 3D 运动），零人工标注。这些结构化演示作为上下文指导提供给策略，使模型推理任务意图而非模仿像素轨迹。采用并行双训练设计，通过联合动作与语言目标在训练中内化推理能力，推理时仅使用动作专家，保持实时高频控制。

Result: 在 VLA-Arena 排行榜上以 0.63 总分排名第一（对比 π0.5 的 0.44 和 LingBot-VLA 的 0.22）；LIBERO 平均成功率 98.8%，LIBERO-Plus 成功率 85.1%。真实机器人实验证明可同时使用人类/机器人演示及 XR 人-机演示作为上下文结构化演示，帮助 VLA 适应 OOD 任务。

Conclusion: 通过将演示从"模仿行为"提升为"传达意图"的结构化推理指导，VLA 模型可以在测试时自适应 OOD 场景，且结构化演示具有跨具身（真实机器人、人手、XR）的可迁移性，为无需微调的具身泛化提供了有效路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 StellaVLA，一种面向 Vision-Language-Action (VLA) 模型的具身智能框架，通过上下文结构化演示实现测试时自适应，属于机器人具身智能与动作规划控制范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Strong robotics VLA results but only tangential to your core continuous-model and video-generation priorities. Signals: world model, VLA, in-context adaptation, OOD generalization, LIBERO SOTA

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can follow instructions and manipulate objects, but their performance often collapses out of distribution (OOD), when the scene, viewpoint, or object differs from training. Adapting to each new situation typically requires collecting more data and fine-tuning. We present StellaVLA, a framework that instead adapts at test time by conditioning on a single retrieved demonstration. The key idea is to move beyond imitating what an expert did and instead convey why: an automated offline pipeline converts each raw trajectory into a structured demonstration, e.g., a task plan, sub-goal descriptions, and verbalized 3D motion, at zero human-annotation cost. Provided as in-context guidance, this structured demonstration lets the policy reason about the task rather than mimic a pixel trajectory, which also makes it transferable across embodiments (real-robot, human-hand, or XR demonstrations). A parallel dual-training design internalizes this reasoning during training through a joint action-and-language objective, while inference uses the action expert alone, preserving real-time, high-frequency control with no added latency. On the VLA-Arena leaderboard(Aug 1, 2026), StellaVLA ranks first with an overall score of 0.63, versus 0.44 and 0.22 for the strong prior models ($\pi_{0.5}$ and LingBot-VLA), and it further leads on LIBERO with 98.8% average success rate and LIBERO-Plus with 85.1% success rate. Our real-robot benchmark demonstrates that StellaVLA can use both human/robot demos and human-to-robot (XR) demos as in-context structured demonstration to help VLA model adapt to OOD tasks.

</details>

*Yicheng Liu, Zibin Dong, Baijun Ye, Tianyuan Yuan, Tao Jiang, Anqi Yang, Shicheng Cao, Haonan Liu, Yue Sun, Zihan Guo, Xiao Liu, Dong Ke, Changxun Pan, Chenru Wu, Tailai Cheng, Xiaoshu Ren, Xinlei Zhang, Jianning Cui, Zijie Zhao, Haoyu Zhang, Kaiming Xu, Haodong Yang, Bowen Zhang, Jiahui Niu, Shaoting Zhu, Shiduo Zhang, Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

TL;DR: G0.5 是一个预训练的自回归 Vision-Language-Action (VLA) 模型，用单一 transformer decoder 在统一目标下同时生成推理 token 和动作 token，通过可学习的跨具身动作 tokenizer、原生思维链流和视觉记忆模块，使预训练 VLM 的能力直接迁移到物理行为，在 7 个独立评测基准上超越 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型通常将预训练 VLM 与单独训练的 flow-matching 动作专家耦合，使 VLM 仅作为上下文编码器而非真正的决策者。作者希望让推理与动作共享同一套权重，使预训练 VLM 的能力直接迁移到物理行为，实现无需额外训练即可通过提示控制动作粒度、任务时域和分布外场景处理。

Method: G0.5 采用单一 transformer decoder，在单一目标下同时发射推理 token 和动作 token。三个关键组件：(1) 可学习的跨具身动作 tokenizer，将异构机器人动作映射到共享词汇表；(2) 原生思维链流，将任务分解、物体定位和动作提示与动作 token 交错；(3) 视觉记忆模块，通过视觉编码器注入多秒历史信息。模型在大规模机器人数据集与 VQA 样本上联合预训练。

Result: G0.5 在 7 个独立评测基准上超越 SOTA：真实机器人微调 R1lite/R1pro 达 76.7%（对比 π0.5 的 53.3% 和 GR00T-N1.7 的 24.4%）；2025 BEHAVIOR Challenge 50 个长时域家庭移动操作任务达 31.4%；DROID 后训练零样本迁移到未见环境达 82.5%；LIBERO 语言跟随 Pick-and-Place 达 98.9%；RoboTwin 2.0 达 93.3%；SimplerEnv-Bridge 达 87.3%。

Conclusion: G0.5 证明推理与动作共享单一权重集合的自回归 VLA 架构是可行的，预训练 VLM 的能力可以自然迁移到物理行为，且提示可以直接控制动作粒度、任务时域和分布外场景处理，无需额外训练。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 G0.5，一个面向机器人推理与动作的自回归 VLA 模型，属于具身智能与机器人动作规划控制范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Unifies reasoning and action in a single autoregressive stream, directly advancing world-model and continuous-multimodal directions with strong benchmark gains. Signals: autoregressive VLA, single transformer decoder, cross-embodiment action tokenizer, chain-of-thought action stream, visual memory

Abstract: The prevailing recipe for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models couples a pretrained VLM with a separately trained flow-matching action expert. This makes the VLM a context encoder rather than a decision-maker. We introduce G0.5, a pretrained autoregressive VLA in which a single transformer decoder emits reasoning and action tokens under a single objective. Three components make this tractable at foundation-model scale: a learnable cross-embodiment action tokenizer that maps heterogeneous robot actions into a shared vocabulary; a native chain-of-thought stream interleaving task decomposition, object grounding, and action hints with action tokens; and a visual memory module that injects multi-second history through the vision encoder. Because reasoning and action share a single set of weights, the pretrained VLM's capabilities carry over to physical behavior: the model follows instructions closely, and prompts directly steer action granularity, task horizon, and out-of-distribution scene handling without further training. Pretrained on a large collection of robot datasets together with VQA samples, G0.5 surpasses state-of-the-art models across 7 independent regimes: real-world fine-tuning on R1lite and R1pro robots (76.7\% vs.\ 53.3\% for $\pi_{0.5}$ and 24.4\% for GR00T-N1.7), the 2025 BEHAVIOR Challenge on 50 long-horizon household mobile manipulation tasks using a generalist policy (31.4\% vs.\ 26.3\% for $\pi_{0.5}$ and 26.1\% for the challenge winner), DROID post-training followed by zero-shot transfer to an unseen environment and objects (82.5\%), a language-following Pick-and-Place benchmark, LIBERO (98.9\%), RoboTwin 2.0 (93.3\%), and SimplerEnv-Bridge (87.3\%).

</details>

Conclusion: SMPC演示结合稀疏奖励的offline-to-online RL是一种高效、可扩展的具身技能学习方法，能显著减少奖励塑形负担，并产生优于最优控制教师的行为，适用于多种机器人形态。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于机器人具身智能中的locomotion-manipulation技能学习，利用SMPC专家生成离线数据并结合稀疏奖励RL训练策略，属于具身智能规划控制范畴，映射到[具身世界模型与规划控制]。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot control and RL, not on the user's priority of continuous/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: loco-manipulation, SMPC demonstrations, sparse reward RL, sim-to-real, quadruped and humanoid robots

Abstract: Integrating locomotion and manipulation is essential for robot autonomy, but scaling standard Reinforcement Learning (RL) to complex tasks is severely bottlenecked by the slow, manual process of dense reward shaping. To bypass this limitation, we leverage Sample-based Model Predictive Control (SMPC) entirely in simulation as an automated, rapidly tunable expert to generate massive offline datasets. Because this data solves the fundamental exploration problem, we can train an off-policy RL agent using purely sparse task rewards, drastically reducing the time required to learn new skills and eliminating the need for manual tuning. Integrating this high-level agent with a low-level dynamic stability controller yields more optimal behaviors that strictly align with true task objectives, ultimately allowing the learned policies to surpass the original optimal control teacher. We validate the robustness of this sim-to-real framework by successfully deploying complex loco-manipulation skills across different morphologies, including an arm-equipped Spot quadruped and a G1 humanoid.

</details>

Abstract: Recent research in machine and deep learning has shown the potential of learningbased motion planning approaches to improve the driving behavior of automated vehicles, especially in complex environments. However, their complex nature and lack of transparency can hinder explainability and trustworthiness and complicate safety assurance. Motivated by these challenges, we propose a hybrid planning architecture that combines the advantages of machine learning with the verifiability and the determinism of classical approaches. Specifically, we developed a deep neural network to interpret complex traffic scenes and propose driving behavior, while an optimization-based supervision layer validates this proposal and enforces explicit drivability and safety constraints. We evaluate the learned planner's driving behavior in open-loop studies on real-world urban data, discuss system integration aspects for stable closed-loop operation, and report results from real-world deployment on our research vehicle karl..

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型擅长高效闭环控制，但未显式建模物理场景随任务展开的时空演化；而新兴的世界动作模型（WAM）虽利用预训练视频世界模型捕捉时空演化，但在控制回路中保留未来生成或大型视频骨干会显著增加推理成本。作者希望在不牺牲部署效率的前提下，利用世界建模增强动作策略。

Method: 提出 World Tokens 架构，核心是 World Adapter，它桥接视觉语言理解、世界动力学建模与动作生成。World Adapter 将 VLM 特征转换为固定数量的世界 token，这些 token 同时条件化一个联合微调的未来视频去噪器，并作为动作专家唯一的视觉语言上下文。共享条件化使未来视频去噪的梯度直接塑造用于动作预测的表征，而独占路由防止策略绕过该表征。部署时移除世界模型分支，仅保留 VLM、World Adapter 和动作专家，无在线视频模型推理。

Result: 使用 2B 骨干且无具身动作预训练，World Tokens 在 LIBERO 上极具竞争力，在 SIMPLER 上取得最佳平均成绩，在真实世界 R1 Pro 上相比匹配的动作基线显著提升成功率，且每个动作块以 VLA 级延迟生成。

Conclusion: 训练时世界建模能够有效增强动作策略，同时通过部署时移除世界模型分支保持高效推理，验证了"训练时世界建模、部署时轻量策略"这一范式的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文提出 World Tokens 具身策略架构，通过 World Adapter 在训练时进行世界建模以增强动作策略，属于面向机器人/具身智能的动作规划与控制，映射到 [CANONICAL] embodied_world_model（具身世界模型与规划控制）。同时涉及 VLA 模型与视觉语言理解，也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Training-time world modeling with deployment-time efficiency directly aligns with your world-model and efficient-inference priorities. Signals: world model, embodied policy, training-time world modeling, efficient deployment, VLA

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are a widely adopted paradigm for embodied policies. They excel at efficient closed-loop control but do not explicitly model how physical scenes evolve as a task unfolds. Recently emerging world-action models (WAMs) leverage pretrained video world models to capture spatiotemporal evolution, yet retaining future generation or a large video backbone in the control loop substantially increases inference cost. We introduce World Tokens, an embodied policy architecture built around a World Adapter that bridges visual-language understanding, world-dynamics modeling, and action generation. It uses world modeling during training to enhance the action policy while preserving efficient deployment. Specifically, the World Adapter transforms VLM features into a fixed set of world tokens, which condition a jointly fine-tuned future-video denoiser and simultaneously serve as the action expert's sole visual-language context. This shared conditioning allows gradients from future-video denoising to directly shape the representation used for action prediction, while exclusive routing prevents the policy from bypassing that representation. At deployment, the world-model branch is removed, leaving only the VLM, World Adapter, and action expert, with no online video-model inference. With a 2B backbone and no embodied action pretraining, World Tokens is highly competitive on LIBERO, attains the best reported averages on SIMPLER, substantially improves real-world R1 Pro success over a matched action-only baseline, and generates each action chunk at VLA-level latency.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体的成功表明，智能体的能力不仅取决于模型本身，还取决于围绕它的基础设施（harness 范式）。作者质疑这一范式能否扩展到物理世界的具身智能体，并指出物理世界与软件世界不同，无法免费提供"读取世界状态"和"判断动作结果"两种能力，因此需要专门设计来弥合这一差距。

Method: Thea 是一个 harness 框架，其中 agentic loop 编排机器人能力，每种能力封装为可调用工具。它继承了编码智能体的核心组件，并根据物理世界需求进行修改。核心创新包括：(1) Scene Graph as Context——持久、符号化的世界表征；(2) Evaluation as Exit Codes——检测动作何时应终止、判断是否成功、失败时诊断原因。两者共同闭合智能体与物理世界之间的循环。

Result: 通过工具组合涌现出丰富行为，闭合循环使长时程任务在真实环境中得以完成。

Conclusion: Thea 证明编码智能体的 harness 范式可以成功扩展到物理世界的具身智能体，场景图与评估机制是弥合物理世界与软件世界能力差距的关键设计。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Thea，一个面向具身智能体的 harness 框架，通过 agentic loop 编排机器人能力，引入场景图作为世界状态表征、评估作为动作退出码，实现真实环境中的长时程任务。这属于具身智能、机器人规划与控制范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: 具身智能体harness框架与场景图世界表征，但偏离用户核心的连续多模态与生成方向。 Signals: embodied agent, harness paradigm, scene graph as context, evaluation as exit codes, world model

Abstract: The success of coding agents has established the harness as a paradigm: what an agent achieves depends not on the model alone, but on the infrastructure around it. We ask whether the same paradigm extends to embodied agents in the physical world. We present Thea, a harness in which an agentic loop orchestrates robot capabilities, each wrapped as a callable tool. It inherits the core components of coding agents, modified as the physical world requires. The world, however, withholds two abilities that software grants for free: reading the state of the world, and judging the outcome of an action. To bridge these gaps, Thea introduces Scene Graph as Context, a persistent, symbolic representation of the world, and Evaluation as Exit Codes, which detects when an action should terminate, judges whether it succeeded, and on failure diagnoses the cause. Together they close the loop between the agent and the physical world. Rich behaviors then emerge from the composition of tools, and the closed loop carries long-horizon tasks to completion in real environments.

</details>

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ForeWAM 是一种动力学条件化的直接策略世界动作模型（WAM），通过 Future-KV 机制在动作去噪过程中复用 Video DiT 的层间键值状态，无需解码未来视频即可为动作生成提供预测性上下文，在 LIBERO 上达到 96.7% 的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）在如何将预测动力学暴露给动作路径上存在权衡：显式未来 WAM 提供直接访问的预测场景演化但推理成本高（需迭代视频去噪）；直接策略 WAM 高效预测动作但缺乏显式的推理时接口来向 Action DiT 暴露预测动力学。作者希望弥合这一差距，在保持高效动作预测的同时，让动作路径能够利用预测性动力学信息。

Method: 提出 ForeWAM，一种动力学条件化的直接策略 WAM。核心是 Future-KV 机制：对当前视觉 latent 和随机未来槽位执行单次 Video DiT prefill，并在整个动作去噪过程中复用得到的层间键值状态。同时引入动力学寄存器（dynamics registers），由冻结的潜在动作教师模型监督，促使隐式未来状态捕获交互引发的转变（如物体运动、接触变化、任务进度）。未来观测和教师仅在训练时使用，部署时无需未来视频生成。

Result: 无需具身机器人数据预训练，标准版和加速版 ForeWAM 在 LIBERO 上分别达到 96.7% 和 96.9% 的平均成功率；标准版在 LIBERO-Plus 上达到 61.6% 成功率。

Conclusion: 直接策略 WAM 可以在不显式生成未来观测的情况下，将预测动力学暴露给动作路径，同时保持高效的动作预测能力，验证了隐式未来状态作为动作生成上下文的可行性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ForeWAM，一种面向机器人动作生成的动力学条件世界动作模型（WAM），将未来视觉预测与机器人动作生成耦合，属于具身智能中的世界模型与动作规划控制范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Novel latent-future WAM design that reuses Video DiT KV states for efficient action generation, strongly aligned with continuous latent/world-model priorities. Signals: world model, latent future, action DiT, efficient inference, LIBERO 96.7%

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) couple future visual prediction with robot action generation, enabling policies to model how the physical world evolves during interaction. Existing WAMs differ in how predictive dynamics are exposed to the action pathway. Explicit-future WAMs provide direct access to predicted scene evolution, but incur substantial inference costs from iterative video denoising. In contrast, direct-policy WAMs efficiently predict actions from the current observation but lack an explicit inference-time interface for exposing predictive dynamics to the Action DiT. To bridge this gap, we propose ForeWAM, a dynamics-conditioned direct-policy WAM that provides predictive context for action generation without decoding future videos. At its core, Future-KV performs a single Video DiT prefill over the current visual latent and stochastic future slots, and reuses the resulting layer-wise key-value states throughout action denoising. We further introduce dynamics registers supervised by a frozen latent action teacher, encouraging the implicit future states to capture interaction-induced transitions such as object motion, contact changes, and task progress. Ground-truth future observations and the teacher are used only during training; deployment requires neither and performs no future video generation. Without embodied robot data pretraining, the standard and accelerated variants of ForeWAM achieve average success rates of 96.7% and 96.9% on LIBERO, respectively. The standard variant further achieves 61.6% success on LIBERO-Plus. These results demonstrate that direct-policy WAMs can retain efficient action prediction while exposing predictive dynamics to the action pathway without explicitly generating future observations.

</details>

*Xikai Sun, Cangtian Zhou, Kebin Liu, Ke Ma, Xu Wang, Zaishu Chen, Haotian Wang, Li Liu, Yunhao Liu*

TL;DR: HUGIN 是一个面向自主物流分拣系统（ALSS）的视觉语言规划训练框架，将多相机视角下的联合规划形式化为 Joint Multi-Scene Understanding (JMSU)，通过内生数据增强与全局上下文排序提升 VLM 的跨场景规划能力，并构建 SortingBench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主物流分拣系统是具身 AI 的重要工业应用，需要在空间分离的多相机视角上进行联合规划。现有 VLM 直接应用于 JMSU 面临跨场景监督稀缺和长视觉上下文导致的注意力分散两大挑战。

Method: 提出 HUGIN 训练框架，包含两个互补组件：内生数据增强（Endogenous Data Augmentation）在运行约束下重组已验证的原子事实；全局上下文排序（Global Context Ranking）使指令表征与完整视觉上下文的关联强于部分视觉上下文。同时构建 SortingBench 工业分拣数据集与基准。

Result: 在五个开放 VLM 上 HUGIN 一致优于匹配基线，例如 Qwen3-VL-8B 在 SortingBench 上的准确率从 63.6% 提升到 78.8%。消融实验验证了各组件的有效性，JMSU 在具身任务上具有溢出收益，超过 15000 件包裹的部署测试支持了实际可行性。

Conclusion: HUGIN 通过数据增强与上下文排序有效提升了 VLM 在多场景联合规划中的表现，验证了基于 VLM 的规划方法在自主物流分拣这一具身工业场景中的实用价值。

Classification reason: 论文面向自主物流分拣系统（embodied AI 的工业应用），涉及多视角场景理解与任务规划，属于具身智能规划控制范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Niche industrial logistics planning with no direct alignment to your core continuous multimodal, autoregressive generation, or world-model priorities. Signals: embodied AI planning, VLM training framework, logistics sorting, JMSU formulation, SortingBench benchmark

Abstract: Autonomous logistics sorting systems (ALSS) are an important industrial application of embodied AI, which requires joint planning over spatially disjoint camera views. We formulate this setting as Joint Multi-Scene Understanding (JMSU). With open-world visual understanding and task-planning capabilities, vision-language models (VLMs) are promising candidates for JMSU. However, directly applying existing VLMs to JMSU is non-trivial due to scarce cross-scene supervision and attention dispersion caused by long visual context in JMSU. To address these challenges, we propose HUGIN, a training framework with two complementary components. Endogenous Data Augmentation recombines verified atomic facts under operating constraints, while Global Context Ranking aligns the instruction representation more strongly with the complete visual context than with a partial visual context. To support ongoing research, we construct a high-quality industrial sorting dataset and benchmark named SortingBench from four layouts of autonomous logistics sorting systems. Across five open VLMs, HUGIN consistently outperforms matched baselines; for example, the accuracy on SortingBench of Qwen3-VL-8B increases from 63.6% to 78.8%. Additional experiments verify the effectiveness of each component and JMSU's spillover benefits in embodied tasks. Deployment tests involving more than 15,000 packages support the practical viability of VLM-based planning for autonomous logistics sorting.

</details>


### [16] [Better Slots, Better Worlds: Representation Quality & Robustness in Object-Centric World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12078)
*Shukrullo Nazirjonov, Sai Prasanna, Anna Manasyan, Georg Martius*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对对象中心世界模型（OCWM）进行受控研究，沿对象中心表征质量和分布外泛化两个维度，与场景中心模型对比，发现规划成功与无监督槽质量指标正相关、良好绑定的槽可消除对辅助输入和掩码归纳偏置的依赖，且良好绑定的 OCWM 在分布外更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前对象中心世界模型（OCWM）将槽编码器视为给定，且仅在分布内评测，未验证对象中心归纳偏置是否真正有利于规划，以及是什么因素驱动了其表现。本文旨在填补这一空白，进行受控研究。

Method: 沿两条轴对 OCWM 进行受控研究：(i) 对象中心表征质量（用 FG-ARI、mBO 等无监督槽质量指标衡量）与规划成功的关系；(ii) 相对场景中心模型在分布偏移下的泛化鲁棒性。对比对象包括端到端训练的场景中心 LeWM 和基于冻结预训练特征的 DINO-WM。

Result: (i) 规划成功与槽质量指标（FG-ARI、mBO）正相关，但在高槽质量处增益饱和；(ii) 当槽绑定良好时，先前方法依赖的辅助本体感觉输入和掩码归纳偏置变得不必要；(iii) 在未见分布偏移下，槽绑定良好的 OCWM 整体比端到端场景中心 LeWM 更鲁棒，而基于冻结预训练特征的 DINO-WM 仍具竞争力，表明预训练特征是鲁棒性的关键贡献者。

Conclusion: 对象中心表征质量是 OCWM 规划性能与鲁棒性的关键驱动因素；良好绑定的槽可简化模型设计（去除辅助输入与掩码），且预训练特征对分布外鲁棒性贡献显著。

Classification reason: 论文研究基于对象中心表征的世界模型用于视觉模型预测控制（MPC）与规划，属于具身智能/机器人规划控制范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Directly advances world-model robustness and object-centric representation quality, aligning with the user's world-model priority and boosting keywords. Signals: object-centric world model, world model robustness, distribution shift, slot quality, planning

Abstract: Learning world models from offline trajectories enables agents to accomplish different tasks through planning. Object-centric (OC) representations, which decompose a scene into a set of slots that bind to its objects, have been proposed as an inductive bias for world models that are more sample-efficient and generalize better. Yet prior object-centric world models (OCWMs) take the slot encoder as given and evaluate only in-distribution, leaving open whether the object-centric bias actually delivers for planning and what within the OCWM drives it. We conduct a controlled study of OCWMs for visual model-predictive control along two axes: object-centric representation quality and generalization under distribution shift relative to scene-centric models. We find that (i) planning success correlates positively with unsupervised slot-quality metrics (FG-ARI, mBO), though the gains saturate at high slot quality; (ii) with well-bound slots, the auxiliary proprioception inputs and masking inductive bias that prior methods relied on become unnecessary; and (iii) under unseen distribution shifts, the OCWM with well-bound slots is more robust overall than the end-to-end trained scene-centric LeWM, while DINO-WM, built on similar frozen pretrained features, remains competitive -- pointing to pretrained features as a key contributor to robustness.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度量3D目标检测是具身智能体的核心能力，但多数可靠系统依赖深度传感器，成本、功耗和集成复杂度高。单目3D检测虽避免额外约束，但单张图像中深度和绝对尺度欠约束，2D检测后预测3D属性的范式脆弱，尺度先验在域偏移下易失效。

Method: 将短时间窗口映射到多个视角，复用前馈度量3D重建模型作为几何骨干并调整其目标感知能力，在此重建表征上直接预测度量3D空间中的目标框，无需2D到3D提升。

Result: 在多个基准上实验表明该设计支持强在线性能，且无需适配即可稳健迁移，验证了为检测训练重建先验是稳定度量3D检测的实用路径。

Conclusion: 将检测直接引入由RGB重建的3D空间，利用重建先验替代脆弱的尺度先验，为单目视频流的稳定度量3D检测提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 论文面向具身智能体的度量3D目标检测，利用前馈度量3D重建模型作为几何骨干，从单目视频流中直接预测3D框，属于具身智能环境感知与建模范畴，映射到具身世界模型与规划控制这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Solid CV contribution but outside your core continuous-multimodal/generation focus, with only weak world-model overlap. Signals: metric 3D detection, feed-forward reconstruction prior, monocular video, online multi-view, no 2D-to-3D lifting

Abstract: Metric 3D object detection is a core capability for embodied agents, yet most reliable systems lean on depth sensors, trading away cost, power, and integration simplicity. This motivates monocular 3D detection, which avoids additional constraints, yet it faces a major obstacle: from a single image, depth, and especially absolute scale, are underconstrained. As a result, the prevailing pattern of detecting in 2D and then predicting 3D attributes is often brittle, since modest range errors can dominate 3D localization, and the learned scale prior can fail when cameras, motion, or environments undergo domain shifts. To address this, we propose Map-Det3D, an online multi-view 3D object detection model that brings detection directly into a 3D space reconstructed from RGB. We map a short temporal window into multiple views and repurpose a feed-forward metric 3D reconstruction model as our geometric backbone while tuning its object-aware capabilities. Building on this representation, Map-Det3D directly predicts boxes in metric 3D space, without the widely used 2D-to-3D lifting. Experiments across different benchmarks show that this design supports strong online performance and robust transfer without adaptation, suggesting that training reconstruction priors for detection is a practical route to stable metric 3D detection from monocular video. Code and models are available at .

</details>

TL;DR: DreamFly 是一个基于 Dream-VLA 的扩散式空中视觉语言导航框架，引入因果对齐的历史记忆、receding-horizon 扩散规划（plan-K execute-one）以及 LiteStop 显式终止估计，在 OpenFly 基准上取得 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中视觉语言导航（VLN）要求具身智能体在部分可观测条件下整合时序视觉证据、规划未来动作并判断是否到达目标。现有 VLA 模型在适配空中导航时面临历史上下文有限、规划视野短、隐式终止不可靠等挑战。

Method: DreamFly 包含三个核心组件：(1) 因果对齐的历史记忆，仅用当前决策步之前的观测增强视觉表征，避免未来信息泄漏；(2) receding-horizon 扩散规划，策略预测 K 步动作块但只执行第一步后重新规划，以未来动作为辅助规划目标同时保持闭环视觉反馈；(3) LiteStop 从初始 all-mask 状态的动作 logits 直接估计停止概率，将显式终止与动作生成解耦。

Result: 在 OpenFly 基准上，DreamFly 在 test-seen/test-unseen 上分别取得 32.04%/29.46% SR 和 28.22%/23.54% SPL，两项指标均优于所有对比方法，并取得最低导航误差。

Conclusion: 联合建模历史上下文、未来动作结构与显式终止对于空中 VLN 是有效的，验证了扩散规划范式在具身空中导航中的潜力。

Classification reason: 论文提出 DreamFly，一个基于扩散的空中视觉语言导航（VLN）框架，将导航建模为 receding-horizon 扩散规划，属于具身智能中的动作规划与控制，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。同时涉及视觉语言导航，也匹配多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Solid embodied world-model planning work, but aerial VLN is outside your core continuous-multimodal and video-generation focus. Signals: diffusion planning, receding-horizon, world model, embodied navigation, causal memory

Abstract: Aerial vision-language navigation (VLN) requires an embodied agent to integrate visual evidence over time, plan future actions, and determine when it has reached a navigation goal under partial observability. Although recent VLA models offer a promising perception-to-action paradigm, adapting them to aerial navigation remains challenging due to limited historical context, short planning horizons, and unreliable implicit termination. To address these challenges, we propose DreamFly, a diffusion-based aerial VLN framework built on Dream-VLA. DreamFly introduces a causally aligned historical memory that augments the current visual representation using only observations preceding the current decision step, enabling temporal reasoning without future information leakage. We further formulate navigation as receding-horizon diffusion planning, where the policy predicts a $K$-step action chunk but executes only the first action before replanning. This plan-$K$, execute-one strategy uses future actions as auxiliary planning targets while preserving closed-loop visual feedback. Finally, LiteStop estimates the stop probability directly from action logits at the initial all-mask state, decoupling explicit termination from action generation. Experiments on the OpenFly benchmark demonstrate consistent improvements in seen and unseen environments. DreamFly achieves 32.04%/29.46% SR and 28.22%/23.54% SPL on the test-seen/test-unseen splits, respectively, outperforming all compared methods on both metrics while attaining the lowest navigation error. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of jointly modeling historical context, future action structure, and explicit termination for aerial VLN.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作为使用离线动力学模型支持真实世界环境中的RL部署提供了概念验证，同时指出了未来工作需解决的重要实践挑战。

Classification reason: 论文提出用离线数据训练的校准模型（calibration models）来近似环境动力学，用于真实世界（污水处理厂）的强化学习超参数选择。这属于环境动力学建模/神经仿真器范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 的 interactive_physical_world（可交互与物理世界建模）高度相关。

Signals: offline RL hyperparameter selection, real-world water treatment plant, kNN with Laplacian distance, nexting prediction, long-horizon rollouts

Abstract: A key obstacle to deploying reinforcement learning in real-world systems is hyperparameter selection, particularly when simulators are unavailable and online experimentation is costly. Prior work has proposed calibration models trained on offline data to approximate environment dynamics and enable offline hyperparameter selection, but these methods have so far been evaluated only in simple simulated settings. In this paper, we present the first application of calibration models in a real-world industrial setting: a municipal water treatment plant. We evaluate several calibration model approaches, including a k-nearest neighbors model with a Laplacian distance metric, on high-dimensional, non-stationary sensor data for nexting prediction tasks. Our results show that these models can generate realistic long-horizon rollouts and recover meaningful hyperparameter sensitivity trends. We further examine how calibration models scale to year-long datasets, how they support the selection of fine-tuning learning rates for pre-trained agents, and how robust they are under distribution shift. Overall, our findings provide a proof of concept for using offline dynamics models to support RL deployment in real-world environments, while highlighting important practical challenges for future work.

</details>

TL;DR: AutoWorldModel-Bench 是一个面向世界模型研究的闭环评测基准，让前沿编码智能体在固定计算预算下自主改进给定的世界模型 starter。基准覆盖 8 个游戏环境，采用统一结构化状态表示，隔离动力学建模与感知，实现分钟级迭代。在 64 次会话中，Codex-5.4 和 Claude Opus 4.6 在 63 次中改进了 starter，其中 91% 的获胜编辑是非平凡的研究型修改（新目标、表征、回滚流程或架构改动），而非超参数微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界建模领域尚未定型，架构、训练目标和状态表示之间交互复杂，没有单一方案能在所有环境中占优。这使其成为评测 AI 编码智能体作为自主研究者的理想测试平台——与当前以"按规格工程"为主流的智能体基准不同，这里改进方向未预先指定，需要开放式研究能力。

Method: 构建 AutoWorldModel-Bench 闭环基准：覆盖 8 个游戏环境，采用统一结构化状态表示（从每个游戏提取 ground-truth 实体状态，通过共享张量格式消费），从而将动力学建模与感知解耦，支持分钟级迭代。让前沿编码智能体在固定计算预算下自主改进提供的世界模型 starter，并评估其改进效果。

Result: 在 64 次会话中，Codex-5.4 和 Claude Opus 4.6 在 63 次中成功改进了 starter；在 91% 的会话中，获胜编辑是非平凡的研究型修改（新目标、表征、回滚流程或架构改动），而非超参数微调。

Conclusion: AutoWorldModel-Bench 提供了一个可在开放式研究而非按规格工程问题上评估前沿编码智能体的平台，展示了 AI 智能体在世界模型研究中的自主改进能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AutoWorldModel-Bench，一个用于评测 AI 编码智能体自动改进世界模型的闭环基准，属于世界模型方向的评测基准工作。在 world_model 方向下，非 canonical 的 generation_benchmark 子主题（生成模型评测与数据集）最匹配，因为该工作核心是评测基准与数据集构建。

Importance reason: Novel benchmark for autonomous world-model research, but focuses on agent evaluation rather than advancing world-model methods themselves. Signals: world model benchmark, autonomous coding agents, closed-loop evaluation, 8 game environments, research-style modifications

Abstract: World modeling is an unsettled field: architectures, training objectives, and state representations interact in complex ways, and no single recipe dominates across environments. This makes it an ideal testbed for AI coding agents acting as autonomous researchers--a setting in which the improvement direction is not specified in advance, unlike the engineering-to-spec tasks that dominate current agent benchmarks. We introduce AutoWorldModel-Bench, a closed-loop benchmark in which frontier coding agents autonomously improve a provided world-model starter under a fixed compute budget. The benchmark spans eight game environments under a unified structured-state representation--ground-truth entity state extracted from each game and consumed through a shared tensor format--which isolates dynamics modeling from perception and enables minutes-per-run iteration. Across 64 sessions, Codex-5.4 and Claude Opus 4.6 improve their starter on 63; in 91% of sessions the winning edit is a non-trivial research-style modification--a new objective, representation, rollout procedure, or architectural change--rather than a hyperparameter tweak. Our benchmark offers a setting in which frontier coding agents can be evaluated on open-ended research rather than engineering-to-spec problems.

</details>

*Yuyang Yin, Zixiang Li, Longxuan Deng, Hongkai Li, Shifang Zhao, Junnan Liu, Weirong Huang, Mengyu Wang, Tianxiao Fu, Yikai Wang, Peng-Shuai Wang, Xiaojie Jin, Yao Zhao, Yunchao Wei*

TL;DR: StateFlow 提出一种以状态为中心的生成式预可视化框架，用可编辑的持久化 3D 世界状态组织场景结构、演化与相机，替代一次性视频生成，支持迭代编辑与高质量视频创作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式预可视化方法依赖简单提示词通过一次性图像或视频合成联合控制场景、动作、相机与时空动态，可控性弱且不支持迭代编辑。作者认为缺失的是显式且持久的工作状态——世界由多个具有几何、外观等属性的元素与相机组成，不同帧由共享状态的局部修改或重组产生。

Method: StateFlow 采用三阶段框架：1) State construction 通过先验引导、冲突感知的双视图初始化将生成的 2D 内容提升为连贯的 3D 世界；2) State evolution 将用户意图转化为结构化状态转移，同时保留世界记忆，避免每次编辑都全场景重生成；3) State access 使用渲染反馈反思来细化相机规划为视觉上可行的轨迹，避免仅依赖 VLM 语义。世界被维护为场景元素与相机配置的持久化结构化 3D 状态，需要更高保真度时用现成视频模型增强视觉质量。

Result: 实验表明 StateFlow 能为视频创作和类游戏原型制作生成高质量的 3D 世界，支持场景、动作、相机与时空动态的迭代式精炼。

Conclusion: 显式且持久的 3D 世界状态是生成式预可视化的关键缺失组件，StateFlow 通过构建、演化与访问世界状态三阶段，实现了比一次性生成更强的可控性与迭代编辑能力。

Classification reason: 论文提出 StateFlow，以可编辑的持久化 3D 世界状态为核心工作表征，组织场景结构、演化与相机，属于空间 3D 世界建模，映射到 [CANONICAL] spatial_3d_4d_world_model。

Importance reason: Strong fit for world-model and 3D/4D state-centric generation, though it lacks direct continuous-token or autoregressive video focus. Signals: 3D world state, previsualization, editable persistent state, state evolution, render-feedback reflection

Abstract: Previsualization is an intermediate layer between ideas and production in film, games, architecture, and urban design. It lets creators iteratively refine scenes, actions, cameras, and spatial-temporal dynamics. Yet existing generative methods rely on simple prompts to jointly control all of these factors through one-shot image or video synthesis, offering weak controllability and limited support for iterative editing. Fundamentally, a world comprises multiple elements with geometry, appearance, and other attributes, together with cameras. Different frames are produced through local modifications or recombinations of this shared state, which is otherwise largely reused. Therefore, we argue that the missing component is an explicit and persistent working state. To address this, we present StateFlow, a state-centric framework for generative previsualization. Rather than generating videos in one shot, StateFlow uses an editable 3D world to organize scene structure, evolution, and cameras, while off-the-shelf video models enhance visual quality when higher fidelity is desired. This world is maintained as a persistent structured 3D state of scene elements and camera configurations, serving as the core working representation for previsualization. Built on this insight, StateFlow has three stages to construct, evolve, and access the world state. State construction lifts generated 2D content into a coherent 3D world through prior-guided, conflict-aware dual-view initialization, while State evolution translates user intent into structured state transitions while preserving world memory, avoiding full-scene regeneration for each edit. State access uses render-feedback reflection to refine camera plans into visually feasible trajectories, avoiding reliance on VLM semantics alone. Experiments show that StateFlow produces high-quality 3D worlds for video creation and game-like prototyping.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文指出驾驶世界模型作为反事实模拟器的目标与直接动作条件预测之间存在根本性不匹配：直接预测未利用事实轨迹中历史之后的实际延续，因此无法保证保留该片段实际发生的内容。作者用因果"溯因-动作-预测"框架形式化该差距，构建受控仿真基准，并在两个代表性世界模型上验证直接预测无法匹配反事实真值。作为建设性验证，提出一个简单、免训练、将观测证据迁移到反事实视角的流水线，显著提升恢复比例并降低感知距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶世界模型常被用作反事实模拟器：给定事实驾驶日志，回答"若采取替代自我动作会发生什么"。但直接动作条件预测仅使用共享历史与替代动作，未利用历史之后观察到的事实延续，因此可能生成合理却未保留该片段实际发生内容的未来。作者希望形式化并量化这一差距，推动更好的反事实预测方法。

Method: 作者用因果"溯因-动作-预测"（abduction-action-prediction）框架形式化直接预测与反事实预测之间的差距，并在短时间窗口设定下研究（替代自我动作不改变周围智能体演化）。构建受控仿真基准，包含事实结果与匹配的反事实结果。提出一个简单、免训练的流水线：将观测证据迁移到反事实视角，让冻结模型补全未知部分。

Result: 在两个代表性世界模型上，直接预测无法匹配反事实真值，支持了作者的分析。所提出的简单免训练流水线在两个模型上均显著提高整体恢复比例，并降低与匹配反事实之间的感知距离。

Conclusion: 驾驶世界模型的反事实预测存在系统性差距，直接动作条件预测不足以实现反事实模拟。即使简单的证据迁移流水线也能显著改善，说明该方向值得更深入的研究，以开发更好的反事实预测方法。

Classification reason: 论文研究驾驶世界模型的反事实预测问题，属于动作条件视频生成与未来预测范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型（video_world_model）子主题。

Importance reason: Directly targets the user's high-priority video world model direction with a novel causal framing and actionable counterfactual pipeline. Signals: world model, video world model, counterfactual prediction, driving world model, causal abduction-action-prediction

Abstract: Driving world models are often interpreted as counterfactual simulators for observed driving episodes: given a factual driving log, they are asked what would have happened under an alternative ego action. In this paper, we identify a fundamental mismatch between this goal and direct action-conditioned prediction. The direct prediction uses the shared history and the alternative action but not the factual continuation observed after that history. It can therefore generate a plausible future without preserving what actually happened in this episode. We formalize this gap using the causal recipe of abduction, action, and prediction and study it in a setting with a short time horizon, where the alternative ego action does not alter how surrounding agents evolve. To make the gap measurable, we construct a controlled simulation benchmark with factual outcomes and matched counterfactual outcomes. Across two representative world models, direct predictions fail to match the counterfactual ground truth, supporting our analysis. As a constructive check of this analysis, we introduce a deliberately simple, training-free pipeline that moves observed evidence into the counterfactual view and lets the frozen model complete what remains unknown. Even this simple construction raises the overall recovered fraction substantially and reduces perceptual distance to the matched counterfactual on both models. We hope this work draws attention to this gap and motivates better counterfactual prediction methods for driving world models.

</details>

*Zongjian Ding, Yudong Gao, Jiale Liu, Xinglin Yu, Junxing Ren, Dong Wei, Yajing Chen, Shan Huang, Mingjun Cheng, Min Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: Seed2GS 提出一种无需原始重建相机、无需场景特定训练的 3DGS 场景目标物体提取方法，通过单参考视图定位（QD-SAM3）分离目标身份与 3D 覆盖，结合 seed lift 与可见性自适应虚拟轨道传播掩码，在 LERF-MASK 上达到 92.1% mIoU，计算延迟仅 9.3 秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从预构建的 3DGS 场景中提取目标物体是交互式 3D 编辑的关键。现有方法要么需要每场景训练数十分钟，要么牺牲精度，要么依赖原始重建相机（预构建资产可能不包含）。作者希望实现无需原始相机、无需场景特定训练的高精度目标提取。

Method: Seed2GS 的核心思想是分离目标身份（identity）与 3D 覆盖（coverage）。首先用 QD-SAM3 从多个开放词汇候选掩码中选择一个可靠参考掩码以固定目标身份；然后通过 seed lift 和可见性自适应虚拟轨道从新视角暴露物体，利用跟踪传播 seed 而无需重复检测；由于场景保持冻结，这些掩码仅监督每个高斯的一个临时前景 logit。

Result: 在 LERF-MASK 上达到 92.1% 平均 mIoU，计算延迟仅 9.3 秒，比最强的场景训练基线高 3.7 点，比最近的免相机基线高 7.6 点。每场景固定一个测试参考时完整流程保持 91.1% mIoU；用真实掩码替换预测 seed 仅提升 0.72 点。在 3D-OVS 上达到 95.7% mIoU。

Conclusion: Seed2GS 证明了无需原始重建相机和场景特定训练即可实现高精度的 3DGS 目标提取，其身份与覆盖分离的设计有效且高效，为交互式 3D 编辑提供了实用方案。

Importance reason: Solid 3DGS object extraction work, but it is outside the user's configured priority directions and subtopics. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, object extraction, camera-free, training-free, LERF-MASK

Abstract: Extracting a target object from a pre-built 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scene enables interactive 3D editing. Existing methods either train for tens of minutes per scene, sacrifice accuracy, or require original reconstruction cameras that pre-built assets may not include. We present Seed2GS, which achieves the highest reported LERF-MASK accuracy without original reconstruction cameras or scene-specific representation training. Its key insight is to separate target identity from 3D coverage. QD-SAM3 selects one reliable reference mask from several open-vocabulary candidates, fixing identity once. Seed lift and visibility-adaptive virtual orbits then expose the object from new viewpoints, while tracking propagates the seed without repeated detection. Because the scene remains frozen, these masks supervise only one temporary foreground logit per Gaussian. On LERF-MASK, Seed2GS reaches 92.1% mean intersection over union (mIoU) with a measured compute-only latency of 9.3 seconds, 3.7 points above the strongest scene-trained baseline and 7.6 points above the closest camera-free baseline. With one fixed test reference per scene, the complete pipeline retains 91.1% mIoU; replacing its predicted seed with a ground-truth mask improves mIoU by only 0.72 points. On 3D-OVS, Seed2GS reaches 95.7% mIoU.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏体素重建的几何通常仅从局部光度证据和离散可见性统计优化，导致在弱纹理或稀疏观测区域出现碎片化表面、过度细分和漂浮伪影。现有方法直接提升噪声像素级深度预测，缺乏结构化的几何约束。

Method: SurfSVR 首先将每张图像组织为相干表面区域，联合推理外观、单目深度、法线与跨视角几何；每个区域基于拟合可靠性和几何复杂度自适应选择平面或二次曲面模型，并通过跨模型一致性区分可靠几何与模糊预测。这些结构化 2D 先验被提升到 3D 并贯穿重建流程：引导表面自适应体素细分、提供区域级深度与法线监督、增强几何可靠的稀疏观测表面体素剪枝、抑制后精修训练中的离面漂浮物。

Result: 在 3 个公开基准上的大量实验表明，SurfSVR 在可见性与几何特征差异显著的场景中一致提升稀疏体素重建质量，达到 SOTA 重建效果。

Conclusion: 将图像空间的语义与几何一致性转化为 3D 中持久的结构约束，能够有效提升稀疏体素重建的几何质量，验证了 2D 表面先验作为 3D 几何正则化器的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文提出 SurfSVR，一种稀疏体素 3D 重建方法，利用 2D 表面先验作为 3D 几何正则化器，属于 3D 场景重建范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] three_d_generation（3D 生成与重建）。

Importance reason: Solid 3D reconstruction work but outside your core continuous/multimodal generation and world-model focus. Signals: 3D reconstruction, sparse voxel, surface priors, geometric regularization, SOTA on benchmarks

Abstract: Sparse voxel reconstruction offers an efficient representation for high-fidelity 3D modeling, yet its geometry is commonly optimized from local photometric evidence and discrete visibility statistics. This often leads to fragmented surfaces, excessive subdivision, and floating artifacts, particularly in weakly textured or sparsely observed regions. We introduce SurfSVR, a novel sparse voxel reconstruction paradigm that treats 2D surface priors as explicit 3D geometric regularizers. Instead of directly lifting noisy pixel-wise depth predictions, SurfSVR first organizes each image into coherent surface regions by jointly reasoning over appearance, monocular depth, normals and cross-view geometry. Each region is then represented by an adaptively selected planar or quadratic surface model based on fitting reliability and geometric complexity, while cross-model agreement distinguishes reliable geometry from ambiguous predictions. These structured 2D priors are lifted into 3D and integrated throughout the reconstruction pipeline. They guide surface-adaptive voxel subdivision, provide region-level depth and normal supervision during optimization, enhance geometrically reliable sparse-observed surfaces in voxel pruning, and suppress off-surface floaters during post-refinement training. This unified design converts semantic and geometric coherence in image space into persistent structural constraints in 3D. Extensive experiments on 3 public benchmarks demonstrate that SurfSVR consistently improves sparse voxel reconstruction across scenes with substantially different visibility and geometry characteristics, achieving state-of-the-art reconstruction quality. Codes and models will be released soon.

</details>

Method: 将几何与纹理生成解耦。几何阶段使用高分辨率生成模块生成多视角法线图，并采用几何雕刻策略保持视角一致性并支持宽松衣物；纹理阶段从密集环绕视角采样，通过纹理先验获取策略和扩散渲染器生成空间一致的RGB观测，实现精细纹理合成。

Conclusion: 通过解耦几何与纹理生成并采用显式多视角观测生成与优化，TGRHuman有效缓解了NeRF方法的局限，实现了高质量、一致且高效的3D人体生成。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文本引导的3D人体生成（text-to-3D human），属于3D生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "three_d_generation"（3D生成与重建）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Solid text-to-3D human generation work, but it is outside the user's core continuous/multimodal generation and world-model focus. Signals: 3D human generation, text-to-3D, diffusion renderer, geometry-texture decoupling, multi-view normals

Abstract: Realistic 3D human generation plays a crucial role in many graphics applications. However, current methods still struggle to generate high-quality human geometry and texture while maintaining 3D consistency and inference efficiency. In this work, we address these limitations by introducing TGRHuman, a novel approach for generating realistic 3D humans from text. Our method decouples geometry and texture generation to alleviate the issues commonly encountered in NeRF-based methods. Instead of relying on slow, implicit score-distillation-based optimization, we directly use explicit multi-view observation generation and optimization for efficient 3D synthesis. For geometry generation, we propose a high-resolution generative module for multi-view normals together with a geometry-carving strategy that preserves view consistency and supports loose clothing. For texture generation, we produce spatially consistent RGB observations from densely sampled surrounding views using a carefully designed texture-prior acquisition strategy and a diffusion renderer, enabling detailed human texture synthesis. Experiments show that our method can generate high-quality and consistent 3D human geometry and texture efficiently. TGRHuman outperforms existing text-to-3D human methods in both geometry and texture quality.

</details>

*Zhenjie Yang, Xingyu Jiao, Guopeng Zhong, Shuzhe Yang, Shi Che, Chao Wu, Chenyu Jiang, Dongjie Zhang, Yideng Zhang, Zheng Zhang, Muyun Jiang, Haisheng Su, Shuang Jin, Donghang Zhang, Chao Yang, Li Chen, Hongyang Li, Zuxuan Wu, Yu-Gang Jiang, Xiaosong Jia, Junchi Yan*

TL;DR: HandEdit 是一个大规模具身感知图像编辑数据集与基准，将第一视角人类手部/手臂图像转换为各种灵巧机器人形态，包含超过 2 亿个编辑实例、26 种 URDF 配置，并建立了统一的基准协议与多维度评测体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手机器人操作是具身智能的核心，但采集具身感知的遥操作数据成本高昂。虽然大量第一视角人类手部视频可作为可扩展替代，但人类与机器人数据在外观、关节结构和相机视角上存在巨大差异，难以直接联合训练。现有通用图像编辑模型缺乏具身感知先验，无法弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 构建 HandEdit 数据集与基准：从五个不同来源数据集生成超过 2 亿个编辑实例，覆盖 26 种 URDF（13 种仅手部、13 种手-臂组合）。建立统一基准协议，包含 Hand-only 和 Hand-Arm 两个赛道，支持 URDF 条件化评测。采用多维度指标套件，包括通用相似度指标、VLM 判断和具身感知指标，对 11 个代表性图像编辑基线进行全面评估。

Result: 对 11 个代表性图像编辑基线进行了系统评估，使用包含通用相似度指标、VLM 判断和具身感知指标的多维度评测体系，验证了 HandEdit 作为图像编辑与机器人交叉领域关键资源的有效性。

Conclusion: HandEdit 填补了图像编辑与机器人学交叉领域的空白，推动具身感知编辑模型的发展，并支持从丰富的人类视频数据中实现可扩展的灵巧机器人学习，为更通用的具身智能铺平道路。

Classification reason: 论文提出 HandEdit，一个将人类手部/手臂图像编辑为灵巧机器人形态的具身感知图像编辑数据集与基准，核心是图像编辑任务，映射到 [CANONICAL] visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）。同时涉及机器人灵巧操作数据生成，与 world_model 方向下的具身智能相关，故也匹配 world_model 方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on egocentric hand-to-robot image editing for robotics, which is outside the user's core continuous/multimodal generation and world model priorities. Signals: image editing benchmark, dexterous robotics, egocentric video, embodiment-aware, 200M instances

Abstract: Robotic manipulation with dexterous hands is a cornerstone of Embodied AI, yet its progress is stifled by the high cost of collecting embodiment-aware teleoperation data. While abundant egocentric videos of human hands offer a scalable alternative, the profound discrepancies in appearance, articulation, and camera viewpoints between human and robotic data raise significant challenges for co-training. Though existing general image-editing models demonstrate strong capabilities, they lack necessary embodiment-specific priors to fully bridge this gap. In this work, we present HandEdit, a unified large-scale embodiment-aware image-editing dataset and benchmark specifically designed to transform human hands and arms into various dexterous robotic embodiments within egocentric frames. HandEdit comprises over 200M editing instances derived from five diverse source datasets, covering 26 distinct URDFs, including 13 hand-only and 13 hand-arm configurations. Alongside the dataset, we establish a unified benchmark protocol with two tracks: Hand-only and Hand-Arm, supporting URDF-conditioned evaluation. We conduct extensive evaluations of 11 representative image-editing baselines using a multi-dimensional metric suite, including generic similarity metrics, VLM-based judgment, and embodiment-aware metrics. HandEdit serves as a critical resource at the intersection of image editing and robotics: it advances embodiment-aware editing models while enabling scalable dexterous robotic learning from abundant human video data, paving the way for more generalizable Embodied AI.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出全局风格迁移（GST）范式，通过 Global Style Guidance（GSG）在扩散模型的 h-space 中学习残差全局风格偏移，以 Many-to-One 方式聚合艺术家多幅作品的共享风格并迁移到单张内容图像，同时提出 Content Alignment Guidance（CAG）保持内容语义结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风格迁移方法多为 One-to-One 方式，只能迁移单幅参考作品风格，难以捕捉艺术家的全局风格分布；而基于艺术家名字的文生图扩散模型存在文本诱导偏差，仅复现少数标志性作品模式。作者希望实现 Many-to-One 的全局风格迁移。

Method: 提出 Global Style Transfer（GST）范式，包含两个核心组件：1) Global Style Guidance（GSG），在扩散模型中间特征空间（h-space）中学习残差全局风格偏移，在固定 prompt 下仅从视觉统计中学习艺术家级风格语义，缓解文本依赖偏差；2) Content Alignment Guidance（CAG），一种免训练感知引导机制，在允许艺术家特定几何形变的同时保持内容图像的语义结构。

Result: 在 WikiArt 数据集上的实验表明，GST 相比现有风格迁移和基于扩散的艺术合成方法，在风格保真度、内容保持和输出多样性方面均取得更优表现。

Conclusion: 通过聚合艺术家多幅作品的全局风格并在扩散模型潜空间中学习风格偏移，可以有效克服文本诱导偏差和单作品风格局限，实现更忠实且多样的艺术家级风格迁移。

Classification reason: 论文提出全局风格迁移（GST）方法，将多位艺术家的作品风格迁移到内容图像上，属于图像风格化/编辑范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）子主题。

Importance reason: Style transfer with diffusion is a niche application, not aligned with the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: diffusion-based style transfer, h-space residual offset, Many-to-One style aggregation, content alignment guidance, WikiArt benchmark

Abstract: Artistic image synthesis aims to recreate the expressive visual identity of a target artist, yet existing methods often fail to capture an artist's global style. Conventional style transfer methods transfer the style of one or a few reference artworks to a content image in a One-to-One manner, making them effective for artwork-level stylization but limited in representing the broader stylistic distribution of an artist. Text-to-image diffusion models conditioned on artist names, such as '~ in Van Gogh style', offer greater flexibility, but they often suffer from text-induced bias and reproduce patterns from only a few iconic works. To address these limitations, we introduce Global Style Transfer (GST), an artistic image synthesis paradigm, in a Many-to-One manner, that aggregates multiple artworks from a target artist and transfers their shared global style to a single content image. For GST, we propose Global Style Guidance (GSG), which learns a residual global style offset in the intermediate feature space, or h-space, of a diffusion model under a fixed prompt. By learning artist-level style semantics purely from visual statistics, GSG mitigates text-dependent artistic bias. We further propose Content Alignment Guidance (CAG), a training-free perceptual guidance mechanism that preserves the semantic structure of the content image while allowing artist-specific geometric deformation. Experiments on WikiArt demonstrate that GST achieves superior stylistic fidelity, content preservation, and output diversity compared to existing style transfer and diffusion-based artistic synthesis methods.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出闭环框架 S2R：(1) S2R-Synthesis 在结构空间进行物理驱动的增强，并用训练好的视频扩散渲染器渲染逼真的反射视频，建模粗糙度模糊、厚度鬼影和反射率变化等玻璃效应；(2) S2R-Removal 是首个基于扩散的视频反射去除模型，通过反射感知的潜空间适配和单步像素-几何精化，在单次去噪步骤中恢复干净的透射内容；(3) S2R-Bench 是首个视频反射去除基准，支持全参考评测和真实世界人类感知评估。

Result: 在 S2R-Bench 和多个公开图像基准上取得最先进性能，推理速度甚至快于非扩散基线，并验证了 S2R-Synthesis 数据合成的有效性。

Conclusion: 物理驱动的反射仿真与扩散式去除相结合能有效解决视频反射去除问题，为缺乏成对数据的视频恢复任务提供了可借鉴的闭环范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频去反射（video dereflection），属于视频编辑/恢复类任务，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度相关，直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Specialized video dereflection task with limited overlap with the user's core continuous multimodal and autoregressive generation priorities. Signals: diffusion-based video dereflection, physics-grounded reflection simulation, video reflection removal benchmark, video diffusion prior adaptation, single-step denoising inference

Abstract: Videos captured through glass often contain reflections that degrade visual quality and interfere with downstream vision tasks. Although single-image reflection removal has been extensively studied, video reflection removal remains largely underexplored due to the lack of paired video data, temporally coherent removal models, and dedicated evaluation benchmarks. We present a closed-loop framework that unifies physics-grounded reflection simulation, diffusion-based video dereflection, and benchmark evaluation. Our S2R-Synthesis pipeline generates paired reflected and reflection-free videos by performing physics-grounded augmentation in the structure space and rendering realistic reflected videos with a trained video diffusion renderer; the augmentation models key glass-related effects including roughness-induced blur, thickness-induced ghosting, and reflectance variation. Based on the synthesized data, we introduce S2R-Removal, the first diffusion-based video reflection removal model, which adapts a pretrained video diffusion prior through reflection-aware latent adaptation and one-step pixel-geometric refinement, recovering the clean transmission in a single denoising step. We further build S2R-Bench, the first benchmark for video reflection removal, supporting both full-reference evaluation and real-world human perceptual assessment. Experiments on S2R-Bench and multiple public image benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance and faster inference than even non-diffusion baselines, and validate the effectiveness of S2R-Synthesis. Project page: .

Conclusion: ScaleVid 通过解耦前景变换与背景合成、采用伪源重建训练策略，实现了无需显式 3D 重建的高效几何感知视频物体缩放，兼顾几何合理性、时间一致性与背景一致性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频中物体的几何感知缩放（video object scaling），属于视频编辑/可控编辑范畴，与 [CANONICAL] visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）高度相关，故复用该子主题。

Abstract: Geometry-aware video object scaling aims to anisotropically resize the object along object-centric axes while preserving geometric plausibility, temporal coherence, and background consistency. Existing text-guided methods mainly operate in the 2D image plane, while depth-guided approaches provide coarse control and mesh-based methods require costly 3D reconstruction. We present a progressive two-stage training framework that decouples geometry-aware foreground transformation from background preservation and realistic video composition, without mesh-pixel alignment and explicit 3D reconstruction at inference. In both stages, geometrically perturbed pseudo-sources are constructed from real videos, while the original complete videos are retained as reconstruction targets. The first stage uses planar transformations to learn robust foreground-background composition, whereas the second introduces object-centric 3D deformation guidance for geometry-aware scaling. This pseudo-source reconstruction formulation enables real-video synthesis without paired real-world scaling targets. We construct complementary paired-geometry and real-background benchmarks and further evaluate on in-the-wild videos. Extensive experiments demonstrate superior geometric consistency, foreground fidelity, and background preservation, together with faster and more practical inference than methods requiring explicit 3D reconstruction.

</details>
