### [30] [LiveAnimate: Stable Long-Form Streaming Human Animation in Real-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11745)
*Yuxuan Zhang, Haozhong Xiong, Yubo Huang, Jiayi Song, Jinpeng Yu, Haofan Wang, Jiaming Liu, Ruihua Huang, Liwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 高优先级 (86.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LiveAnimate 是首个在十亿参数规模下实现实时流式与稳定长时生成的人体动画系统，基于 14B 视频 DiT，通过两阶段训练（Reference-Anchored Teacher-Forcing Adaptation 与 Block-wise Self-Forcing Distillation）将双向 DiT 改造为块因果自回归生成器，并引入 Pose-Retrieval Sink Attention（PR-Sink）有界 KV-cache 机制，在两张 H100 上实现 19.63 FPS 流式推理，三分钟长视频中保持近乎恒定的感知质量与身份一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 姿态驱动的人体动画在直播、远程临场和虚拟化身等交互应用中需要实时生成，但现有扩散系统生成单个片段需要数分钟到数小时，无法支持响应式交互。同时长时生成中身份外观保持也是关键挑战。

Method: 基于 14B 参数视频 DiT，采用两阶段训练：首先通过 Reference-Anchored Teacher-Forcing Adaptation 将预训练双向 DiT 适配为块因果自回归生成器，再通过 Block-wise Self-Forcing Distillation 将采样预算降至三步。引入 Pose-Retrieval Sink Attention（PR-Sink）有界 KV-cache 机制，包含永久锚定首块的 Static Sink、保存姿态检索历史块的 Dynamic Sink 以及三槽 Rolling Window，当姿态重现时恢复相关外观上下文而无需保留整个序列。结合 Ulysses 序列并行与算子融合实现高效推理。

Result: 在两张 NVIDIA H100 GPU 上实现 19.63 FPS 流式推理。在三分钟基准上，从最初 30 秒到最后一分钟保持近乎恒定的感知质量与身份一致性（IQA 4.047 vs 4.026），而先前系统显著退化或需要数小时离线计算。

Conclusion: LiveAnimate 在质量、延迟和时长三个维度上为交互式全身动画建立了新的工作点，证明了十亿规模视频 DiT 可实现实时流式长时生成，为直播、远程临场和虚拟化身等交互应用提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究姿态驱动的全身人体动画生成（pose-driven human animation），属于数字人与动作生成范畴，与 CANONICAL 子主题 avatar_motion_generation（数字人与动作生成）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong engineering for real-time long-form video DiT but tangential to the user's core continuous-language/world-model focus. Signals: video diffusion transformer, real-time streaming, long-form stability, KV-cache, autoregressive video generation

Abstract: Pose-driven human animation synthesizes a video of a target person from a single reference image and a driving pose stream. Real-time generation is essential for interactive applications such as live streaming, telepresence, and virtual avatars, yet diffusion-based systems require minutes to hours per clip, precluding responsive interaction. We present LiveAnimate, to our knowledge the first animation system to combine real-time streaming with stable long-form generation at billion scale, built on a 14B-parameter video Diffusion Transformer (DiT). A two-stage training pipeline first adapts a pretrained bidirectional DiT into a block-causal autoregressive generator through Reference-Anchored Teacher-Forcing Adaptation, and then reduces the sampling budget to three steps through Block-wise Self-Forcing Distillation. To preserve appearance over extended streams, we introduce Pose-Retrieval Sink Attention (PR-Sink), a bounded KV-cache mechanism combining a Static Sink that permanently anchors the first generated block, a Dynamic Sink that holds a pose-retrieved historical block, and a three-slot Rolling Window. When a pose recurs, PR-Sink restores the relevant appearance context without retaining the entire sequence, so memory and per-block latency remain constant regardless of stream duration. Together with Ulysses sequence parallelism and operator fusion, these designs enable 19.63\,FPS streaming inference on two NVIDIA H100 GPUs. On a three-minute benchmark, LiveAnimate maintains nearly constant perceptual quality and identity from the first 30 seconds to the final minute (IQA 4.047 vs.\ 4.026), while prior systems degrade substantially or require hours of offline computation for the same rollout. These results establish a new operating point in quality, latency, and duration for interactive full-body animation.

</details>


### [31] [UniSwap: Streaming Audio-Visual Identity Swapping for Talking Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11752)
*Yuxuan Zhang, Haozhong Xiong, Jiayi Song, Jinpeng Yu, Yang Shi, Jiaming Liu, Ruihua Huang, Liwei Wang*

Main category: cs.SD, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 高优先级 (81.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UniSwap 是首个用于说话视频的流式音视频联合身份替换框架，在单一音视频扩散 Transformer 中同时迁移参考外观与声音音色，通过 swap-and-reconstruct 训练、条件流式适配、自强制 DMD 蒸馏与多 LoRA 切换实现高效流式生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有说话视频身份替换方法对视觉和音频两个模态分别使用独立优化的模型，难以保证音视频一致性；同时缺乏对齐的跨身份训练对，且长视频流式生成存在曝光偏差与位置编码越界问题。

Method: 提出 UniSwap 框架：1) swap-and-reconstruct 流水线，从真实片段中移除视觉与语音身份并以原片段为重建目标；2) 从双向骨干出发，通过 In-context Pretraining 进行联合替换预训练，Conditional Streaming Adaptation 实现 block-causal KV-cached 生成，Efficient Self-forcing DMD 将采样步数从 30 降至 3；3) Efficient Multi-LoRA Switching 让三个 DMD 角色共享单一冻结骨干；4) Feature-RoPE Decomposition 保持缓存位置在训练范围内以支持稳定长序列推理。

Result: 实验表明 UniSwap 在音视频同步、身份保持、流式效率与长序列生成稳定性上均表现优异，实现了高效的流式音视频联合身份替换。

Conclusion: UniSwap 证明了在单一音视频扩散 Transformer 中联合进行流式身份替换的可行性，通过自监督重建与高效蒸馏解决了数据稀缺与推理效率问题，为说话视频身份替换提供了统一框架。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦说话视频中的身份替换（identity swapping），涉及说话头、肖像动画与面部重演，属于数字人与动作生成这一规范主题。同时涉及音视频联合生成，但核心是说话视频的身份替换，映射到 avatar_motion_generation 最合适。

Importance reason: Novel streaming audio-visual identity swap, but tangential to the user's core continuous-model, autoregressive generation, and world-model priorities. Signals: streaming generation, audio-visual diffusion transformer, identity swapping, talking video, DMD distillation

Abstract: Talking-video character replacement requires coordinated transfer of appearance and voice while preserving the source motion, scene, linguistic content, and audio-video timing. Existing methods use separately optimized models for the two modalities, making audio-visual consistency difficult to enforce. We present UniSwap, the first framework for streaming joint audio-visual identity replacement in talking videos. Given a source video, a reference image, and a reference voice clip, UniSwap transfers the reference appearance and vocal timbre within a single audio-visual diffusion transformer while preserving the source content and dynamics. To address the scarcity of aligned cross-identity training pairs, we introduce a swap-and-reconstruct pipeline that removes visual and vocal identity from real clips and uses the original clips as reconstruction targets. Starting from a bidirectional backbone, we progressively adapt the model through In-context Pretraining for joint replacement, Conditional Streaming Adaptation for block-causal KV-cached generation, and Efficient Self-forcing DMD for mitigating exposure bias and reducing sampling from 30 to 3 denoising steps per block. Efficient Multi-LoRA Switching enables the three DMD roles to share a single frozen backbone. Feature-RoPE Decomposition keeps cached positions within the training range, supporting stable long-form inference. Experiments demonstrate strong audio-visual synchronization, competitive identity preservation, efficient streaming, and stable long-form generation.

</details>


### [32] [Avatar-Forever: Decoupled Parallel Training for High-Quality Real-Time Infinite Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12107)
*Ruibin Li, Tao Yang, Zhiyuan Ma, Fangzhou Ai, Shilei Wen, Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 值得关注 (78.83) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Avatar-Forever 提出一种解耦并行训练框架，将高效生成与长时序鲁棒性作为两个独立能力并行训练，配合 ForeverCache 分块特征缓存，实现基于 22B 视频基础模型的高质量实时无限时长音频驱动数字人生成，单卡 H100 上达到 768x512@27.2 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频系统依赖顺序式、以蒸馏为中心的流水线来实现少步长视频生成，但存在两个局限：早期阶段的失败或分布偏移会影响后续优化，使训练难以收敛；蒸馏中心目标偏向短期生成，在长时序自回归误差累积下易出现质量退化。

Method: 提出解耦并行训练框架 Avatar-Forever，将生成效率与长时序鲁棒性视为两个独立能力并行训练。一个分支进行全参数蒸馏以训练高质量高效生成器；另一分支通过 Recovery-oriented Rollout Training (RRT) 训练轻量长时序适配器，提升长时序推理下的生成鲁棒性。同时引入 ForeverCache 分块特征缓存机制，减少流式推理中冗余的历史计算。

Result: 基于 22B 视频基础模型，支持无界音频驱动数字人生成，保持身份一致性、动作连贯性与视觉保真度，在单张 H100 GPU 上实现 768x512 高分辨率视频 27.2 FPS 的端到端吞吐。

Conclusion: 解耦并行训练避免了少步生成与长时序适配之间的目标冲突，简化了训练流程；ForeverCache 显著降低流式推理冗余计算，为稳定数字人提供了一条实用路径。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于无限时长、音频驱动的数字人（avatar）生成，属于数字人与动作生成这一规范主题。同时其长时序视频生成与流式推理特性与世界模型方向相关，但核心仍是数字人生成。

Importance reason: Solid engineering for real-time avatar streaming, but it is a niche application with no direct alignment to your continuous-model, world-model, or autoregressive generation priorities. Signals: decoupled parallel training, long-horizon robustness, feature caching, 22B video foundation model, real-time 27.2 FPS

Abstract: Existing streaming video systems often rely on sequential, distillation-centered training pipelines to enable few-step long-video generation. However, this paradigm suffers from two limitations. First, failures or distribution shifts introduced in earlier stages affect later optimization, complicating the training process to converge. Second, the distillation-centric objective favours short-term generation but is prone to quality degradation when autoregressive errors accumulate over long rollouts. We propose Avatar-Forever, a decoupled parallel training framework for high-quality real-time infinite interactive avatars. Instead of coupling generation efficiency and long-horizon robustness under a sequential distillation pipeline, we treat them as two independent capabilities that can be trained in parallel. One branch performs full-parameter distillation to train an efficient generator with high visual quality, while another trains a lightweight long-horizon adapter via Recovery-oriented Rollout Training (RRT), which improves generation robustness under long-horizon inference conditions. Our decoupled parallel training design simplifies the overall training process and avoids unnecessary objective conflicts between few-step generation and long-horizon adaptation. We further introduce ForeverCache, a chunk-wise feature caching mechanism to substantially reduce redundant history computation during streaming inference. Built upon a 22B video foundation model, Avatar-Forever supports unbounded audio-driven avatar generation while maintaining identity consistency, motion coherence, and visual fidelity, enabling an end-to-end throughput of high-resolution 768x512 videos at 27.2 FPS on a single H100 GPU and providing a practical path toward stable digital humans.

</details>

### [33] [Generative Semantic Segmentation via an Observable Semantic-Image Interface and Hierarchical Generator Evidence Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11537)
*Weize Cai, Yongqi Dong, Zhida Shao, Zixin Fu*

Main category: eess.IV, cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 值得关注 (71.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Semantic Prism 提出一种条件语义图像生成与精炼框架，通过扩散蒸馏的单步生成器渲染语义 RGB 图像，利用像素到类色码本的显式概率接口，并结合层级生成器证据对齐预测残差来精炼分布，实现确定性推理的语义分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式语义分割将结构化预测暴露为图像，但直接颜色解码易受颜色漂移和边界混合影响，而潜在特征解码器预测独立输出分布可能使渲染图像沦为中间可视化。作者希望设计一种既保留图像接口又提升分割精度的生成式框架。

Method: Semantic Prism 包含：(1) 扩散蒸馏的单步生成器渲染语义 RGB 图像；(2) 渲染颜色到固定类色码本的逐像素距离定义显式概率接口；(3) 层级生成器证据对齐（Hierarchical Generator Evidence Alignment）空间对齐多层级生成器特征，并用零初始化输出投影在接口 logit 空间预测加性残差；(4) Contextual Interface–Hierarchy Disagreement (C-IHD) 固定读出器，无需辅助预测器或额外前向即可对剩余像素误差排序。

Result: 在 Cityscapes 500 张验证集上达到 72.07% mIoU，比直接接口解码高 11.39 个点，期望校准误差 0.41%；BDD100K 上 62.22% mIoU；Cityscapes 训练模型在 ACDC 源冻结迁移下达 46.89% mIoU。C-IHD 在三个数据集上均提升像素误差排序的 AUPR（ACDC 上从 0.6580 提升到 0.7557）。

Conclusion: 联合对齐多层级证据能显著提升生成式语义分割精度，显式概率接口结合层级残差精炼是有效范式，C-IHD 提供无需额外模型的像素误差排序机制，且模型具备良好的跨域迁移能力。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Semantic Prism，一种基于扩散蒸馏的单步生成器进行语义图像生成与精炼的框架，属于图像生成方法（生成语义分割图作为图像），映射到多模态生成方向下的文生图与图像扩散规范子主题。

Importance reason: Semantic segmentation via generative image interface is methodologically interesting but falls outside your core continuous-multimodal, video/world-model, and autoregressive generation priorities. Signals: generative semantic segmentation, diffusion-distilled one-step generator, hierarchical evidence alignment, deterministic inference, Cityscapes/BDD100K/ACDC benchmarks

Abstract: Generative semantic segmentation exposes structured predictions as images, but direct color decoding is susceptible to color drift and boundary mixing, whereas latent-feature decoders that predict a separate output distribution may relegate the rendered image to an intermediate visualization. We present Semantic Prism, a conditional semantic-image generation-and-refinement framework with deterministic inference. A diffusion-distilled one-step generator renders a semantic RGB image; per-pixel distances from the rendered colors to a fixed class-color codebook define an explicit probabilistic interface. Hierarchical Generator Evidence Alignment spatially aligns multi-level generator features and uses a zero-initialized output projection to predict an additive residual in the interface logit space, retaining the image-defined interface as the reference for the final distribution. The interface and refined distributions further enable Contextual Interface--Hierarchy Disagreement (C-IHD), a fixed readout for ranking remaining pixel errors without an auxiliary predictor or additional forward pass. On the 500-image Cityscapes validation set, Semantic Prism achieves 72.07% mean intersection over union, 11.39 mIoU points above direct-interface decoding, with 0.41% expected calibration error. Matched-capacity ablations over three seeds support the benefit of jointly aligned multi-level evidence. A separately trained model attains 62.22% mIoU on BDD100K, while the Cityscapes-trained model reaches 46.89\% mIoU under source-frozen transfer to the Adverse Conditions Dataset with Correspondences, without target-domain adaptation. Across all three datasets, C-IHD consistently improves the area under the precision--recall curve for pixel-error ranking over maximum softmax probability on the same segmentation predictions; on ACDC, it raises AUPR from 0.6580 to 0.7557.

</details>


### [34] [Draw This First](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12064)
*Dazhi Zhong, Rowan Bradbury, Grant Davis*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 值得关注 (78.79) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 该论文反转了草图生成的典型范式：不按顺序绘制笔画，而是预测一个定义笔画绘制顺序的 2D 场。使用预训练的 latent flow-matching transformer 提供图像先验，训练 VAE 解码器预测顺序场、笔画掩码和笔画分割，将分割矢量化并按场排序，生成有序矢量草图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统草图生成按顺序逐笔画绘制，难以控制绘制顺序。作者希望反转这一范式，通过预测一个定义笔画顺序的 2D 场，实现从文本描述或图像反渲染生成有序矢量草图，并支持文本指令指定绘制顺序。

Method: 使用预训练的 latent flow-matching transformer 作为图像先验，预测中间表示；训练 VAE 的 decoder 预测顺序场、笔画掩码和笔画分割。将预测的分割矢量化成折线，并按顺序场排序，得到有序矢量草图。模型可从文本描述预测有序矢量草图，或将图像反渲染为有序矢量。

Result: 模型能够从文本描述或图像反渲染生成有序矢量草图，并遵循文本指令指定绘制顺序。

Conclusion: 通过预测绘制顺序场而非逐笔画生成，实现了可控的草图绘制顺序，为草图生成提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文使用预训练的 latent flow-matching transformer 作为图像先验来预测绘制顺序场，属于图像生成方法，映射到文生图与图像扩散这一 CANONICAL 子主题。

Importance reason: Novel inversion of sketch ordering via 2D fields, but tangential to the user's core continuous/multimodal generation priorities. Signals: sketch generation, order field, flow-matching prior, vectorization, text-to-sketch

Abstract: We invert the typical formulation of sketch generation: instead of drawing strokes in order, we predict a 2D field that defines the order in which strokes are drawn. We use a pretrained latent flow-matching transformer to supply the image prior to predict an intermediate representation, while training the VAE's decoder to predict the order field, stroke mask, and stroke segmentation. We vectorize the predicted segmentation into polylines and sort them by the field, producing an ordered vector sketch. Our model can predict an ordered vector sketch from a text description or derender an image into ordered vectors; for either, it follows text instructions specifying the order of drawing.

</details>

## 文生视频与视频扩散 [[Back]](#toc)

### [35] [Generative Video Compression Based on Hierarchical Referencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11618)
*Daowen Li, Ding Ding, Zifu Zhang, Kai Li, Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (85.35) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 GVCHR，一种基于分层引用的生成式视频压缩方法，通过分层组织潜在帧、耦合分层引用结构与分层质量结构，并引入分层时序上下文挖掘与分层注意力适配器，在视频扩散 Transformer 上实现高效编码与高质量生成重建，相比 SOTA 在 LPIPS 和 DISTS 上分别获得 50.5% 和 54.0% 的 BD-rate 增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的生成式视频压缩方法在潜在帧编码时未精心设计引用与质量结构，也未考虑帧级质量变化对去噪过程的影响，导致编码效率受限并加剧生成重建中的伪影传播。

Method: GVCHR 将潜在帧分层组织，选出的高质量参考帧同时服务于潜在编码与生成重建。编码端将分层引用结构与分层质量结构耦合，为被更频繁复用的低层帧分配更多比特；引入分层时序上下文挖掘以利用互补的短时与长时时序上下文。重建端将编码端层级融入分层注意力适配器，附加到视频扩散 Transformer 上，通过分层注意力限制每个潜在帧只关注同层或更低层参考，减少去噪过程中的伪影传播。

Result: 在多个基准上验证，相比先前 SOTA 方法，GVCHR 在 LPIPS 和 DISTS 指标上分别获得 50.5% 和 54.0% 的 BD-rate 增益，并带来明显更优的视觉质量。

Conclusion: 通过分层引用与分层质量结构的耦合设计，以及分层注意力适配器，GVCHR 有效提升了生成式视频压缩的编码效率与重建质量，验证了分层参考策略在扩散式视频压缩中的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文提出基于视频扩散 Transformer 的生成式视频压缩方法 GVCHR，核心是视频扩散模型与视频 DiT 架构，属于多模态生成方向下的文生视频与视频扩散主题。

Importance reason: Solid but niche video compression work; relevant to video diffusion and efficient coding, yet not central to the user's top continuous-model or autoregressive generation priorities. Signals: video diffusion transformer, hierarchical referencing, latent coding, BD-rate gains, artifact propagation

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative video compression has emerged as a promising paradigm to improve perceptual quality, where latent frames are required to be encoded efficiently while serving as denoising conditions. However, existing methods neither carefully design reference and quality structures during latent coding nor account for the impact of frame-level quality variation on denoising procedure, which limits coding efficiency and aggravates artifact propagation during generative reconstruction. In this paper, we propose GVCHR, Generative Video Compression based on Hierarchical Referencing. The key idea is to organize latent frames hierarchically, where the selected high-quality references benefit both latent coding and generative reconstruction. In latent coding, GVCHR couples a hierarchical reference structure with a hierarchical quality structure, assigning more bits to lower-layer frames that are reused more frequently as references. Built on this design, we introduce Hierarchical Temporal Context Mining to exploits complementary short- and long-term temporal context for effective latent coding. In generative reconstruction, the coding-side hierarchy is incorporated into a Hierarchical Attentive Adapter which is attached to a video diffusion transformer. This adapter uses hierarchical attention to restrict each latent frame to attend only to the same- or lower-layer references, thereby reducing artifact propagation during denoising. Experiments validate GVCHR on multiple benchmarks. Compared with the previous state-of-the-art method, GVCHR achieves 50.5% and 54.0% BD-rate gains in terms of LPIPS and DISTS, respectively, while also delivering clearly improved visual quality.

</details>


### [36] [GeoFlow: Efficient Driving Video Generation via Geometry-Aligned Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12203)
*Jiazheng Liu, Hang Li, Jiawei Zhang, Jiahe Li, Xiaohan Yu, Shengyin Fan, Jin Zheng, Xiao Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (86.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GeoFlow 提出利用多视图几何与空间自适应噪声注入构建几何对齐先验（GAP）分布作为扩散/流匹配的起始点，替代标准高斯噪声，从而缩短采样轨迹、减少推理步数，实现高效的驾驶视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流匹配在生成高保真驾驶视频方面表现出色，但受限于大量采样步骤导致的高推理延迟。现有方法依赖标准高斯源分布，将连续帧初始化为独立高斯噪声，忽略了驾驶视频中丰富的时空相关性，迫使模型从噪声中重新生成前一帧已存在的确定性场景结构，既计算冗余又易产生几何不一致。

Method: GeoFlow 提出几何对齐先验（GAP）分布作为采样起点。利用多视图几何和空间自适应噪声注入，构建一个介于源分布与数据分布之间的初始化分布，使采样轨迹更直更短。该框架可对基线模型进行少量微调即可提升少步生成质量。

Result: 实验表明 GeoFlow 在训练和推理效率上均有显著提升：仅需数小时对基线模型微调即可大幅提升少步生成质量；完全收敛训练后，可将 SOTA 视频生成所需的推理步数大幅减少。

Conclusion: 通过利用驾驶视频固有的时空相关性与几何结构作为先验，可以显著缩短扩散/流匹配的采样轨迹，从而在保持生成质量的同时大幅提升驾驶视频生成的训练与推理效率。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 GeoFlow 框架，利用几何对齐先验实现高效驾驶视频生成，属于视频扩散/流匹配生成方法，映射到 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 子主题。

Importance reason: Efficient driving video generation via geometry-aligned priors is solid but niche, with moderate relevance to your video generation and efficiency interests. Signals: video generation, efficient inference, flow matching, diffusion, driving video

Abstract: Generative models like Diffusion Models and Flow Matching have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in synthesizing high-fidelity driving videos, but are severely constrained by high inference latency due to the requirement of extensive sampling steps. We argue that this inefficiency stems from the prevailing reliance on a standard Gaussian source distribution, where consecutive frames are initialized as independent Gaussian noise. This paradigm disregards the rich spatiotemporal correlations inherent in driving videos, compelling the model to regenerate deterministic scene structures existing in previous frames from noise, which is both computationally redundant and prone to geometric inconsistency. To address this problem, we propose GeoFlow, a novel framework designed to achieve efficient driving video generation by harnessing explicit geometric priors. Instead of sampling from standard Gaussian noise, we leverage multi-view geometry and spatially-adaptive noise injection to construct a Geometry-Aligned Prior (GAP) distribution as starting point. This initialization bridges the gap between source distribution and data distribution, yielding a significantly straighter and shorter sampling trajectory. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GeoFlow can achieve remarkable efficiency of both training and inference: merely several hours of fine-tuning on baseline models can significantly boost few-step generation quality, while fully converged training drastically reduces number of inference steps required for state-of-the-art video generation.

</details>

### [37] [CORA-Diff: Confidence-Oriented Residual Acceptance for Efficient Diffusion Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11235)
*Yifan Wu, Yufeng Zhang, Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (89.38) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CORA-Diff 是一种免训练的扩散语言模型推理加速方法，利用原生轨迹信号（置信度与持续性）识别已稳定的 token 位置，仅对未解析位置继续去噪，从而减少重复的前向计算，在保持任务质量的同时实现 2.7x-13.14x 的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）并行更新多个 token，但实际解码器常使用固定的去噪步数。许多预测在早期就已稳定，但 blockwise 解码仍会继续直到所有位置都解析完成，导致大量重复的密集前向计算。现有加速方法依赖学习过滤器、修改分数、依赖模型或缓存机制，作者希望探索是否可以利用原生轨迹信号来识别那些很可能与确定性密集端点匹配的残差位置。

Method: CORA-Diff 是一种免训练方法，保留原始转移规则，仅对规则未解析的位置应用置信度与持续性（confidence-and-persistence）门控。被接受的 token 作为上下文保持可见，当所有位置都解析后 block 终止。该方法不需要修改骨干网络、学习接受模型或修改 logit。理论部分解释了为何高置信度、持续性的预测更可能匹配固定步数密集端点，并通过配对干预轨迹提供实证支持。

Result: 在匹配 Learn2PD 风格的 LLaDA 协议下，CORA-Diff 在所有八种任务-长度设置中运行时最低。任务分数在五种设置中达到或超过密集解码，最大下降仅 1.22 分。相比 EOS-aware 密集解码，在 GSM8K 和 HumanEval 上分别获得 2.70x 和 3.32x 加速；在固定步数 1024/1024 机制隔离协议下达到 13.14x；迁移到 Dream 无需重新调参即可获得 3.18x-3.53x 加速。

Conclusion: 原生置信度与持续性信号能够实现可靠的残差接受，在减少重复去噪计算的同时保持任务质量。CORA-Diff 证明了无需学习模型或修改 logit 的免训练加速方案在扩散语言模型推理中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CORA-Diff，一种针对扩散语言模型推理的免训练加速方法，通过置信度与持续性门控减少重复去噪计算。这属于生成模型（扩散语言模型）的推理效率优化，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly targets efficient diffusion language model inference, a high-priority subtopic, with strong speedups and quality preservation. Signals: efficient inference, diffusion language model, training-free acceleration, model efficiency, flow matching

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) update many tokens in parallel, yet practical decoders often use a fixed denoising horizon. Many predictions stabilize early, but blockwise decoding continues until all positions are resolved, causing repeated dense forward passes. Existing accelerators often rely on learned filters, modified scores, dependency models, or cache-specific mechanisms. We ask whether native trajectory signals can identify residual positions likely to match the deterministic dense endpoint. We propose CORA-Diff, a training-free method that preserves the original transfer rule and applies confidence-and-persistence gating only to positions that rule leaves unresolved. Accepted tokens remain visible as context, and the block terminates once all positions are resolved. This requires no backbone change, learned acceptance model, or logit modification. Our theory explains why high-confidence, persistent predictions are more likely to match the fixed-horizon dense endpoint, and paired post-intervention trajectories provide direct empirical support. We select one operating point on a separate GSM8K calibration subset and freeze it for all evaluations. Under a matched Learn2PD-style LLaDA protocol, CORA-Diff has the lowest measured runtime in all eight task-length settings. Task scores match or exceed dense decoding in five settings, and the largest observed drop is 1.22 points. Its incremental speedups over EOS-aware dense decoding are 2.70x and 3.32x on GSM8K and HumanEval. It also reaches 13.14x under the fixed-horizon 1024/1024 mechanism-isolation protocol and transfers to Dream without retuning at 3.18x-3.53x. These results show that native confidence and persistence enable reliable residual acceptance, reducing repeated denoising computation while preserving task quality.

</details>


### [38] [LoSA: Near-Lossless Sparse Attention for Training-Free Video Diffusion Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12032)
*Enhuai Liu, Yunke Wang, Yutong Wang, Changming Sun, Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (91.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LoSA 是一种训练无关的稀疏注意力方法，通过固定 99% 注意力质量阈值而非稀疏率，在早期密集步骤测量块注意力质量并冻结块索引，实现视频扩散模型近无损加速，最高达 3.2 倍加速且质量损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散 Transformer 采样成本高昂，每个去噪步骤都要对长 3D token 序列做自注意力，其二次复杂度随分辨率和时长增长而主导开销。现有稀疏注意力方法追求激进稀疏，导致进一步加速时注意力保真度损失不成比例。作者希望从相反方向出发：通过构造保证近无损保真度，再在此约束下尽可能移除计算。

Method: LoSA 提出固定保留质量阈值（99%）而非稀疏率：在一个早期密集步骤测量精确的块注意力质量，为每个头和查询块保留满足阈值的最小 key/value 块集合，并在所有后续去噪步骤中复用冻结的块索引。该方法无需训练，可单独使用或与特征缓存组合。

Result: 在 Wan2.1-1.3B 上，LoSA 单独实现 1.36 倍加速，VBench Overall 仅下降 0.06 分；与特征缓存组合后在 HunyuanVideo 上达到 3.2 倍加速，仅下降 0.02 分，而同等速度下最强稀疏基线下降 0.32 分。在三个视频扩散 Transformer 上、最高 3.2 倍加速下均取得最佳训练无关速度-质量权衡。

Conclusion: LoSA 证明在近无损保真度约束下，通过固定注意力质量阈值而非稀疏率，可以在视频扩散模型中实现显著且稳定的加速，同时保持极小的质量损失，为训练无关的稀疏注意力加速提供了新的设计范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出训练无关的稀疏注意力方法加速视频扩散模型采样，属于生成模型效率优化范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] efficient_generation（生成模型加速与高效化）。

Importance reason: Strong training-free acceleration for video diffusion with near-lossless quality, directly matching the user's efficiency and video generation priorities. Signals: video diffusion acceleration, training-free sparse attention, near-lossless 99% attention mass, 3.2x speedup with feature caching, Wan2.1/HunyuanVideo

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers are costly to sample: every denoising step applies self-attention over a long 3D token sequence, a quadratic cost that dominates as resolution and duration grow. Sparse attention reduces this cost without retraining, but existing methods pursue aggressive sparsity, where further speedup costs disproportionately more attention fidelity. We target the opposite end of this trade-off: fix near-lossless fidelity by construction, and remove as much computation as this constraint permits. Two observations make this regime practical: roughly 40% of block interactions can be removed while retaining 99% of the attention mass, and the high-mass support remains stable across denoising steps. We propose LoSA, a training-free sparse-attention method that fixes a retained-mass threshold of 99% rather than a sparsity ratio: it measures exact block attention masses at one early dense step, keeps, for each head and query block, the smallest key/value block set meeting the threshold, and reuses the frozen block indices for all remaining steps. On Wan2.1-1.3B, LoSA alone gives a $1.36\times$ speedup with a 0.06-point VBench Overall drop. The benefit is largest under composition: combined with feature caching, LoSA reaches a $3.2\times$ speedup on HunyuanVideo at a 0.02-point drop, versus 0.32 points for the strongest sparse baseline at comparable speed. Across three video diffusion transformers and speedups up to $3.2\times$, LoSA consistently achieves the best training-free speed-quality trade-off.

</details>


### [39] [XYZFlow:Scaling Multi dimensional Shortcut Flows for Efficient Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12276)
*Jinxiu Liu, Xuanming Liu, Kangfu Mei, Yandong Wen, Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (94.22) - Top 1 精读候选

TL;DR: XYZFlow 通过时间维度和空间维度两个正交方向对 flow matching 进行多维缩放，实现高效高保真图像生成，达到 7.2-8.5 倍教师模型加速并保持竞争性 FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真图像生成面临速度与质量的权衡。扩散模型视觉质量高但迭代采样成本高，现有高效方法主要依赖对预训练模型的蒸馏，过程困难且高度依赖教师模型质量。作者希望重新思考高效生成，通过多维缩放 flow matching 而非蒸馏来提升效率。

Method: XYZFlow 通过两个正交维度增强 flow matching 的表达能力：(1) 时间缩放（temporal scaling），使用非马尔可夫条件化，对完整去噪历史进行条件建模；(2) 空间缩放（spatial scaling），通过 Next Shortcut Prediction 顺序生成 patch，利用前面 patch 的去噪轨迹作为先验。作者将自回归建模视为隐式流直线化（flow straightening），更丰富的上下文可减少轨迹歧义。

Result: XYZFlow 在实验中达到 SOTA 性能，实现 7.2-8.5 倍教师模型加速，同时保持竞争性 FID。Next Shortcut Prediction 在质量-延迟权衡上优于模型缩放或步数减少。

Conclusion: 通过多维缩放 flow matching 而非依赖蒸馏，XYZFlow 展示了高效生成的新范式，其中时间与空间两个维度的结构化条件化能有效提升概率路径的可识别性与可学习性，实现更优的速度-质量权衡。

Classification reason: 论文提出 XYZFlow，通过多维 flow matching 缩放实现高效图像生成，实现 7.2-8.5 倍教师模型加速，属于生成模型加速与高效化方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] efficient_generation 子主题。

Importance reason: 提出多维缩放flow matching的高效生成框架，与连续语言模型和自回归生成方向高度契合。 Signals: flow matching, 高效生成, 自回归式生成, 图像生成加速, 非马尔可夫条件

Abstract: High-fidelity image generation faces a trade-off between speed and quality. Diffusion models produce strong visuals but require costly iterative sampling. Existing efficient methods mainly distill pretrained models into few-step samplers, a challenging process that depends heavily on teacher-model quality. In this paper, we introduce XYZFlow, a framework that rethinks efficient generation through multidimensional scaling of flow matching. Unlike single-step mappings, XYZFlow enhances expressivity by making probability paths more identifiable and learnable through structured multidimensional conditioning. We view autoregressive modeling as implicit flow straightening, where richer context reduces trajectory ambiguity. XYZFlow realizes this idea through two orthogonal dimensions: temporal scaling, which uses non-Markovian conditioning on the full denoising history; and spatial scaling, enabled by Next Shortcut Prediction, which sequentially generates patches using preceding patches' denoising trajectories as priors. Experiments show that XYZFlow achieves state-of-the-art performance, with 7.2-8.5X teacher speedups and competitive FID, while Next Shortcut Prediction delivers superior quality-latency trade-offs over model scaling or step reduction.

</details>

### [40] [Beyond Trial-and-Error: Agentic Optimization for Image-to-Video Adherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12290)
*Aman Tyagi, Hemanth Boinpally, Jonathan Chen, Douglas Gebert, Steven Hickson*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.MM

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (85.35) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Agentic Self-Improvement 框架，将图像到视频（I2V）生成重构为闭环目标导向优化：第一阶段用多模态 LLM 迭代优化提示词，结合 Davidsonian Scene Graph 查询与 Common Mistake Questions 检测语义一致性与伪影；第二阶段用贝叶斯优化联合优化随机种子与 CFG scale，由 Video-Text Adherence 评分引导，人类偏好胜率最高达 69%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代黑盒图像到视频（I2V）模型在专业工作流中缺乏细粒度控制与可靠性，其随机性导致提示词或超参数的微小变化产生截然不同的输出，常需低效的暴力试错过程。

Method: 提出两阶段 Agentic Self-Improvement 框架。第一阶段为迭代提示词优化循环，使用多模态 LLM 精炼输入提示词，并通过 Davidsonian Scene Graph（DSG）查询确保语义一致性、Common Mistake Questions（CMQ）检测伪影。第二阶段使用贝叶斯优化联合优化随机种子与 CFG scale，由包含新颖 Video-Text Adherence（VTA）评分在内的质量指标套件引导搜索。

Result: 在人类偏好研究中，通过 agentic 方法生成的视频显著优于基线输出，胜率最高达 69%，显著优于无引导的搜索方法。

Conclusion: 该框架为提升 SOTA 视频生成模型的可预测性与可控性提供了实用且可扩展的方法，推动视频生成从投机性探索走向可靠的生产级工具。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Agentic Self-Improvement 框架，通过提示词优化、贝叶斯优化种子与 CFG scale，以及 VTA 质量评分来提升文生视频的语义一致性与可控性，属于生成模型的可控性与对齐优化范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] generation_alignment（生成模型对齐与奖励）。

Importance reason: Focuses on prompt/seed optimization for I2V adherence rather than core model architecture or continuous representations, limiting direct alignment with top priorities. Signals: agentic optimization, image-to-video adherence, Bayesian optimization, prompt refinement, human preference win rate

Abstract: Modern black-box Image-to-Video (I2V) models offer powerful capabilities in automated content creation, yet their lack of fine-grained control and reliability presents significant challenges in professional workflows. Their inherent stochasticity causes minor variations in textual prompts or hyperparameters to yield drastically different outputs often necessitating inefficient, brute-force trial-and-error processes. To address these limitations, we introduce the ``Agentic Self-Improvement" framework, which reframes video synthesis into a closed-loop, goal-directed optimization. Our framework systematically navigates the generation parameter space using a novel two-stage approach. In the first stage, an iterative prompt optimization loop uses a multimodal Large Language Model (mLLM) to refine the input prompt. This refinement implements two automated evaluations: Davidsonian Scene Graph (DSG) queries ensure semantic adherence, and Common Mistake Questions (CMQ) for artifact detection. At the second stage, we use Bayesian optimization to efficiently co-optimize stochastic seeds and CFG scales. This search is guided by a suite of quality metrics, including the novel Video-Text Adherence (VTA) score derived from the DSG and CMQ evaluations. Our framework significantly outperforms unguided search methods: in human preference studies, videos generated via our agentic approach were strongly preferred over baseline outputs, achieving win rates up to 69\%. This work provides a practical and extensible methodology for enhancing the predictability and control of state-of-the-art video generation models, moving the field beyond speculative curiosities toward reliable, production-ready tools.

</details>


### [41] [Fingerprinting Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Collapsed Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11732)
*Yuanmin Huang, Chen Chen, Geng Hong, Xiaoyu You, Hui Xue, Zhenxing Qian, Mi Zhang, Min Yang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (68.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出一种基于"塌缩生成"（collapsed generation）现象的非侵入式文生图扩散模型指纹识别框架，利用特定输入条件下模型跨随机种子产生高度一致图像的特性作为模型专属行为签名，用于知识产权归属验证，支持白盒与黑盒两种访问场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有文生图扩散模型常以托管服务或可下载权重形式分发，当发生模型泄露、复制或未经授权微调时，知识产权保护成为关键问题。现有水印方法需要侵入式嵌入，本文提出无需嵌入水印的非侵入式指纹识别方案。

Method: 利用"塌缩生成"现象——某些输入条件会使模型跨多个随机种子产生高度一致的图像。该现象是模型内在的、依赖模型学习过程的属性，可作为模型专属行为签名。框架先在源模型上准备塌缩条件，然后在两种访问设置下验证嫌疑模型：(1)白盒管线访问，注入优化后的连续embedding；(2)黑盒API访问，通过自然语言提示查询。通过嫌疑模型是否复现源模型的塌缩行为来判定归属。

Result: 在UNet和Transformer架构的扩散模型上实验表明，塌缩生成指纹能以低混淆度区分不同源模型；在微调衍生模型以及常见和自适应模型级/查询级混淆下仍可验证，且只需适度的验证查询预算。

Conclusion: 塌缩生成可作为非侵入式扩散模型所有权验证的可靠内在证据来源，为专有模型知识产权保护提供了无需嵌入水印的新方案。

Classification reason: 论文研究文生图扩散模型的指纹识别与知识产权保护，属于生成模型的可控性与安全性范畴。虽然更偏向安全/版权，但最接近的规范子主题是"生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment），涵盖生成模型的可控性与保护机制。该子主题虽不完全精确，但按规则优先复用规范子主题。

Importance reason: Interesting IP-protection fingerprinting for diffusion models, but it is tangential to the user's core focus on continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: diffusion model fingerprinting, collapsed generation, IP protection, black-box verification, non-invasive

Abstract: Proprietary text-to-image diffusion models are increasingly distributed as hosted services and downloadable checkpoints, making their intellectual property (IP) protection an increasingly critical concern when model leakage, copying, or unauthorized fine-tuning is disputed. In this work, we present a non-invasive model fingerprinting framework based on \emph{collapsed generation}, a phenomenon where certain input conditions produce highly consistent images across multiple stochastic seeds. We show that collapsed generation is an intrinsic, model-dependent property of the learned generation process. These collapse-prone conditions therefore expose model-specific behavioral signatures, enabling reliable ownership verification without embedding invasive watermarks. After preparing conditions on the source model, the framework verifies a suspect model under two access settings: (1) white-box pipeline access, where optimized continuous embeddings can be injected into the generation process, and (2) black-box API-only access, where natural language prompts are queried through the service interface. In both cases, ownership evidence is measured by whether the suspect model reproduces the source model's collapse behavior across stochastic samplings. Extensive experiments across UNet- and transformer-based diffusion models show that collapsed generation fingerprints can distinguish different source models with low confusion. These fingerprints remain verifiable in fine-tuned derivatives and under common and adaptive model- or query-level obfuscations, while requiring only a modest verification query budget. Together, these results establish collapsed generation as a reliable intrinsic evidence source for non-invasive diffusion model ownership verification.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一个系统化的水下图像重建评测流程，用于评估重建方法的准确性、跨相机移动的一致性以及水参数的影响，并据此对比基于物理散射模型的传统方法与视觉语言模型（VLM）。结果显示VLM在整体上显著优于物理模型，原因在于其强大的图像先验，真实水下场景图像的结果也强烈印证了这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下图像重建（去除水的影响恢复清晰图像）长期以来缺乏系统化的评测方法，导致不同方法难以公平比较。作者希望建立一个可复用的评测流程，同时探究基于显式物理散射模型的方法与未显式训练物理模型的VLM之间的性能差异。

Result: VLM在整体上完全且显著地优于基于物理模型的方法，作者认为这主要归因于VLM强大的图像先验。在真实水下场景图像上的实验结果强烈印证了评测结论。

Conclusion: 系统化评测表明，VLM凭借强大的图像先验在水下图像重建任务上显著优于基于显式物理散射模型的方法，为水下重建方法的选择与未来研究方向提供了依据。

Classification reason: 论文提出水下图像重建的系统化评测流程，评估物理散射模型与VLM的重建效果，属于生成模型评测方向，映射到[CANONICAL] generation_benchmark（生成模型评测与数据集）。

Importance reason: Systematic evaluation of VLM vs physics-based underwater restoration, but outside core priority topics and lacks methodological novelty for generation. Abstract: Underwater image restoration consists of recovering an image which looks like there is no water present. To date, evaluation has not been systematic. This paper describes a systematic evaluation pipeline for underwater reconstruction, which can be used to assess a method for accuracy; consistency of reconstruction over camera moves; and the effect of water parameters. We use this pipeline to evaluate a range of current procedures, from models constructed using explicit but approximate physical models of scattering to Vision-Language Models (VLMs which are not currently trained with explicit physical models). Overall, VLMs wholly and significantly outperform physically based models in our evaluation, likely because of the importance of a strong image prior. Results on images of real underwater scenes strongly confirm the evaluation.

</details>


### [43] [TangPoetryBench: A Multi-Dimensional Benchmark and Rubric-Conditioned Evaluator for Poetry-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11452)
*Haoqi Hu, Tongji Luo, Li Zhang, Boning Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 值得关注 (68.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TangPoetryBench 是一个面向诗歌到图像生成的多维度评测基准，包含 1,280 张图像（320 首唐诗 × 4 个 SOTA T2I 模型）及十个维度的人工标注，并引入 PoemAutoEvaluator（PAE）——一个基于 rubric 条件的开源评测器，达到与 Claude 相当的水平，可泛化到未见生成器与宋词传统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 T2I 模型被越来越多地用于插图文学与文化内容，但缺乏衡量图像是否准确传达诗歌含义的方法。诗歌插图任务是多维度的，需兼顾视觉质量、意象忠实度、文化风格适配、无杂散文字及情感表达，其中意象与隐含情感是文字中未明说的深层要求。现有指标（CLIPScore、BLIPScore、VQAScore）只奖励字面图文对应，无法判断插图是否成功，更无法解释原因或区分最佳与最差模型。

Method: 构建 TangPoetryBench 基准：320 首唐诗 × 4 个 SOTA T2I 模型共 1,280 张图像，进行十个维度的质量控制人工标注。基于该数据揭示各 T2I 模型的共性与特异性优缺点。进一步提出 PoemAutoEvaluator（PAE），一个开放的、基于 rubric 条件的自动评测器，通过 rubric 条件化提示引导评测，达到与强专有评测器（Claude）相当的性能。

Result: PAE 达到与 Claude 相当的水平，能泛化到未见过的生成器以及第二种诗歌传统（宋词），使基准无需新的人工标注即可扩展到新图像。分析揭示了当前 T2I 模型在诗歌插图上的共性与模型特有优缺点，包括唤起诗歌隐含情感的能力。

Conclusion: 论文发布了 TangPoetryBench 基准、标注与评测器，为诗歌到图像生成提供了多维度评测框架，弥补了现有指标无法评估文学文化内容插图质量的空白，并提供了可扩展的自动评测方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TangPoetryBench 诗歌到图像生成的评测基准与自动评测器 PoemAutoEvaluator，属于生成模型评测与数据集方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] generation_benchmark（生成模型评测与数据集）。

Importance reason: Focuses on poetry-to-image evaluation, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: benchmark, evaluation, text-to-image, poetry, rubric-conditioned evaluator

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models are increasingly asked to illustrate literary and cultural content, yet we cannot measure how well an image renders the meaning of a poem. The task is many-sided: a good illustration must be visually sound, faithful to the poem's imagery and scene, culturally and stylistically apt, free of spurious text, and true to its emotion, and its deepest requirements, imagery and especially implicit emotion, are never stated in the words. Existing metrics (CLIPScore, BLIPScore, VQAScore) reward literal text-image correspondence and so cannot tell whether an illustration succeeds, let alone why, or even separate the best model from the worst. We introduce TangPoetryBench, a multi-dimensional benchmark of 1,280 images (320 classical Chinese Tang poems x 4 state-of-the-art T2I models) with quality-controlled human annotations across ten dimensions. Analyzing this data, we reveal the shared and model-specific strengths and weaknesses of current T2I models, including their ability to evoke a poem's implicit emotion. We further introduce PoemAutoEvaluator (PAE), an open, rubric-conditioned evaluator that reaches parity with a strong proprietary judge (Claude), generalizes to an unseen generator and a second poetic tradition (Song Ci), and lets the benchmark scale to new images without fresh human annotation. We release the benchmark, annotations, and evaluator.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究 AI 艺术检测器在生成器迁移（从 U-Net 潜扩散到 SD3.5 的 DiT 架构）下的鲁棒性。在零样本跨生成器设置下，深度学习检测器在分布内表现强但在迁移后显著退化，将大量 SD3.5 图像误判为人类作品；CLIP ViT-L/14 表现最佳，Grad-CAM 分析显示误判样本激活更弱更分散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图模型（尤其 DiT 架构）生成的图像越来越难以与人类艺术区分，引发版权、虚假信息、欺诈等担忧。然而大多数 AI 艺术检测器仅在单一生成器家族上训练和评测，对更新架构的鲁棒性未被充分探索。

Method: 构建基于 Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium 的十种艺术风格数据集（通过对留出的人类艺术作品反向提示生成）。在 U-Net 潜扩散艺术数据上训练五种检测器，并在 SD3.5m 数据集上进行零样本跨生成器评测，结合 Grad-CAM 分析误判样本的激活模式。

Result: 深度学习模型在分布内表现强，但在生成器迁移下显著退化，将大量 SD3.5m 图像误判为人类，而人类假阳性保持较低。CLIP ViT-L/14 整体表现最佳；Grad-CAM 显示误判样本的激活更弱且更分散。

Conclusion: 当前 AI 艺术检测器存在明显的泛化差距，无法可靠应对快速演进的生成架构。检测器应作为分层防御体系的一个组件，以在跨生成器场景下保持可靠性。

Classification reason: 论文研究 AI 艺术检测器在生成器迁移（generator shift）下的鲁棒性，属于对生成模型（文生图）检测/评测的泛化能力分析，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 generation_benchmark（生成模型评测与数据集）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on AI-art detector robustness rather than core generative modeling advances, with limited alignment to your priority subtopics. Signals: generator shift, AI-art detection, CLIP ViT-L/14, diffusion transformer, zero-shot evaluation

Abstract: Text-to-image generative models have advanced rapidly, with modern Diffusion Transformer architectures producing images that are increasingly difficult to distinguish from human-created artwork. This development has raised significant concerns regarding copyright protection, misinformation, fraud, impersonation, and the authenticity of digital content. Most AI-art detectors are trained and evaluated on the same generator family, leaving robustness to newer architectures underexplored. In this chapter, we analyze generator shift based on a Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium (SD3.5m) artwork dataset spanning ten art styles through reverse prompting of held-out human artwork samples. Five detectors are trained on U-Net-based latent diffusion artwork and evaluated in a zero-shot cross-generator setting on the SD3.5m dataset. Deep learning models perform strongly in-distribution but degrade under generator shift, misclassifying many SD3.5m images as human while human false positives remain low. The CLIP ViT-L/14 model performs best overall, while Grad-CAM analysis reveals weaker and more diffuse activation on false negatives. These findings highlight a generalization gap in current AI-art detectors and motivate the development of detectors as one component of a layered defense that remains reliable across rapidly evolving generative architectures.

</details>


### [45] [RA-ClipScore: Making Generative Model Evaluation More Interpretable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12088)
*Yifan Lu, Taras Kucherenko, Hedvig Kjellström, Judith Bütepage*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 RA-CLIPScore，一种更可解释的生成模型评测指标，通过双提示解耦竞争属性并利用局部 patch token 捕捉细粒度区域语义，将 CLIP 评测扩展到空间分布对齐，衡量生成对象是否符合训练数据中的位置先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统指标如 FID 仅提供标量分数，诊断信息有限；CLIP 类指标虽能进行语义评测，但受限于 CLIP 训练范式，难以进行属性级分析，也无法评估生成对象是否符合训练数据的空间位置先验。

Method: RA-CLIPScore 引入双提示（dual prompts）来解耦相互竞争的属性，并利用局部 patch token 捕捉细粒度区域语义，从而将 CLIP 评测扩展到空间分布对齐，衡量生成对象是否遵循训练数据中的位置先验。

Result: 大量实验表明 RA-CLIPScore 在分布错位或部分无关文本属性场景下，比先前方法提供更稳健、更可解释的评测；能揭示生成模型的空间偏差；用户评测确认基于 RA-CLIPScore 的区域单属性散度比现有语义指标更贴近人类对视觉多样性的感知。

Conclusion: RA-CLIPScore 为生成模型评测提供了更可解释、更稳健的指标，能够同时评估属性分布与空间分布对齐，并揭示生成模型的空间偏差，与人类感知更一致。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 RA-CLIPScore 这一新的生成模型评测指标，用于评估生成图像在属性与空间分布上的对齐程度，属于生成模型评测与数据集方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] generation_benchmark 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on generative model evaluation metrics rather than core generation methods, with limited alignment to your priority subtopics. Signals: evaluation metric, CLIP-based, spatial alignment, interpretability, generative model evaluation

Abstract: Generative models can produce images nearly indistinguishable from real data, yet rigorous and interpretable evaluation remains challenging. Conventional metrics such as FID provide only scalar scores with limited diagnostic insight. Widely adopted CLIP-based metrics enable semantic evaluation beyond simple training class labels, but inherit limitations from CLIP's training paradigm that restrict attribute-wise analysis. We propose RA-CLIPScore, a novel metric that mitigates these issues and extends CLIP-based evaluation to spatial distribution alignment, measuring whether generated objects adhere to the positional priors found in the training data. RA-CLIPScore introduces dual prompts to decouple competing attributes and leverages local patch tokens to capture fine-grained regional semantics. We evaluate image generative models on their ability to match both attribute and spatial distributions of the training data. Extensive experiments show that RA-CLIPScore provides more robust and interpretable evaluations than prior methods, particularly under distribution misalignment or partially irrelevant textual attributes. We further demonstrate how it reveals spatial biases in generative models. User evaluations confirm that Regional Single Attribute Divergence based on our RA-CLIPScore aligns more closely with human perception of visual diversity than existing semantic metrics.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本TTS系统在推理时通常依赖音频提示的转录文本，这限制了跨语言声音克隆的应用，因为真实场景中的参考音频往往没有转录。作者旨在构建一个无需参考音频转录即可进行跨语言克隆的多语言零样本TTS系统。

Method: Confucius4-TTS采用两阶段架构：1) 基于LLM的文本到语义（T2S）模块，使用可学习说话人编码器从自监督语音表征中提取音色特征，将文本转换为语义token；2) 条件flow-matching的语义到声学（S2A）模块，将预测的语义token转换为mel频谱。系统在大型多语言语音数据上训练，支持14种语言，同时支持有参考转录时的延续克隆。

Result: 在CV3-Eval跨语言基准上，Confucius4-TTS在六个方向上平均WER为3.73%；在内部跨语言测试集上，在最近的开源和商业系统中获得人类评估最佳平均总体排名。

Conclusion: Confucius4-TTS通过可学习说话人编码器与两阶段架构，实现了无需参考转录的高质量跨语言零样本声音克隆，在可理解性和说话人相似度上均达到领先水平，并开源了代码、模型权重和演示。

Importance reason: TTS is outside the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video/image generation, though it shares flow-matching and learnable speaker encoder techniques. Signals: flow matching, zero-shot TTS, cross-lingual voice cloning, learnable speaker encoder, two-stage T2S+S2A

Abstract: Recent advances in zero-shot text-to-speech (TTS) have substantially improved speech quality and voice cloning fidelity. However, many zero-shot TTS systems still depend on audio prompt transcripts at inference time. This dependency limits cross-lingual voice cloning, since in-the-wild reference audio is often untranscribed. In this technical report, we present Confucius4-TTS, a multilingual zero-shot TTS system that supports 14 languages and performs both intra-lingual and cross-lingual reference cloning without requiring transcripts of audio prompts. Confucius4-TTS follows a two-stage architecture, consisting of text-to-semantic (T2S) and semantic-to-acoustic (S2A) modules. The LLM-based T2S module uses a learnable speaker encoder to extract timbre features from self-supervised speech representations, and the conditional flow-matching S2A module converts the predicted semantic tokens into mel-spectrograms. The same model also supports continuation cloning when a reference transcript is available. Confucius4-TTS is trained on large-scale multilingual speech data. It achieves high intelligibility and speaker similarity on public benchmarks. On the CV3-Eval cross-lingual benchmark, Confucius4-TTS obtains an average WER of 3.73% across six directions. On our internal cross-lingual set, it achieves the best average overall rank in human evaluation among recent open-source and commercial systems. We release code, model checkpoints, and demos at .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 V2A 生成方法将视频压缩为单一特征表示，丢失时序动态与细粒度视觉信息；同时依赖重建式训练目标，与人类对音频质量与适配度的主观感知相关性差。

Method: (1) 采用双视频表征：全局上下文结合帧级特征以保留时序动态与语义细节；(2) 借鉴 RLHF 思想，使用在线直接偏好优化（online-DPO）从偏好判断中微调扩散式 V2A 模型，提升感知质量与对齐；(3) 提出 Dual-scale Diffusion Search（DDS），一种测试时缩放算法，在推理阶段自适应优化输出保真度。

Result: 实验表明 HarmoniDPO 在音视频同步和主观音频质量上均优于现有最先进方法，能生成更真实、更符合人类偏好的视频配音频。

Conclusion: 将偏好优化与扩散式 V2A 生成结合，配合双视频表征与测试时缩放，可有效解决时序同步与感知质量问题，为视频配音频生成提供稳健方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频到音频（V2A）生成，属于音视频联合生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 audio_av_generation（音频与多模态联合生成）高度匹配，直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Solid V2A method with preference optimization, but outside the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and autoregressive/diffusion video generation. Signals: video-to-audio generation, diffusion model, online-DPO, preference optimization, test-time scaling

Abstract: Video-to-audio (V2A) generation faces significant challenges in achieving precise temporal synchronization and high perceptual quality due to the complex, ambiguous relationship between visual and auditory cues. Existing methods typically compress video inputs into single feature representations, leading to significant loss of temporal dynamics and fine-grained visual information. These approaches also rely on reconstruction-based training objectives that poorly correlate with human perceptual judgments of audio quality and appropriateness. We propose HarmoniDPO, a novel framework that integrates preference-based optimization into diffusion-based V2A generation to address these limitations. (1) Our approach leverages a dual video representation: combining global context with frame-wise features to preserve temporal dynamics and semantic detail. (2) Inspired by reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), HarmoniDPO employs online Direct Preference Optimization (online-DPO) to fine-tune a diffusion-based V2A model from preference judgments, enhancing perceptual quality and alignment. (3) Additionally, we introduce Dual-scale Diffusion Search (DDS), a test time scaling algorithm that adaptively optimizes output fidelity during inference. Experiments demonstrate that HarmoniDPO outperforms state-of-the-art methods in audio-video synchronization and subjective audio quality, offering a robust solution for generating realistic, human-preferred audio from video.

</details>

Abstract: Video-to-music generation has drawn growing interest for its role in conveying the emotion of visual media, including film. Progress in the field, however, is hampered by a reproducibility gap: models are often trained on crawled corpora referenced through YouTube URLs that may be deleted, with the underlying data often difficult and time-consuming to retrieve. To address this, we introduce the Open Screen Soundtrack Library version 2 (OSSL-v2), a self-hosted corpus of 34,343 video clips totaling 246.4 hours, sourced from public-domain films. Unlike crawled corpora, OSSL-v2 is reproducible (i.e., not subject to link rot) and copyright-conscious, yet still large enough to train functional video-to-music models. We then use this film-domain corpus to study dialogue as a conditioning signal for video-to-music generation, motivated by the close temporal coupling between film music and on-screen speech. Specifically, we augment existing models' video cross-attention with a time axis and modulate it frame-by-frame with the dialogue track. Evaluated on both public-domain and commercial films, our approach shows improvement over the state-of-the-art baselines. The dataset is available at .

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CookVoice，一个统一的语音/歌声生成框架，涵盖 TTS、歌声生成、语音克隆、语音转换与编辑等多任务。这属于音频生成方向，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "audio_av_generation（音频与多模态联合生成）" 高度匹配，因此复用该子主题。

Abstract: Human voice generation has made rapid progress in speech generation, singing voice generation, voice cloning, and voice editing. However, most existing systems are designed for specific tasks and often rely on task-dependent architectures, control signals, or autoregressive decoding, limiting fine-grained controllability and inference efficiency. In this paper, we propose CookVoice, a unified framework for multimodal, multi-style, and multi-task human voice generation. CookVoice decomposes the human voice into three key factors: content, prosody, and style, enabling both speech and singing voice generation within a unified model. To achieve precise and flexible controllability, we design a flexible alignment strategy that maps text, style, and prosody control signals onto the frame-level of spectrogram. This design allows CookVoice to support a wide range of tasks, including text-to-speech, text-to-singing voice, style-controllable generation, voice mimicry, voice conversion, and voice editing. Experimental results show that CookVoice achieves generation quality comparable to existing Text-to-Speech and text-to-singing voice baselines, while providing stronger style and prosody controllability. Moreover, CookVoice achieves comparable performance to large-scale baselines with only 43.51 million parameters and efficient inference using as few as 4 ODE steps, making it a practical solution for real-world human voice generation applications. Demo page is available at .

</details>

### [50] [How China-Origin Vision-Language Models Move from Refusal to Reframing in State Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11816)
*Guang Yang, Fengchen Liu, Alex Wang, Homa Hosseinmardi, Amir Ghasemian*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CR, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (51.68) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了9个视觉语言模型（7个中国来源、2个非中国）在10个政治敏感话题上的审查行为，构建了200条核心条目基准和7种视觉抽象探针，通过4种诱导范式和2种提示语言进行21,708次试验。研究发现中文提示使状态对齐框架概率约增加3倍，中国来源模型比非中国模型更倾向于重新框架而非拒绝，且跨Qwen四代模型，显式拒绝下降而状态对齐框架上升——审查从可见的拒绝行为迁移为不可见的流畅重新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究记录了基于文本的中国来源大语言模型中的状态对齐扭曲，但多模态系统中是否以及以何种形式出现这一问题尚未被系统检验。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统考察VLM中的多模态审查行为。

Method: 构建200条核心条目的平衡基准（覆盖10个政治敏感话题）和7种视觉抽象探针，运行9个VLM（7个中国来源、2个非中国），跨4种诱导范式和2种提示语言，共21,708次试验。每个响应由两个独立的前沿LLM法官在6个维度（显式拒绝、信息完整性、视觉接地、状态对齐框架、语言一致性、响应长度）上审计，并在200条试验样本上与3位人类专家验证。将审查分解为独立信号而非单一拒绝分数。

Result: (i) 中文提示使状态对齐框架概率约增加3倍；(ii) 中国来源模型比非中国模型更倾向于重新框架（幅度1.6-3.2倍）；(iii) 效果在纯文本政治评论中最强（36.5%），且由对描绘主体的识别而非像素细节所门控，即使在标志性图像的剪影下仍持续；(iv) 跨Qwen四代，状态对齐框架上升而显式拒绝下降。

Conclusion: 审查行为正从可见的拒绝（refusal）迁移为不可见的流畅重新框架（reframing），这从根本上是一个人机交互问题：它移除了用户赖以识别信息被扣留的信号。作者主张将多模态审查分解为独立信号而非单一拒绝分数，以更准确地刻画模型行为。

Classification reason: 论文研究中国来源视觉语言模型（VLM）在政治敏感话题上的审查与状态对齐行为，属于多模态安全与可靠性范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态安全与攻防 子主题。

Importance reason: This paper is about political censorship in VLMs, which is outside your configured research directions and priority subtopics. Signals: multimodal safety, state alignment, VLM censorship, refusal vs reframing, China-origin models

Abstract: State-aligned distortion has been documented in China-origin text-based large language models (LLMs), but whether, and in what form, it arises in multimodal systems has not been systematically examined. We construct a balanced benchmark of 200 core entries spanning ten politically sensitive topics, plus a seven-variant visual-abstraction probe, and run nine vision-language models (VLMs), seven China-origin and two non-China, across four elicitation paradigms and two prompt languages, yielding 21,708 trials. Each response is audited on six dimensions -- explicit refusal, information integrity, visual grounding, state-aligned framing, language consistency, and response length -- by two independent frontier LLM judges, validated against three human experts on a 200-trial sample. Measuring each dimension separately lets us decompose multimodal censorship into individual signals rather than a single refusal-based score; in particular, refusal and framing are measured independently, so a model can stop refusing while still reframing. We find that (i) Chinese-language prompting roughly triples the odds of state-aligned framing, within every model; (ii) China-origin models reframe more than non-China models (direction robust across judges and human raters; magnitude 1.6--3.2x); (iii) the effect is strongest in text-only political commentary (36.5%) and is gated by recognition of the depicted subject rather than pixel detail, persisting even at silhouette for iconic images; and (iv) across four Qwen generations, state-aligned framing rises while explicit refusal falls: censorship migrates from a visible act (refusal) to an invisible one (fluent reframing). We argue this shift to invisible reframing is fundamentally a problem of human-AI interaction: it removes the very signal users rely on to recognize that information has been withheld.

</details>

### [51] [LookBack: Where and How to Score LVLM Responses via Visual Reference Usage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11847)
*Beomsik Cho, Jinhyeong Kim, Dongseok Lee, Jaehyung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (55.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 LookBack，一种无需训练的 LVLM 响应评分方法，通过视觉回看分数（visual lookback score）衡量每个响应 token 对图像 token 的引用强度，增强 token 似然以改进 Best-of-N 选择，缓解 LVLM 对图像的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM 不仅继承文本级幻觉，还会对图像产生幻觉，生成与所见内容不符的流畅响应。现有从 LLM 借鉴的基于置信度的评分指标对 LVLM 不足——诊断显示移除输入图像几乎不改变基于置信度的选择，说明输出空间置信度主要捕捉文本合理性而非与图像的一致性。

Method: 提出 LookBack，一种训练免费的 LVLM 响应评分方法。它用视觉回看分数（visual lookback score）增强 token 似然，该分数是一种轻量度量，衡量每个响应 token 对图像 token 的引用强度，从而在 Best-of-N 选择中优先选择与图像更一致的响应。

Result: 在四个基准和三个模型上，LookBack 一致地优于现有基线，在 Best-of-N 选择上取得改进，且额外开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: LookBack 通过引入视觉回看分数有效弥补了现有置信度指标在 LVLM 上的不足，为 LVLM 响应评分提供了更可靠的、与图像对齐的度量，且无需额外训练。

Classification reason: 论文关注 LVLM 响应评分中图像幻觉问题，即模型产生与图像不符的流畅响应，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_hallucination。

Importance reason: Focuses on LVLM hallucination scoring, which is adjacent to but not central to the user's priority on continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: LVLM hallucination, training-free scoring, Best-of-N selection, visual reference usage, token likelihood augmentation

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) integrate visual perception with language generation, enabling responses that span image understanding and complex reasoning. However, LVLMs do not just inherit the text-level hallucinations; they also hallucinate against the image, producing fluent responses ungrounded in what they see. This makes LVLM response scoring inherently harder, and our diagnostics show that existing confidence-based metrics adopted from LLMs are insufficient for LVLMs. Specifically, removing the input image barely changes confidence-based selection, suggesting that output-space confidence primarily captures textual plausibility rather than agreement with the image. To address this gap, we propose LookBack, a training-free LVLM response scoring method that augments token likelihood with visual lookback score, a lightweight measure of how strongly each response token refers to image tokens. Across four benchmarks and three models, LookBack consistently improves Best-of-$N$ selection over existing baselines with negligible additional overhead.

</details>


### [52] [Test-Time Hallucination Control in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11474)
*Mehran Tamjidi, Hamidreza Dastmalchi, Ali Cheraghian, Mohammadreza Alimoradijazi, Aijun An, Hossein Rahmani*

Main category: cs.CV

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

TL;DR: 提出 Test-Time Hallucination Mitigation (TTH) 方法，一种训练无关的测试时幻觉抑制技术。通过零样本多模态分类器（MMC）作为 token 验证器生成基于输入图像的辅助 logits，与 LVLM 原始输出在 token 级别融合，并采用基于熵的加权方案，在多个 LVLM 家族和基准上一致提升准确率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型（LVLM）中的物体幻觉问题严重阻碍其在真实场景中的可靠性。现有缓解策略中，基于训练的方法性能好但成本高昂；训练无关方法要么需要多轮解码增加计算开销，要么以模型特定方式修改内部状态而可能损害预训练知识。作者希望提出一种既高效又通用的训练无关方法，同时避免上述两类局限。

Method: 提出 TTH 方法：引入 token-validator 模块，实现为零样本多模态分类器（MMC），生成基于输入图像的辅助 logits。这些 logits 在 token 级别与 LVLM 原始输出融合，针对从候选池中选出的物体 token 进行处理。采用基于熵的加权方案，使预测更稳健准确。

Result: 在多个 LVLM 家族和多样化基准上的大量实验表明，TTH 一致地提升了准确率和鲁棒性，验证了其泛化性和实际有效性。

Conclusion: TTH 作为一种训练无关的测试时幻觉抑制方法，通过 token 级 logits 融合与熵加权，在保持高效性的同时有效缓解 LVLM 物体幻觉，且具有良好的跨模型泛化能力。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于大视觉语言模型（LVLM）中的物体幻觉缓解问题，提出测试时训练无关的幻觉抑制方法，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "multimodal_hallucination"。

Importance reason: Solid training-free hallucination mitigation work, but it targets reliability rather than the user's core continuous/multimodal generation priorities. Signals: training-free, test-time, hallucination control, LVLM, token-level fusion

Abstract: Object Hallucination in large vision-language models (LVLMs), where models generate non-factual content about input images, remains a critical barrier to their reliability in real-world applications. Existing mitigation strategies can be categorized into training-based and training-free methods. Training-based methods often achieve strong performance but are costly, requiring extensive computational resources, large-scale data, and time-consuming fine-tuning. Training-free approaches are particularly appealing due to their efficiency. However, existing training-free methods either require multiple decoding rounds, which adds computational overhead, or modify internal states in a model-specific way that risks degrading pretrained knowledge. We propose Test-Time Hallucination Mitigation (TTH) method, a novel training-free method that addresses both limitations. TTH introduces a token-validator module, implemented as a zero-shot Multi-Modal Classifier (MMC), to generate auxiliary logits grounded in the input image. These logits are fused with the original LVLM outputs at the token level for object tokens selected from a candidate pool. An entropy-based weighting scheme is then applied to enable robust and accurate predictions. Extensive experiments across multiple LVLM families and diverse benchmarks demonstrate that TTH consistently improves accuracy and robustness, underscoring its generalizability and practical effectiveness. Code is released at

</details>

### [53] [Retry, Switch, or Abstain? Learning Strategy-Aware Tool-Use Policies via Controlled Error Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11977)
*Chaoran Chen, Vy Nguyen, Ziji Zhang, Abhinav Gullapalli, Ziyi Wang, Yuxuan Lu, Dakuo Wang, Jing Huang, Zhou Yu, Jin Lai*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 BENCH2ROBUST 框架，将无故障工具使用基准转换为受控随机环境，研究工具故障下 LLM 智能体的鲁棒恢复策略（重试/切换/放弃），并引入 Bayesian Tool Memory (BTM) 与课程化强化学习两种互补干预手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型 LLM 智能体通常在工具调用可靠的环境中训练和评估，但实际部署中工具可能瞬时、持续或静默失败。仅靠重复重试不足以实现鲁棒恢复，智能体需要学会重试、切换或识别无可行路径后停止。现有研究缺乏对这类故障场景的系统性评估与训练方法。

Method: 提出 BENCH2ROBUST 框架，将无故障工具使用基准转换为场景可控可解性的随机环境，显式要求智能体在路径耗尽后重试、切换或停止。研究两种互补干预：结构化运行时恢复上下文（Bayesian Tool Memory, BTM）与课程化强化学习（curriculum-controlled RL）。

Result: 在 4 个模型家族 7 个模型及两个多轮基准家族上，工具故障产生近乎普遍的鲁棒性差距。在留出 Retail 任务上，BTM 无需重训即可将鲁棒性提升最多 16.8 个百分点；RL 学习到互补的恢复行为，在无推理时 BTM 的情况下依然有效。两者结合在注入下达到 40.8-45.5%，同时保持无故障性能。

Conclusion: 鲁棒工具使用受益于将环境特定的恢复知识与学习到的恢复行为相结合。BTM 提供结构化运行时上下文，RL 学习互补的恢复策略，两者结合能显著提升工具故障场景下的鲁棒性而不牺牲无故障性能。

Classification reason: 论文研究工具使用型 LLM 智能体在工具故障下的鲁棒恢复策略，通过课程化强化学习（curriculum-controlled RL）学习重试/切换/放弃策略，属于多模态强化学习与对齐方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] multimodal_rl_alignment。

Importance reason: Focuses on tool-use robustness for LLM agents, which is outside the user's core continuous/multimodal generation and world-model priorities. Signals: tool-use agents, robustness, reinforcement learning, error injection, LLM agents

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents are commonly trained and evaluated in environments where tool calls succeed reliably, yet deployed tools can fail transiently, persistently, or silently. Robust recovery therefore requires more than repeated retries: an agent may need to retry the same path, switch to an alternative, or recognize that no viable path remains. We present BENCH2ROBUST, a framework that converts failure-free tool-use benchmarks into controlled stochastic environments with scenario-controlled solvability, where episodes explicitly require retrying, switching, or stopping after available paths are exhausted. We use BENCH2ROBUST to study two complementary interventions: structured runtime recovery context through Bayesian Tool Memory (BTM), and curriculum-controlled reinforcement learning. Across 7 models from 4 families and two multi-turn benchmark families, tool failures produce a near-universal robustness gap. On held-out Retail tasks, BTM improves robustness by up to 16.8 percentage points without retraining, while RL learns complementary recovery behavior that remains beneficial without inference-time BTM. Combining the two reaches 40.8-45.5% under injection while preserving failure-free performance. These results suggest that robust tool use benefits from combining environment-specific recovery knowledge with learned recovery behavior.

</details>

*Byungoh Ko, Jinyoung Park, Jongha Kim, Jeehye Na, Jaewon Cho, Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Contextual Preference Gain (CPG) 指标衡量 DPO 对上下文信息的利用程度，发现标准 DPO 及其变体对上下文利用不足，进而提出 Context-Calibrated DPO (C²-DPO) 直接最大化 CPG 同时保持偏好排序，显著降低 MLLM 对象幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 仍存在对象幻觉问题，DPO 通过偏好优化缓解幻觉，但现有方法是否真正利用了偏好数据中的相关上下文尚不明确。作者希望探究 DPO 对上下文的利用程度，并据此改进以降低幻觉。

Method: 提出 Contextual Preference Gain (CPG) 指标，量化模型在提供相关上下文时偏好强度的提升。基于 CPG 分析发现标准 DPO 及其变体对上下文利用有限，进而提出 Context-Calibrated DPO (C²-DPO)，在保持原始偏好排序的同时直接最大化 CPG，使模型更充分地利用上下文信息。

Result: 在多个基准上，C²-DPO 在保持通用推理能力的同时显著降低幻觉，将 Qwen2-VL-Instruct-2B 在 Object HalBench 上的幻觉率相对降低 36%。

Conclusion: CPG 与幻觉率呈一致负相关，说明上下文利用程度是影响幻觉的关键因素；C²-DPO 通过直接优化 CPG 有效增强上下文利用，从而在不牺牲推理能力的前提下大幅缓解对象幻觉。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Context-Calibrated DPO (C²-DPO)，通过偏好优化缓解 MLLM 的对象幻觉，属于多模态 DPO/偏好学习与对齐方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐 (multimodal_rl_alignment)。

Importance reason: Solid MLLM alignment work, but it targets DPO/hallucination rather than the user's core continuous-model and generation priorities. Signals: MLLM alignment, DPO, object hallucination, preference optimization, context calibration

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made rapid progress, yet they still exhibit object hallucination, generating plausible but incorrect descriptions that are inconsistent with the visual input. Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) mitigates this by training models to prefer non-hallucinated responses over hallucinated ones, and recent efforts further enrich the preference data with relevant context. However, it remains unclear whether DPO actually leverages such context. To investigate this, we propose Contextual Preference Gain (CPG), a simple metric that measures how much a model's preference strengthens when relevant context is provided. We find that higher CPG consistently corresponds to lower hallucination, yet standard DPO and its variants exhibit only limited CPG, indicating that they underutilize contextual information and thus remain prone to hallucination. To address this, we propose Context-Calibrated DPO (C$^2$-DPO), which directly maximizes CPG while preserving the original preference ordering. Across multiple benchmarks, C$^2$-DPO substantially reduces hallucination without compromising general reasoning, relatively reducing the Object HalBench hallucination rate of Qwen2-VL-Instruct-2B by 36%. Code is available at

</details>

### [55] [Reinforcing Step-level Reasoning for Effective Self-Correction in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11573)
*Vu Duc Anh, Nhat M. Hoang, Do Xuan Long, Cong-Duy Nguyen, Ponhvoan Srey, Luu Anh Tuan*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 的自我纠错能力（验证并修正自身错误）仍是根本性挑战，现有方法在逐步推理与自我纠错方面效果有限。

Method: 提出 Self-Fix Step-DPO（SFS-DPO）两阶段框架：第一阶段通过逐步偏好优化强化逐步推理；第二阶段显式训练模型进行自我验证与自我纠错。另提出教师辅助变体 SFS-DPO-R，引入错误验证的解释性理由以提供更强的纠错信号。

Result: 在多个 LLM 上的域内与域外评测中，SFS-DPO 与 SFS-DPO-R 一致优于既有逐步训练基线；分析显示自我纠错频率与有效性均有提升。

Conclusion: 强化逐步推理对提升 LLM 自我纠错的鲁棒性能至关重要，SFS-DPO 系列方法验证了该思路的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文关注 LLM 的逐步推理强化与自我纠错，属于推理与思维链范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM reasoning self-correction, not aligned with the user's continuous multimodal, video generation, or world model priorities. Signals: LLM reasoning, self-correction, step-level preference optimization, DPO, no multimodal/video/world model keywords

Abstract: Achieving effective self-correction, where models verify and correct their own mistakes, remains a fundamental challenge for large language models (LLMs). In this work, we propose Self-Fix Step-DPO (SFS-DPO), a reinforcement learning based, two-stage framework for step-level self-verification and self-correction. The first stage strengthens step-level reasoning via step-level preference optimization, while the second stage explicitly trains models to self-verify and self-correct. We further introduce a teacher-assisted variant, SFS-DPO-R, which incorporates explanatory rationales for error verification to provide stronger corrective signals. Comprehensive in-domain and out-of-domain evaluations across multiple LLMs demonstrate that SFS-DPO and SFS-DPO-R consistently outperform prior step-level training baselines. Our analysis further reveals improvements in self-correction frequency and effectiveness, highlighting the importance of strengthening step-level reasoning for robust performance.

</details>
