TL;DR: 本文提出 Social Chain of Thought (SCoT)，一种将多智能体交互结构化为协作式审议框架的多轮医学鉴别诊断管线。实验表明其召回优势无法仅靠单体推理复现，且在最难诊断病例中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学诊断推理是 LLM 的高影响力应用场景，复杂病例的鉴别诊断常需整合多种专科推理。现有多智能体医学诊断方法虽已存在，但尚不清楚何时需要此类系统、为何有效、以及在何处优于单体推理。

Method: SCoT 将多智能体交互组织为多轮协作式审议框架，模拟医学鉴别诊断方法论，通过多轮专科对话逐步收敛到高召回率的鉴别诊断列表。作者将其与单智能体基线、单智能体管线消融及 best-of-n 扩展进行对比评估。

Result: SCoT 的召回优势无法通过单体推理（包括 best-of-n 扩展）复现；在最难的诊断病例中，多轮专科对话有助于恢复真实诊断并收敛到更高召回率的鉴别诊断。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作式推理在复杂医学诊断中具有独特价值，其优势源于结构化多轮交互而非简单的采样扩展，为可解释、透明的医学 LLM 诊断系统提供了设计依据。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Social Chain of Thought (SCoT)，一种基于多智能体协作的医学鉴别诊断推理框架，属于 LLM 推理与思维链（CoT）范畴。虽然面向医学领域而非多模态，但核心是推理与思维链方法，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链 (multimodal_reasoning_cot) 最为贴切。

Importance reason: Medical multi-agent reasoning is outside your core directions and priority subtopics, with no overlap in keywords. Signals: multi-agent, medical diagnosis, chain of thought, differential diagnosis, LLM reasoning

Abstract: Medical diagnostic reasoning is a high-impact use case for LLMs that carries significant implications for the health and wellbeing of users. When OpenAI (2026) reports that more than 5% of ChatGPT messages globally are healthcare-related, the transparency of these systems becomes a serious design concern. This is especially true for complex cases, where differential diagnosis often requires integrating multiple forms of specialist reasoning. Existing work has proposed multi-agent approaches to medical diagnosis, but it remains unclear when such systems are needed, why they help, and where they outperform monolithic inference. We introduce Social Chain of Thought (SCoT),a multi-round pipeline for medical differential diagnosis that structures multi-agent interaction as a deliberative framework for collabora. tive LLM reasoning. Evaluating SCoT against single-agent baselines, one-agent pipeline ablations, and best-of-n scaling, we show that its recall advantage is not reproduced by monolithic inference alone. SCoT is most successful in the hardest diagnostic cases, where multiple rounds of specialist conversation help recover ground-truth diagnoses and converge on a higher-recall differential.

</details>


### [57] [Claim-Level Reliability Assessment for Efficient Test-Time Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11994)
*Sen Xu, Wei Wang, Shixi Liu, Jixin Min, Yingwei Dai, Zhibin Yin, Yirong Chen, Junlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (81.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Claim-Level Reliability Assessment (CLR)，一种无需训练的测试时推理扩展框架，将推理轨迹压缩为决策关键声明，通过语义证伪（寻找单一决定性缺陷）而非完整构造来评估可靠性，在四个 LLM 和四个推理基准上以更少 token 提升 pass@1 和 self-consistency。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法通常通过额外采样更多解决方案来提升推理可靠性，但整条轨迹评估会因常规 token 的信号稀释而掩盖关键错误。同时，在固定模型能力下生成完全正确的解决方案本身很困难，需要更高效的验证策略。

Method: CLR 框架包含两步：(1) 将每条推理轨迹压缩为一组决策关键声明（claims），隔离逻辑锚点；(2) 采用语义证伪策略，利用"构造有效解需要完美推理路径，而反驳错误声明只需找到一个决定性缺陷"的不对称性，通过非线性可靠性评分压缩高置信错误轨迹的生存空间，抑制错误共识。

Result: 在四个 LLM 和四个推理基准上，在匹配预算下 CLR 普遍优于 pass@1 和 self-consistency。例如在 GPT-OSS-20B/CMIMC25 上，CLR 比 pass@1 提升 27.15 个百分点，并将 self-consistency 准确率从 77.50% 提升到 82.19%，同时减少 37.0% 的 token。

Conclusion: 声明级证伪是测试时扩展的有效原则，通过将计算从额外采样重定向到针对性验证，可以在更少 token 预算下显著提升推理可靠性，为高效测试时推理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 论文提出测试时推理扩展（test-time scaling）框架 CLR，通过声明级证伪来提升推理可靠性，属于推理时计算扩展与推理可靠性方向，映射到 CANONICAL 子主题"多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot），该子主题涵盖推理时计算扩展。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM reasoning efficiency rather than the user's core continuous/multimodal generation and world-model directions. Signals: test-time scaling, reasoning efficiency, claim-level verification, self-consistency, training-free

Abstract: We propose claim-level falsification as a principle for test-time scaling and instantiate it through Claim-Level Reliability Assessment (CLR), a training-free framework that reallocates test-time compute from additional solution sampling to targeted verification. Since whole-trace evaluation often obscures decisive errors due to signal dilution from routine tokens, CLR condenses each reasoning trace into a compact set of decision-critical claims, thereby isolating its logical anchors. Furthermore, recognizing the inherent difficulty of generating entirely correct solutions under fixed model capabilities, CLR shifts the focus to semantic falsification. This approach exploits a fundamental asymmetry between solution construction and claim refutation. Constructing a valid solution requires a flawless reasoning path, whereas refuting an incorrect claim requires identifying only a single decisive flaw. This targeted search for negative evidence systematically compresses the survival space of high-confidence incorrect traces, effectively suppressing erroneous consensus via nonlinear reliability scoring. Across four LLMs and four reasoning benchmarks under matched budgets, CLR generally improves upon pass@1 and self-consistency. On GPT-OSS-20B/CMIMC25, for instance, CLR exceeds pass@1 by 27.15 percentage-points and raises self-consistency accuracy from 77.50\% to 82.19\% with 37.0\% fewer tokens.

</details>


### [58] [Self-Evolving Code-with-Image Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11292)
*Tianze Yang, Liang Wu, Ruitong Sun, Yucheng Shi, Yanqiao Wang, Mayank Darbari, Ninghao Liu, Jin Sun, Liangjie Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (85.35) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Code-with-Image 范式：多模态模型仅凭 Python 解释器，通过编写并执行真实视觉算法代码来解决视觉推理任务，程序本身即推理。作者设计了免训练的反思循环（self-evolving reflection loop），让模型从自身失败程序中学习、用构造性真值测试修复并沉淀为可迁移技能。在自建的 CwI-Bench（30 个任务族）上，GPT-5.6-luna 从无工具 CoT 的 <30% 提升到裸解释器 43%、技能进化后 67%；开源 27B 模型从 9% 提升到 56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 thinking-with-images 范式主要用工具暴露视觉证据，但推理仍停留在语言层面，无法真正执行多步视觉算法。许多视觉问题并非受限于感知，而是需要逐像素执行多步视觉算法，语言能描述算法却无法运行算法，导致模型即使命名正确算法仍答错。因此需要让模型用代码真正执行视觉算法，将推理从语言转移到可执行的程序上。

Method: 提出 Code-with-Image 范式：模型仅凭 Python 解释器，用代码实现真实视觉算法求解任务。核心是免训练的自我进化反思循环（self-evolving reflection loop）：模型研究自己失败的代码程序，利用构造性 ground truth 测试修复方案，保留通过测试的技能作为可移植技能（portable skills）。这些技能是纯文本形式，可跨模型规模与任务族迁移。同时构建 CwI-Bench 基准，包含 30 个由隐藏视觉计算诱导的任务族，具有不重叠的学习与评测划分。

Result: 在 CwI-Bench 上，GPT-5.6-luna 在无工具 CoT 下低于 30%，使用裸解释器达到 43%，通过自身可执行反思进化技能后达到 67%。开源 27B 模型从 9% 提升到 33%（裸解释器）再到 56%（技能进化）。技能为纯文本形式，可跨规模与任务族迁移。

Conclusion: 当代码承载推理时，调试代码即调试推理。通过让模型用代码执行视觉算法并自我反思进化技能，可以显著提升多模态模型在感知非瓶颈的视觉推理任务上的表现，且技能可迁移、无需训练。

Classification reason: 论文研究多模态模型通过编写代码执行视觉算法来解决视觉推理任务（Code-with-Image），核心是多模态视觉推理与思维链/推理能力，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）最为贴切。

Importance reason: Novel code-as-reasoning paradigm with self-evolving reflection, but tangential to the user's core continuous/multimodal generation and world-model focus. Signals: code-with-image reasoning, self-evolving reflection loop, visual algorithm execution, CwI-Bench, training-free skill transfer

Abstract: Multimodal models increasingly reach for tools when solving visual tasks (crop, zoom, rotate, brighten), a paradigm known as thinking-with-images. The central challenge is one of perception: tools mostly serve to expose visual evidence, reasoning over that evidence stays in language, and most targets are ones a human could in principle determine by inspection. Some visual questions, however, are not bottlenecked by perception: recovering their answers requires executing a multi-step visual algorithm over the pixels. On such questions a model often names the correct algorithm at once yet still answers wrong, because language can describe an algorithm without being able to run one. Code-with-Image crosses that line: given nothing but a Python interpreter, the model must implement a genuine visual algorithm in code to solve the task; the program itself becomes the reasoning. The bottleneck then shifts from executing code to deciding which algorithm to implement. So we let the model teach itself: a training-free reflection loop studies its own failed programs, tests repairs against constructive ground truth, and keeps what survives as portable skills. On our Code-with-Image Bench (CwI-Bench), thirty task families induced by hidden visual computations with disjoint learning and evaluation splits, even GPT-5.6-luna stays below 30% with tool-free chain of thought; given a bare interpreter it reaches 43%, and with skills evolved through its own executable reflection, 67%. The open 27B model climbs the same ladder (9% $\rightarrow$ 33% $\rightarrow$ 56%), and the skills are plain text, transferable across scales and families. When code carries the reasoning, debugging code becomes debugging reasoning.

</details>


### [59] [GeoBridge: Decoupled Semantic Conditioning for Generative Image Geolocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11838)
*Zhiyang Dou, Xumeng Han, Fengde Peng, Zipeng Wang, Moxuan Zhao, Zhipei Huang, Zhenjun Han*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (73.63) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GeoBridge 提出一种角色解耦的条件机制，将冻结的语义 MLLM 连接到冻结的黎曼流匹配头，在球面上生成坐标，用于生成式图像地理定位。通过将语义监督与条件接口解耦，避免角色冲突，在 IM2GPS3K 上显著提升定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在图像地理定位中主要改进地理线索的推理，但将推理解码为坐标的方式滞后。预测地名再调用地理编码 API 是离散且有损的，忽略了图像证据并将多粒度语义折叠为粗粒度查找。作者认为瓶颈已从"模型推理什么"转移到"推理如何被表示为连续、几何感知的解码器输入"。

Method: GeoBridge 采用角色解耦的条件机制：将冻结的语义 MLLM 连接到冻结的黎曼流匹配头，后者在球面上生成坐标。核心挑战是角色冲突——用离散语义标签监督条件会使其表征偏向类别判别性几何，与生成头所需的平滑流形相悖。GeoBridge 将语义监督与条件接口解耦，用独立的投影形成冻结头所需的连续条件，注入地理先验而不干扰球面解码器。

Result: 在 IM2GPS3K 上，GeoBridge 在 25/200/750 km 阈值下达到 38.67/52.89/70.37，优于地名到 API 的流水线和推理增强的直接预测方法。

Conclusion: GeoBridge 是解码侧的算法贡献，与思维链推理正交且互补，展示了将语义推理解码为连续几何感知坐标的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的图像地理定位推理，将语义推理解码为连续坐标，属于多模态推理范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链。

Signals: flow matching on sphere, role-decoupled conditioning, frozen MLLM + generative head, image geolocalization, cs.CV

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced image geolocalization mainly by improving how they reason about geographic cues. How that reasoning isdecoded into coordinates, however, has lagged behind. Predicting a place name for a geocoding API is discrete and lossy: it ignores image evidence and collapses multi-granular semantics into a coarse lookup. We argue that the bottleneck has shifted from what a model reasons to how that reasoning is represented for a continuous, geometry-aware decoder. We present GeoBridge, a role-decoupled conditioning mechanism that connects a frozen semantic MLLM to a frozen Riemannian flow-matching head that generates coordinates on the sphere. The central obstacle is arole conflict: supervising the condition with discrete semantic labels biases its representation toward class-discriminative geometry, at odds with the smooth manifold the generative head requires. GeoBridge keeps the semantic supervision decoupled from the condition interface: a separate projection forms the continuous condition the frozen head expects, injecting geographic priors without disturbing the spherical decoder. On IM2GPS3K, GeoBridge reaches 38.67/52.89/70.37 at the 25/200/750 km thresholds, improving over a place-name-to-API pipeline and reasoning-augmented direct prediction at these precision-relevant scales. GeoBridge is a decode-side algorithmic contribution, orthogonal and complementary to chain-of-thought reasoning. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>

*Zile Zhou, Huining Yuan, Weichen Zhang, Xinlei Chen, Xiao-ping Zhang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在稳健空间推理上存在关键瓶颈。基于可验证结果的强化学习方法在中间推理步骤上存在信用分配不佳的问题，而结构化推理方法又忽视了深度感知等关键 3D 理解维度。

Result: SCOUT-3B 在通用空间基准和复杂空间推理任务上分别比基线提升 16.85% 和 6.3%；SCOUT-7B 超越 GPT-4o 4.28%；尽管仅用单图像训练，SCOUT-7B 仍展现出对多图像和视频场景的稳健跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: SCOUT 通过结构化思维链与多目标过程奖励强化学习的结合，显著提升了 VLM 的空间推理能力，并展现出良好的跨域泛化性，是迈向下一代空间感知 VLM 的关键一步。

Classification reason: 论文提出结构化思维链（Structured CoT）框架与多目标过程奖励强化学习，用于增强 VLM 的空间推理能力，属于多模态推理与思维链方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] multimodal_reasoning_cot。

Importance reason: Solid spatial reasoning RL method, but it is not aligned with your core continuous-model, video-generation, or world-model priorities. Signals: spatial reasoning, structured CoT, process reward RL, VLM, 3D perception

Abstract: Existing Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibits a critical bottleneck in robust spatial reasoning. Recent reinforcement learning (RL) methods aim to close this gap with verifiable outcomes, yet they suffer from poor credit assignment across intermediate reasoning steps. Concurrently, structured reasoning approaches overlook the critical depth perception necessary for comprehensive 3D understanding. To address these challenges, we propose SCOUT (Structured Chain-Of-Thought Utilizing Process-Supervised RL Training). Specifically, we design a structured Chain-of-Thought (CoT) framework that explicitly models 3D environmental perception to ensure robust spatial understanding and reasoning. Furthermore, we introduce a novel RL algorithm featuring multi-objective process rewards and a tailored advantage estimation method, facilitating fine-grained credit assignment across distinct segments of the reasoning trajectory. To support our framework, we develop SCOUT-24k, a structured spatial reasoning CoT dataset synthesized through a customized pipeline. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that SCOUT-3B improves upon baseline models by 16.85% and 6.3% on general spatial benchmarks and complex spatial reasoning tasks respectively. Notably, our larger SCOUT-7B even outperforms GPT-4o by a margin of 4.28%. Moreover, despite being trained exclusively on single image, SCOUT-7B exhibits robust out-of-domain generalization to multi-image and video scenarios. These empirical results render SCOUT as a critical step towards next generation of spatially-aware VLMs.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态检索传统上依赖双编码器对比学习模型，而Gemini Embedding 2作为首个原生多模态嵌入模型将文本、图像、视频、音频和文档映射到统一空间，同时前沿LLM也展现出强大的视觉理解能力。作者希望回答：LLM能否作为有效的零样本排序器与原生多模态嵌入竞争？

Method: 在Flickr30k数据集上构建硬负样本文本到图像检索任务，直接对比Gemini Embedding 2（原生多模态嵌入）与GPT-4.1、Claude Sonnet 4.6（LLM视觉排序）的检索性能，同时评估嵌入预计算后的延迟特性。

Result: GPT-4.1和Claude Sonnet 4.6在检索性能上与Gemini Embedding 2持平；但嵌入一旦预计算完成，多模态嵌入模型在低延迟应用场景中更具优势。

Conclusion: 前沿LLM具备与原生多模态嵌入模型相当的零样本视觉排序能力，但多模态嵌入模型凭借预计算特性更适合低延迟检索应用，两者在不同场景下各有优势。

Classification reason: 论文研究文本到图像检索任务，对比原生多模态嵌入模型与LLM视觉排序能力，属于跨模态检索范畴，映射到[CANONICAL]多模态检索子主题。

Importance reason: 多模态检索对比研究，但未涉及用户优先的连续语言模型、视频生成或世界模型方向，且LLM与嵌入模型对比结论较常规。 Signals: 多模态检索, 硬负样本, Gemini Embedding 2, LLM零样本排序, 低延迟嵌入

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval and classification across different types of media, spanning text, images,video and audio, has traditionally relied on dual-encoder models that align visual and textual representations through contrastive learning. The March 2026 release of Gemini Embedding 2, Google's first natively multimodal embedding model to map text, images, video, audio, and documents into a single shared space, raises competition among multimodal retrieval systems. Simultaneously, frontier Large language models (LLMs) have also demonstrated strong visual understanding, raising the question of whether they can serve as effective zero-shot rankers. Our study provides the first direct comparison of native multimodal embeddings against LLM-based visual ranking on Flickr30k. We observe that GPT-4.1 and Claude Sonnet 4.6 perform on par with Gemini Embedding 2. Additionally, once embeddings are precomputed, multimodal embeddings are better suited for low-latency applications.

*Ruoxi Zhao, Maziar Raissi*

TL;DR: Backtrader-Bench 是一个评测 LLM 编码智能体在算法交易中表现的基准框架，包含确定性 MCQ 生成管道和生成器-求解器过滤管道，通过可执行代码验证答案并自动挖掘更难的问题，评测了 11 个无工具模型和 4 个带工具配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评测 LLM 编码智能体在算法交易中的表现具有挑战性：静态基准存在数据污染风险，而数值回测输出需要真实代码执行作为 ground truth。现有评测方法难以可靠衡量智能体的真实能力。

Method: 提出 Backtrader-Bench 框架，包含两条互补管道：(1) 确定性 MCQ 管道，从五种交易策略、33 个模板和三个难度等级的回测配置生成问题，并由独立检查器重新推导每个答案；(2) 生成器-求解器过滤管道，由生成器编写可执行代码验证的问题并转换为 MCQ，丢弃无工具求解器无需代码执行即可回答的问题。

Result: 在 30 题精选集上，工具增强智能体单次通过达到 90.0% 准确率（GPT-5.5 和 Opus 4.7），比最佳无工具基线（10 次运行平均 73.0%）高出 17 个百分点。在 38 个单独挖掘的问题上，无工具准确率进一步下降，一半模型降至接近随机水平（25%）。

Conclusion: Backtrader-Bench 提供了可扩展、抗污染的算法交易智能体评测框架，其 MCQ 基础设施还可用于生成强化学习训练语料，最终目标是构建面向量化交易工作流的专用智能体。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Backtrader-Bench，一个用于评测 LLM 智能体在算法交易任务上表现的基准框架，属于评测基准类工作，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。

Signals: LLM agent benchmark, algorithmic trading, MCQ generation, tool-augmented agents, no overlap with priority subtopics

Abstract: Evaluating LLM coding agents in algorithmic trading is difficult because static benchmarks risk data contamination and numerical backtest outputs require ground truth from actual code execution. We present Backtrader-Bench, a framework with two complementary pipelines. A deterministic multiple-choice question (MCQ) pipeline generates questions from backtest configurations across five trading strategies, 33 templates, and three difficulty tiers, with an independent checker that re-derives every answer. A generator-solver filtering pipeline autonomously mines harder questions: a generator writes questions verified by executable code, converts them to MCQs, and discards any that a no-tool solver can answer without code execution. We evaluate 11 models without tools (10 runs each) and four with-tools configurations on a 30-question curated set. Tool-augmented agents reach 90.0% accuracy in a single pass (GPT-5.5 and Opus 4.7), outperforming the best no-tools baselines (73.0%, averaged over 10 runs) by 17 percentage points. On 38 separately mined questions, no-tools accuracy drops further, with half the models falling to roughly random-chance level (25%). Beyond evaluation, the scalable MCQ infrastructure is designed to produce a training corpus for reinforcement learning, with the ultimate goal of building a specialized agent for quantitative trading workflows.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 免费聊天转录仅覆盖关键角色档案点的 73.74%，而 TRACE Bench 在更少轮次内达到 99.91% 覆盖率；鲁棒性实验显示在重复运行和 User Agent 替换下排名稳定；在 26 个模型上报告总体排名、能力分解和清单轨迹。

Conclusion: TRACE Bench 通过任务驱动的清单化评测，实现了角色扮演模型评测的可解释、可追溯与高覆盖率，并支持闭环基准演化以持续提升评测可靠性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TRACE Bench，一个任务驱动的角色扮演智能体清单评测框架，属于评测基准与评测协议研究，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。

Importance reason: This paper is an evaluation benchmark for roleplay agents, which is outside the user's configured research priorities in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: roleplay evaluation, agentic checklist, benchmark, no continuous multimodal, no video generation

Abstract: Roleplay evaluation should do more than assign a single score: it should reveal which role requirements were tested, which failed, and which dialogue evidence supports the judgment. We propose TRACE Bench, a task-driven agentic checklist evaluation framework. It decomposes each role profile offline into a fixed checklist, then uses a User Agent to converse naturally with the target roleplay model while privately updating checklist states from model responses. Scores therefore trace back to checklist items and supporting dialogue turns rather than a black-box holistic impression. For coverage cross-validation, we audit released M2 free-dialogue transcripts from the MiniMax Role-play Benchmark against the same role-derived checklist. The released free-chat transcripts cover only 73.74% of key role-profile points, whereas TRACE Bench reaches 99.91% coverage in fewer turns. Robustness experiments show stable rankings under repeated runs and User Agent replacement. Across 26 models, TRACE Bench reports overall rankings together with capability breakdowns and checklist traces. It also supports Closed-Loop Benchmark Evolution, distilling verification methods proven effective in failed traces so later evaluations can more reliably elicit and examine observed failure modes.

</details>

Result: 建立了纵向医学影像差异报告基准，验证了 change-aware 指标的有效性，并展示了 DeltaMed 基线模型在直接配对 CT 差异报告任务上的表现。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CT-ΔBench，一个面向纵向 3D 医学影像差异报告的评测基准，并设计了 change-aware 评测指标与医生验证，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] multimodal_benchmark。

Importance reason: Medical imaging benchmark with no overlap with the user's priority directions in continuous/multimodal generation or world models. Signals: medical CT benchmark, longitudinal difference reporting, vision-language models, patient-level split, change-aware metrics

Abstract: In medical imaging, the clinical value of Computed Tomography (CT) lies not only in depicting current disease status, but crucially in enabling longitudinal comparison of serial scans to determine disease evolution, a process that underpins response assessment, recurrence detection, and ongoing patient management. Yet, despite this central role of temporal comparison in clinical decision-making, existing medical foundation models remain largely confined to single-study understanding, leaving temporally grounded cross-examination insufficiently addressed. To address this gap, we study longitudinal imaging difference reporting, a task in which a model takes two temporally separated scans from the same patient and generates a clinically meaningful report describing interval changes between them. We introduce CT-$\Delta$Bench, a dedicated benchmark for this task with patient-level splitting to prevent information leakage. To better evaluate this task beyond surface-level text similarity, we further develop change-aware metrics specifically designed to capture clinically meaningful longitudinal changes, and conduct an independent physician validation to assess the reliability of the synthesized references and event extraction pipeline. We also compare direct paired-CT reasoning with an indirect two-stage pipeline that first generates single-timepoint reports and then performs textual differencing. Finally, we propose DeltaMed, a baseline model for direct paired-CT difference reporting, and train it on the benchmark training set. Together, these contributions lay the groundwork for temporally aware medical foundation models that better reflect real-world longitudinal clinical reasoning.

</details>


### [65] [Benchmarking LLM Judges for Mobile Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11434)
*Ziqiang Wan, Li Gu, Zhixiang Chi, Zhi Liu, Seyed Mehdi Ayyoubzadeh, Yuanhao Yu, Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MobileJudgeBench，一个用于系统评测 LLM-as-judge 方法在移动智能体轨迹上可靠性的基准，包含 931 条人工标注轨迹，覆盖 6 个移动智能体基准、4 个智能体模型和 68 个应用。实验发现简单基线 judge 与专用方法相当甚至更优，LLM 骨干是主要驱动因素；基准质量指标能可靠预测真实 judge 效用；不同 LLM 骨干呈现相反（保守/宽松）的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动智能体基准日益依赖 LLM-based judge 来评估任务完成情况，但这些 judge 在移动智能体轨迹上的可靠性尚未被系统检验，缺乏专门的评测基准。

Method: 构建 MobileJudgeBench 基准，包含 931 条人工标注轨迹，覆盖 6 个移动智能体基准、4 个智能体模型和 68 个应用。评测 6 种 judge 方法（SPA-Bench 改编的 5 种、A3 两种模式、AndroidArena、AgentRewardBench 及一个简单基线），跨多个 LLM 后端进行实验，并进行失败分析。

Result: 三个关键发现：1) 带采样截图的简单基线 judge 与专用方法相当甚至更优，说明更复杂的 judge 流程并不持续提升质量，LLM 骨干是主要驱动因素；2) 基准质量指标能可靠预测真实 judge 效用，与智能体排序保真度和作为奖励信号的 RL 下游性能相关；3) 两个 LLM 后端的失败分析揭示相反（保守 vs 宽松）的失败画像，与骨干的精确率-召回率特性相关。

Conclusion: 移动智能体评测中，简单 judge 基线往往足够且更优，LLM 骨干选择比 judge 流程设计更重要；基准质量指标可作为 judge 效用的可靠预测器；不同 LLM 骨干的失败模式差异显著，需根据应用场景谨慎选择。

Classification reason: 论文提出 MobileJudgeBench，一个系统评测 LLM-as-judge 方法在移动智能体轨迹上可靠性的基准，属于多模态/智能体评测基准范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准 (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM judge reliability for mobile agents, which is outside the user's core continuous/multimodal generation and world-model priorities. Signals: LLM-as-judge, mobile agent evaluation, benchmark, judge reliability, multimodal understanding

Abstract: Mobile agent benchmarks increasingly rely on LLM-based judges to evaluate task completion, yet the reliability of these judges on mobile agent trajectories remains largely unexamined. We introduce MobileJudgeBench, a benchmark for systematically evaluating LLM-as-judge methods on mobile agent trajectories. Our benchmark comprises 931 human-annotated trajectories spanning 6 mobile agent benchmarks, 4 agent models, and 68 apps. We evaluate 6 judge methods (five adapted from SPA-Bench, A3 with two modes, AndroidArena, and AgentRewardBench, plus a simple baseline we design) across multiple LLM backends. Our experiments reveal three key findings. First, a simple baseline judge with sampled screenshots is competitive with, and often exceeds, purpose-built methods, indicating that more elaborate judge pipelines do not consistently improve judge quality; among competitive methods, the LLM backbone is the primary driver. Second, benchmark quality metrics reliably predict real-world judge utility: they correlate with both agent ranking fidelity for evaluation and downstream performance when judges serve as reward signals for on-policy reinforcement learning. Third, failure analysis across two LLM backends uncovers qualitatively opposite failure profiles, one conservative and the other permissive, linked to the backbone's precision-recall characteristics.

</details>

TL;DR: FrontierFinance 是一个面向金融投资智能体的开放评测基准，包含 220 个专家级查询和 11,543 条带来源标注的评分细则，覆盖投资者工作流的六个关键用例。评测发现工具框架比模型本身更影响质量与效率，最佳系统（Samaya 内部系统）达到 56.0%，领先最强前沿模型 Claude Fable 5（49.2%）且成本低约 2.2 倍。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: FrontierFinance 比现有公开金融基准更广更难，揭示了金融智能体评测中工具框架的重要性，并表明开源模型在成本效益上具有竞争力。数据集与评分代码已公开。

Importance reason: Finance benchmark with no overlap with your continuous multimodal, video generation, or world model priorities. Signals: finance benchmark, agent evaluation, tool harness matters, no multimodal generation, no video/world model

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly deployed for professional investment research, yet no benchmark captures the complexity of the full investor workflow. Existing benchmarks mainly target financial data extraction, a narrow slice that current models have largely saturated, while reference-based metrics and generic LLM-as-a-judge scoring fall short on the open-ended, long-form answers that real analyst queries demand. We introduce FrontierFinance, a fully open benchmark of 220 expert-crafted queries and 11,543 source-attributed rubrics spanning six crucial use cases across the full investor workflow. FrontierFinance is both broader and harder than existing public finance benchmarks. Evaluating frontier models and agent systems under a common harness restricted to publicly available data, we find that the tool harness, not the model alone, strongly shapes quality and efficiency; that Samaya's in-house system leads at 56.0%, ahead of the strongest frontier model (Claude Fable 5, 49.2%) at roughly 2.2x lower cost; and that the best open-weight model (Kimi K3, 46.4%) nearly matches the best proprietary model at 4.5x lower cost. Screening & Discovery and Sector, Industry & Macro remain the hardest use cases across all systems, where even the best systems reach only 33% and 39%. We make the dataset and grading code publicly available.

</details>


### [67] [Who Thinks Best Depends on How Long You Let Them: Budget-Dependent Rankings in LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12150)
*Rodrigo Guedes de Souza, Alison R. Panisson*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (51.58) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文挑战了 LLM 评测中"模型排名跨推理条件稳定"的假设，通过改变 token 生成预算（64-4096）在三个推理基准上评估四个模型（共 56,476 次推理），发现 3-19% 的样本呈现非单调行为、模型排名随预算反转、模型间存在互补性（oracle 增益最高 +27.8pp），并提出预算感知路由器可捕获 14.1% 的 oracle 差距，主张采用预算条件化的评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 LLM 评测假设模型排名在不同推理条件下保持稳定，但实际中 token 生成预算（最大可生成 token 数）可能显著影响模型表现。作者质疑这一假设，希望系统研究预算变化如何影响模型排名稳定性，从而推动更严谨的评测协议设计。

Method: 在七个预算水平（64-4096）上，对四个模型在三个推理基准上进行 56,476 次推理评估。通过控制截断效应分析非单调行为，使用 McNemar 检验验证排名反转的统计显著性，进行 oracle 分析量化模型互补性，并训练预算感知路由器来预测最优模型选择。

Result: (i) 3-19% 的样本呈现非单调行为（更多预算反而降低准确率），且该现象具有模型特异性（跨模型重叠仅 6-14%）；(ii) 所有基准上模型排名随预算反转（p<0.01）；(iii) oracle 分析显示模型互补性最高达 +27.8pp，在受限预算下最显著；(iv) 预算感知路由器跨域捕获 14.1% 的 oracle 差距，域内预算特征带来 +1.6 到 +5.7pp 提升但具有域特异性，跨域迁移反而下降 -1.2pp。

Conclusion: 模型排名并非跨预算稳定，token 生成预算应被视为评测中的关键条件变量。作者主张采用预算条件化的评测协议，并指出预算感知路由虽在域内有效但缺乏跨域泛化能力，需要进一步研究。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 LLM 评测中 token 生成预算对模型排名稳定性的影响，提出预算条件化评测协议，属于评测协议与评估方法研究，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。

Importance reason: Solid but niche LLM evaluation methodology paper with no direct connection to the user's continuous multimodal, video generation, or world model priorities. Signals: LLM evaluation, budget-dependent rankings, non-monotone accuracy, model complementarity, budget-aware router

Abstract: Standard evaluation of large language models assumes stable model rankings across inference conditions. We challenge this assumption by varying the token generation budget, i.e., the maximum tokens a model may produce, across seven levels (64--4,096), evaluating four models on three reasoning benchmarks (56,476 inferences). We report four findings: (i) 3--19% of items exhibit non-monotone behavior (accuracy decreasing with more budget), even after controlling for truncation, and this phenomenon is model-specific (cross-model overlap: 6--14%). (ii) Model rankings reverse across budgets on all benchmarks ($p {<} 0.01$, McNemar). (iii) Oracle analysis reveals model complementarity up to $+27.8$pp, most pronounced at constrained budgets. (iv) A budget-aware router captures 14.1% of the oracle gap cross-domain; budget features help within-domain ($+1.6$ to $+5.7$pp) but are domain-specific and hurt transfer ($-1.2$pp). These results argue for budget-conditioned evaluation protocols.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 评测框架难以在从封闭式事实查询到开放式解释请求的多样化用户查询上提供一致、细粒度的诊断，缺乏查询无关且无参考的评测能力。

Method: Q-CARE 将查询分解为子查询、答案分解为原子声明，建立基于查询覆盖度和声明可验证性的统一评测原则，提出覆盖感知的检索指标（C-Prec@k、C-nDCG@k）和声明级生成指标（Completeness、Conciseness、Verifiableness）。

Result: 在覆盖八个数据集的人工标注基准上，Q-CARE 与人类判断的相关性高于四种现有 RAG 评测指标，包括 RAGEval 和 RAGChecker。

Conclusion: Q-CARE 作为可靠、自动化的 RAG 评测框架，能够跨多样化查询类型提供一致且细粒度的评测，验证了查询覆盖度与声明可验证性作为统一评测原则的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Q-CARE，一个查询无关且完全无参考的 RAG 评测框架，通过查询覆盖度与声明可验证性进行细粒度评估，属于检索增强生成（RAG）评测方法。虽然 RAG 评测不完全等同于多模态评测，但该论文属于生成模型/LLM 的评测协议与自动评估范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）最为贴切。

Importance reason: RAG evaluation framework is outside the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generative world models. Signals: RAG evaluation, reference-free metrics, query coverage, claim verifiability, retrieval-augmented generation

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation improves the factuality of large language models by grounding responses in retrieved evidence, yet existing evaluation frameworks struggle to provide consistent, fine-grained diagnostics across the diverse spectrum of user queries, ranging from close-ended fact-seeking to open-ended explanatory requests. We propose Q-CARE, a query-agnostic and fully reference-free framework that enables fine-grained assessment by decomposing queries into sub-queries and answers into atomic claims. Q-CARE establishes a unified evaluation principle based on query coverage and claim verifiability, yielding coverage-aware retriever metrics (C-Prec@k, C-nDCG@k) and claim-level generator metrics (Completeness, Conciseness, and Verifiableness). On a human-annotated benchmark spanning eight datasets, Q-CARE achieves higher correlation with human judgments than four existing RAG evaluation metrics, including RAGEval and RAGChecker, proving its effectiveness as a reliable, automated evaluation framework. Code and data are publicly available at .

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机航拍图像理解与推理对空中智能至关重要，但现有评测分散在单个数据集和狭窄任务上，缺乏统一的无人机理解与推理能力评估框架，且现有 MLLM 在该领域表现存在明显不足。

Method: 构建 UAVQA-Bench 基准（1500 个标注 QA 对，13 个数据集，6 个能力维度，16 个任务，含多选题与视觉定位格式）；系统评测多种 MLLM 与 agent 系统识别失败模式；提出 UAV-MAS 免训练多智能体系统，包含领域特定感知引擎（DSPE）路由查询到合适视觉工具、上下文感知迭代精化模块（CAIR）验证中间推理抑制错误累积、难度感知自适应搜索机制（DAAS）根据问题难度调整搜索深度。

Result: UAV-MAS 搭配 32B 开源 MLLM 在 UAVQA-Bench 上达到 77.0% 总体准确率，超越 Gemini 3 Pro 4.0%；8B 变体相比其基础模型提升 8.7%。

Conclusion: 无人机航拍图像理解需要专门的评测基准与系统设计；免训练多智能体架构通过领域工具路由、迭代精化与自适应搜索可显著提升 MLLM 在无人机场景下的理解与推理能力。

Classification reason: 论文构建了 UAVQA-Bench 无人机航拍图像理解与推理基准，并系统评测多种 MLLM 与 agent 系统，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] multimodal_benchmark。

Importance reason: UAV benchmark and multi-agent system are outside your core continuous/multimodal generation and world-model focus, with no direct methodological novelty for your priorities. Signals: UAV benchmark, multi-agent system, training-free, MLLM evaluation, domain-specific tools

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM)-based UAV aerial image understanding and reasoning is essential for aerial intelligence yet poses distinct challenges arising from extreme scale variation, arbitrary camera orientations, and high object density. Despite growing interest, existing evaluations remain fragmented across individual datasets and narrow tasks, leaving a critical gap in unified assessment of UAV understanding and reasoning capabilities. To fill this gap, we construct UAVQA-Bench, a benchmark of 1,500 human-annotated QA pairs drawn from 13 public UAV datasets, covering 6 capability dimensions and 16 tasks in both multiple-choice and visual grounding formats. Systematic evaluation of a broad range of open-source and closed-source MLLMs as well as agent-based systems on UAVQA-Bench identifies three key failure modes: domain-toolset mismatch, unchecked error propagation, and static reasoning. Motivated by these findings, we propose UAV-MAS, a training-free multi-agent system for MLLM-based UAV aerial image understanding and reasoning, comprising a Domain-Specific Perception Engine (DSPE) that routes queries to task-appropriate visual tools, a Context-Aware Iterative Refinement module (CAIR) that validates intermediate reasoning to curb error accumulation, and a Difficulty-Aware Adaptive Search mechanism (DAAS) that adjusts search depth to question difficulty. UAV-MAS with a 32B open-source MLLM achieves 77.0% overall accuracy on UAVQA-Bench, surpassing Gemini 3 Pro by 4.0\%, while the 8B variant improves 8.7\% over its base model.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出古代汉字训诂（ACCE）这一视觉-语言问答任务，并构建了首个大规模专家审核的 VQA 训练数据集 JieZi-Dataset（50万+问答对）与评测基准 JieZi-Bench，涵盖字形识别、字形分析、字义训诂与历时演变四个递进层级。实验表明现有 MLLM 在基础识别上表现良好，但在字形分析、语义推理与历时理解上仍有困难，微调后各层级均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古代汉字训诂需要整合视觉观察、语言分析与历史语境，但现有计算方法仅聚焦于字符识别、检索等子任务，缺乏支持全面学术分析的结构化数据集与基准。

Method: 提出 ACCE 任务，将训诂过程建模为四层递进的 VQA 任务（基础字形识别、字形分析、字义训诂、历时演变分析）。构建 JieZi-Dataset：通过专家设计的模板与源文本引用约束生成以减少事实错误，并在关键阶段进行人工审核，共含50万+问答对。构建 JieZi-Bench：由人类专家构建并验证，参考答案取自权威辞书且与训练数据分离。

Result: 实验表明当前多模态大语言模型在基础识别上表现良好，但在字形分析、语义推理与历时理解上表现不佳；在 JieZi-Dataset 上微调后，四个层级的性能均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了古代汉字训诂领域缺乏结构化数据集与基准的空白，为学术级汉字分析提供了首个大规模专家审核资源，并揭示了现有 MLLM 在深层字形与语义推理上的不足。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了古代汉字训诂的 VQA 数据集 JieZi-Dataset 与评测基准 JieZi-Bench，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_benchmark（多模态评测与基准）。

Importance reason: Specialized OCR/VQA benchmark for ancient Chinese characters, far outside the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: expert-audited dataset, VQA benchmark, ancient Chinese exegesis, MLLM evaluation, fine-tuning gains

Abstract: The scholarly exegesis of ancient Chinese characters demands integrating visual observation, linguistic analysis, and historical context. However, existing computational approaches focus narrowly on subtasks such as character recognition and retrieval, lacking the structured datasets and benchmarks required for comprehensive scholarly analysis. To address this limitation, we introduce Ancient Chinese Character Exegesis (ACCE), a vision-language question answering (VQA) task that models the scholarly exegesis process. ACCE is organized into four progressive levels: basic character identification, glyph-form analysis, meaning exegesis, and diachronic evolution analysis. To support this task, we construct two complementary resources. JieZi-Dataset is the first large-scale, expert-audited VQA training dataset for ACCE, comprising over 500K QA pairs. It is constructed via a pipeline that reduces factual errors by constraining generation with expert-designed templates and source-text references. Human verification is further applied at each key stage to ensure scholarly accuracy. JieZi-Bench is an evaluation benchmark aligned with the exegesis process, constructed and verified by human experts to ensure evaluation reliability. It consists of four levels with reference answers curated from authoritative lexicographic works held separate from the training data. Experiments on multimodal large language models show that current models perform well on basic identification but struggle with glyph analysis, semantic reasoning, and diachronic understanding. Fine-tuning on JieZi-Dataset substantially improves performance across all four levels. Code and dataset are available at .

</details>

TL;DR: Apodex Discovery 是一个面向"发现式 AI"（discoverative AI）的评测框架，通过重型求解器（heavy-duty solver）在真实世界问题（如 AAV 衣壳设计、药物重定位）上进行可验证的长期调查，并引入 HDS6 评测协议，将 AI 评测从预定义基准推向可验证的发现任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型在问题、工具和成功标准被明确指定时能解决困难任务，但现实世界中的重大挑战很少以可执行或可验证的形式出现。作者类比阿波罗登月计划，认为 AI 需要从"解决已定义问题"转向"发现式"能力，即自主进行扩展的、有状态的、可验证的调查。

Method: 提出 Apodex Discovery 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 问题侦察过程，调查 16 个行业 561 个产业，汇编 423 个高价值现实问题并选出 20 个发布；(2) 统一的环境-任务-情节（environment-task-episode）抽象，提供数据、工具、约束、反馈、轨迹记录以及中间产物和最终提交的验证；(3) HDS6 评测协议，独立于最终任务成功与否，评估 Tools、Repair、Alternatives、Coherence、Evidence、Scope 六个维度。

Result: 在 AAV 衣壳设计中，Apodex 在可行性、趋向性、结构预测和生成设计上超越已发表的最先进水平 7%。在药物重定位与再配方任务中，任务特定的生物医学环境使 GPT-5.5 和 GPT-5.6-sol 的平均归一化预测分数分别提升 2.5 和 7.6 分。受控消融实验表明固定的 TRACES 情节接口能够将性能差异归因于特定求解器组件。

Conclusion: Apodex Discovery 将 AI 评测从预定义基准推向可验证的发现式调查，为构建和评估能够自主进行长期、有状态、可验证研究的 AI 系统提供了框架和基准。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Apodex Discovery 框架，用于构建和评测"发现式 AI"，包含问题侦察、环境-任务-情节抽象以及 HDS6 评测协议，核心贡献在于新的评测基准与评测环境，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on a broad discovery-AI benchmark with biomedical applications, far from the user's core continuous multimodal and video generation priorities. Signals: benchmark, discovery AI, drug repurposing, AAV capsid, heavy-duty solver

Abstract: Apollo did not reach the Moon merely because its engineers could solve difficult equations. It succeeded by turning a distant ambition into a mission architecture of explicit objectives, simulation, verification, and repeated correction. AI now faces a similar transition: frontier models can solve difficult tasks once the problem, tools, and success criteria are specified, yet consequential real-world challenges rarely arrive in an executable or verifiable form. We introduce Apodex Discovery, a framework for building and evaluating discoverative AI through the heavy-duty solver, a system comprising a foundation model, harness, tools, and control policies that pursues extended, stateful, verifiable investigations. It has three core components. First, a problem-scouting process surveyed 561 industries across 16 sectors, assembled 423 high-value real-world problems, and selected 20 for the initial release. Second, a common environment-task-episode abstraction provides data, tools, constraints, feedback, trajectory recording, and verification of intermediate artifacts and final submissions. Third, HDS6 evaluates Tools, Repair, Alternatives, Coherence, Evidence, and Scope independently of final-task success. In AAV capsid design, Apodex surpassed the published state of the art by 7% across viability, tropism, structure prediction, and generative design. In drug repurposing and reformulation, a task-specific biomedical environment improved the mean normalized prediction score of GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.6-sol by 2.5 and 7.6 points over the same closed-book backbone. Controlled ablations show that the fixed TRACES episode interface enables attribution of performance differences to specific solver components. Apodex Discovery moves AI evaluation beyond predefined benchmarks toward verifiable investigations aimed at genuine discovery.

</details>


### [72] [EnterpriseRAG: Benchmarking LLM Instruction Adherence and Robustness under Non-Ideal Enterprise Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11584)
*Huiqi Miao, Xinbao Sun, Bo Wang, Fanyu Meng, Lijun Mei, Na Wu, Di Jin, Chao Deng, Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnterpriseRAG 是一个包含 983 个专家验证样本的企业级 RAG 评测基准，系统模拟检索噪声、知识缺口和事实冲突三种生产环境失败模式，评测 13 个 SOTA LLM 发现指令遵循崩溃现象：单约束满足率高但整体合规率仅 26.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 评测假设干净检索与简单查询，无法反映生产环境中噪声文档与多维约束并存的条件。论文指出 LLM 虽能满足 80% 的单个约束，但仅 26.8% 的响应同时满足所有要求，存在 57 个百分点的编排差距，亟需一个能模拟真实生产失败模式的评测基准。

Method: 构建 EnterpriseRAG 基准，包含 983 个专家验证样本，覆盖六个领域，系统模拟三种此前工作未涉及的失败模式：检索噪声、知识缺口和事实冲突，并与复杂指令相结合。对 13 个 SOTA LLM 进行系统评测，分析指令遵循的逐约束与整体合规表现。

Result: 评测揭示严重的指令遵循崩溃现象：高单约束满足率掩盖了低整体合规率。在知识缺口和事实冲突场景下，即使采用增强推理的推理方式，模型仍面临深层障碍，表明生产级 RAG 需要显式的上下文感知协议与校准判断。

Conclusion: EnterpriseRAG 为衡量和弥合企业级 RAG 系统的可靠性差距提供了可复现的基础，直接为大规模企业 RAG 部署决策提供参考。基准与评测框架将在发表后公开。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 EnterpriseRAG 基准，用于评测 LLM 在企业检索场景下的指令遵循与鲁棒性，属于评测基准类工作，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。

Importance reason: Solid benchmark for enterprise RAG robustness, but outside the user's core focus on continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: RAG benchmark, instruction adherence, retrieval noise, knowledge gaps, factual conflicts

Abstract: Enterprise RAG deployments face a critical reliability gap: while LLMs satisfy 80% of individual constraints, only 26.8% of responses meet all requirements simultaneously, revealing a 57-point orchestration gap. Existing benchmarks assume clean retrieval with simple queries, failing to capture production conditions where noisy documents and multi-dimensional constraints coexist. We introduce EnterpriseRAG, a benchmark of 983 expert-validated samples across six domains that systematically simulates three failure modes absent from prior work: retrieval noise, knowledge gaps, and factual conflicts, coupled with complex instructions. Evaluation of 13 state-of-the-art LLMs reveals a severe instruction adherence collapse, where high per-constraint satisfaction masks low holistic compliance. Critical findings expose deep barriers under knowledge gaps and factual conflicts, even with reasoning-enhanced inference, indicating production RAG requires explicit context-aware protocols and calibrated judgment. EnterpriseRAG provides a reproducible foundation for measuring and closing these gaps, directly informing deployment decisions for enterprise-scale RAG systems. We will release the benchmark and evaluation framework upon publication.

</details>


### [73] [Do You See What You Draw? A Semantic Closed-Loop Framework for Holistic Evaluation of Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11907)
*Hao Zhang, Jiaxin Qi, Zhijiang Tang, Jianqiang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 高优先级 (89.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Self-Generative-Understanding (SGU)，一个无需标注的语义闭环评测框架，通过让统一多模态模型先理解图像生成文本描述、再基于描述重建视觉上下文、最后对自生成输出进行推理，从而整体评测 UMMs 的理解与生成一体化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测协议将生成与判别能力作为独立任务分别评测，缺乏对统一多模态模型（UMMs）系统级整体能力的评测方法，无法揭示理解与生成一体化中的协同缺陷。

Method: SGU 框架构建语义闭环：模型先感知图像并生成文本描述，再基于该描述重建视觉上下文，最后对自生成输出进行推理。整个过程无需额外标注，形成零成本测试平台，输出综合性能分数以评测 UMMs 作为统一系统的能力。

Result: 实验表明，即使是高性能的 UMMs 也常常难以对自身生成的上下文进行推理，揭示了单独评测理解或生成能力时无法发现的局限性。

Conclusion: SGU 提供了互补的整体评测框架，为下一代统一多模态模型的基准测试发展奠定基础，强调系统级评测对理解与生成一体化模型的重要性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SGU 框架，用于对统一多模态模型（UMMs）进行整体评测，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 multimodal_benchmark 子主题。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel annotation-free closed-loop evaluation for unified multimodal models, directly relevant to your unified multimodal and continuous multimodal priorities. Signals: unified multimodal evaluation, semantic closed-loop, self-generative-understanding, annotation-free benchmark, multimodal understanding and generation

Abstract: As Large Vision-Language Models increasingly aim to integrate visual generation and understanding within a single parameter space, evaluating such structural unification in a cohesive manner remains a critical challenge. Current evaluation protocols predominantly treat generative and discriminative capabilities as separate tasks, leaving a gap in system-level evaluation for unified multimodal models (UMMs). In this work, we propose Self-Generative-Understanding (SGU), a novel, annotation-free evaluation framework that probes the integrated capabilities of unified models through a semantic closed-loop challenge. Without requiring new annotations, SGU leverages the dual understanding-and-generation abilities of UMMs by asking them to first perceive an image and produce a textual description, subsequently reconstruct a visual context based on that description, and finally perform reasoning over the self-generated output. This pipeline provides a zero-cost testbed that yields an integrated performance score specifically tailored for evaluating UMMs as unified systems. Extensive experiments show that even high-performing UMMs often struggle to reason over their own generated contexts, revealing limitations that are not captured by separate evaluations of understanding or generation alone. Our work provides a complementary holistic evaluation framework and offers a foundation for benchmarking the development of next-generation unified multimodal models.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 MBA-Bench，首个面向真实世界商业创意生成的多模态基准，包含 30K 样本、六个领域，并训练了 MBA-b 和 MBA-k 两个代理，分别针对隐藏/公开评测标准，通过 LoRA SFT 和 GRPO 优化，显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的智能体商业创意生成局限于纯文本范式，忽略了真实世界场景固有的多模态特性。缺乏一个多模态基准来训练和评估商业创意生成代理。

Method: 构建 MBA-Bench 基准（30K 样本、六领域），自动为图像生成字幕并用 GPT-4o 通过检索查询生成、市场证据检索和证据增强合成生成参考创意。提出 MBA-b（盲评）和 MBA-k（已知标准）两种设置，分别用创造性和可行性两个奖励目标以及六个公开标准共八个目标，通过 LoRA SFT 加 GRPO 训练。

Result: MBA-b 和 MBA-k 分别比字幕基线提升 63.9% 和 77.1%，比多模态基线提升 25.6% 和 35.8%，多模态基线在多项指标上接近闭源模型性能。

Conclusion: 多模态输入对商业创意生成至关重要，基于多模态基准训练并针对特定评测标准优化的代理能显著提升创意生成质量。

Classification reason: 论文提出 MBA-Bench，一个用于商业创意生成的多模态评测基准，并训练了基于 MLLM-as-a-Judge 的评测代理。这属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "multimodal_benchmark"。

Importance reason: Benchmark for business ideation agents is tangential to your core focus on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: multimodal benchmark, business ideation, MLLM-as-a-Judge, LoRA SFT, GRPO

Abstract: Agentic systems powered by large language models (LLMs) have opened new opportunities for business ideation. Yet existing approaches remain confined to a text-only paradigm, despite the inherently multimodal nature of real-world contexts. We thus introduce MBA-Bench, the first multimodal benchmark for training and evaluating business ideation agents, comprising 30K samples across six domains, each domain characterized by distinct visual cues not fully conveyed by text alone. Concretely, we automatically caption images and employ GPT-4o to generate five reference ideas for each of three business questions through retrieval query generation, market evidence retrieval, and evidence-augmented synthesis. Following prior work, we evaluate agents across six business-oriented criteria using MLLM-as-a-Judge. To consider settings where criteria are hidden or disclosed, we present MBA-b and MBA-k for blind and known, respectively. We train both with two novel reward objectives---creativity and feasibility---while MBA-k further optimizes the six disclosed criteria for eight in total. Both are trained via LoRA-based supervised fine-tuning followed by group relative policy optimization with these setting-specific rewards. For extensive experiments on MBA-Bench, we set up two baselines accommodating either captions only or multimodal inputs, with the latter nearing closed-source performance on several metrics. MBA-b and MBA-k outperform caption baselines by 63.9% and 77.1%, and multimodal baselines by 25.6% and 35.8%, respectively.

</details>


### [75] [Auditing Frame-Level AUC in Weakly Supervised Video Anomaly Detection: Granularity, Resolution, and Scene Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11985)
*Sara Abdulaziz, Egor Bondarev*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文审计弱监督视频异常检测（WSVAD）中帧级AUC评测协议，指出pooled AUC在粒度、分辨率和场景偏差三方面存在缺陷：不能可靠预测视频内异常定位、在测试集规模下无法支撑SOTA差距、且对场景/录制属性敏感。作者提出一种粒度感知的评测协议并公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帧级AUC是WSVAD的主流评测指标，但其标准形式（pooled AUC）跨测试视频聚合帧对，同时衡量事件定位与视频源差异，可能掩盖模型在视频内异常定位上的真实差异，导致评测结果不可靠。

Method: 在UCF-Crime上对多个SOTA模型（覆盖不同backbone家族）进行审计：固定帧分数，在三种配对粒度（全局、按异常类别、视频内）下重新读取AUC，并在模型内部表征上计算零样本分数重复三粒度读取；用配对视频bootstrap评估排序可靠性；在正常片段上检验场景敏感性。

Result: 三个发现：1) pooled AUC不能可靠预测视频内异常定位，相似pooled分数的模型在更严格粒度下出现大差异和排序反转；2) 在测试集规模下pooled AUC缺乏分辨率支撑SOTA差距，backbone家族内无法区分，而视频内AUC能区分；3) 所有模型在正常片段上都能按录制属性（分辨率、颜色编码）分离视频，表明场景敏感性是共享的而非架构特有。

Conclusion: 现有pooled AUC评测协议存在粒度、分辨率与场景偏差问题，需要更严格的粒度感知评测协议来可靠评估WSVAD模型的真实定位能力。作者公开了可计算的粒度感知协议。

Classification reason: 该论文审计弱监督视频异常检测（WSVAD）的帧级AUC评测协议，属于评测方法与基准分析，映射到多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on evaluation protocol auditing for video anomaly detection, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: evaluation metric audit, video anomaly detection, pooled AUC, granularity bias, scene sensitivity

Abstract: Frame-level area under the ROC curve (AUC) is the dominant evaluation metric for weakly supervised video anomaly detection (WSVAD). Its standard form measures whether an anomalous frame outranks a normal frame drawn from anywhere in the test set. We refer to this comparison as pooled AUC, since it aggregates frame pairs across test videos regardless of source. Pooled AUC therefore credits both event localization and differences between video sources. We audit this protocol on UCF-Crime across recent state-of-the-art models spanning different backbone families. Holding each model's frame scores fixed, we read them under three pairing granularities: global, per anomaly category, and within each video, then repeat the same three-granularity readout on zero-shot scores computed from the models' internal representations. We assess ranking reliability with a paired video bootstrap. Three findings follow. First, pooled AUC does not reliably predict within-video anomaly localization: models with similar pooled scores exhibit large localization differences and rank reversals under stricter granularities. Second, at the benchmark's test-split size, pooled AUC lacks the resolution to support state-of-the-art margins reported in the field. Within each backbone family, it resolves no comparison at those margins, while within-video AUC resolves several over identical predictions. Learned representations further reveal that within-video anomaly structure and detector localization are decoupled. Third, on normal footage alone, every model we examine separates videos by recording properties, such as resolution and color encoding, indicating that scene sensitivity is shared across the setting rather than specific to any architecture. We publicly release a granularity-aware protocol computable from existing predictions and scene-factor annotations for UCF-Crime.

</details>


### [76] [SCOPE-Router: Cost-Aware Open-Set VLM Routing for Execution-Oriented Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12127)
*Tao Yu, Yifei Qu, Zhiqing Cui, Pengfei Zhou, Zhongtian Luo, Yujia Yang, Shenghua Chai, Haopeng Jin, Zhenghao Zhang, Xinming Wang, Hongzhu Yi, Wangbo Zhao, Zhenglin Wan, Yan Huang, Yeshani, Jinwen Luo, Yang You*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SCOPE-Router，一个面向执行导向任务（代码、Agentic、搜索）的成本感知开放集 VLM 路由框架，并配套提出 VLM-ExecRouterBench 基准与 CRM+RCCR 成本感知训练目标，在 OOD 与双重 OOD 开放集评测下均取得最优 Rank Score。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 路由研究局限于传统 VQA 评测，缺乏面向开放集场景的系统性校准优化，且训练目标通过 softmax 归一化稀释了多正样本信号且未纳入成本因素。作者希望解决这些局限，构建更贴近真实执行场景的 VLM 路由方法。

Method: (1) 提出 VLM-ExecRouterBench，覆盖 Code、Agentic、Search 三个领域、11 个候选模型、价格跨度近两个数量级的执行导向路由基准；(2) 提出 SCOPE-Router 双塔路由器，通过混合校准（随机/诊断/多样性采样）构建查询与模型行为画像的匹配，新模型无需重训即可加入路由；(3) 提出 CRM+RCCR 架构无关的成本感知目标，通过逐对独立打分将成本偏好编码进连续相关性目标，消除多正样本稀释，并正则化具有相似路由偏好的查询在路由空间中更接近。

Result: SCOPE-Router 在三个基准上均取得最优 Rank Score，在 OOD 设置下超过第二名 1.84 分，在双重 OOD 开放集评测下超过 6.75 分。将 CRM+RCCR 应用于四种不同路由器时，Rank Score 提升 1.25–6.21 分。

Conclusion: SCOPE-Router 结合执行导向基准、混合校准的双塔路由架构与成本感知训练目标，显著提升了 VLM 路由在开放集与 OOD 场景下的质量-成本平衡能力，且具备良好的可扩展性。

Classification reason: 论文提出 VLM-ExecRouterBench，一个面向执行导向任务的 VLM 路由评测基准，并构建 SCOPE-Router 路由模型与成本感知训练目标。核心贡献之一是新的评测基准，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）高度相关，故复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM routing for execution tasks, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: VLM routing, cost-aware, open-set benchmark, execution-oriented tasks, model selection

Abstract: Model routing aims to select the most suitable model from a candidate pool for each query, balancing quality and cost. Existing VLM routing research is limited to traditional VQA evaluation, lacks systematic calibration optimization for open-set scenarios, and employs training objectives that dilute multi-positive signals via softmax normalization without incorporating cost. We address these limitations with three contributions: (1)VLM-ExecRouterBench, the first execution-oriented VLM routing benchmark covering Code, Agentic, and Search domains with 11 candidate models spanning nearly two orders of magnitude in pricing; (2)SCOPE-Router, a dual-tower router that matches queries to model behavior profiles constructed via hybrid calibration (random/diagnostic/diversity sampling), enabling new models to join routing without retraining; (3)CRM+RCCR, an architecture-agnostic cost-aware objective that encodes cost preference into continuous relevance targets through per-pair independent scoring, eliminating multi-positive dilution while regularizing queries with similar routing preferences to be closer in the routing space. Empirically, SCOPE-Router achieves the best Rank Score on all three benchmarks, surpassing the runner-up by 1.84 points under OOD settings and by 6.75 points under doubly OOD open-set evaluation. When applied to four diverse routers, CRM+RCCR improves Rank Score by 1.25--6.21 points.

</details>

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Graph-Structured Rubrics (GSR)，将自然语言评分标准编译为响应无关的类型化评估图，通过准则节点、变换/归约/门控算子与 Readout 输出映射来组合评判，从而提升 LLM 作为评判者的评分与偏好评估质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于评分标准（rubric）的评估器通常将评分标准视为提示上下文或扁平准则，只指定"评判什么"而将准则的组合方式隐式化，即使自然语言规则明确描述了组合逻辑。这导致 LLM 评判者难以准确遵循复杂的评分逻辑。

Method: GSR 将评分标准编译为响应无关的类型化评估图：准则节点负责获取判断，变换、归约与门控算子通过命名端口组合这些判断，Readout 将唯一汇节点转换为分数或偏好。编译过程会拒绝格式错误或类型不兼容的图。点式评估先分别评判各准则维度再图聚合；成对评估复用同一图，对每个候选在每个准则下各做一次判断。

Result: 在 GPT-OSS-120B 下，GSR 在四个点式数据集上相比 Prometheus 风格评分将精确分数一致性提升 0.62–6.75 个百分点，并在两个偏好基准上取得数值最高的端到端成对准确率（在原生平局与弃权策略下）。

Conclusion: 将评分标准显式编译为类型化评估图能够更忠实地表达准则组合逻辑，从而显著提升 LLM 评判者的评分一致性与偏好评估准确率，为可解释、可验证的自动评估提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Graph-Structured Rubrics (GSR)，将评分标准编译为类型化评估图以改进 LLM 作为评判者的评估质量，属于 LLM 自动评估与评测协议方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准 (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM evaluation methodology with no direct connection to the user's core continuous multimodal, video generation, or world model priorities. Signals: LLM judge, rubric compilation, evaluation graph, pointwise/pairwise scoring, no multimodal generation

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluators commonly treat rubrics as prompt context or flat criteria: they specify what to judge but leave criterion composition implicit, even when natural-language rules state it. We introduce Graph-Structured Rubrics (GSR), which compiles a rubric into a response-independent typed evaluation graph before observing responses. Criterion nodes elicit judgments; transformation, reduction, and gating operators compose them through named ports; and a task-specific output mapping, termed Readout, converts the unique sink into a score or preference. Compilation rejects malformed or type-incompatible graphs. Pointwise evaluation judges rubric dimensions separately before graph aggregation; pairwise evaluation reuses the graph with one judgment for each candidate under every criterion. Under GPT-OSS-120B, GSR improves exact score agreement by 0.62--6.75 percentage points over Prometheus-style scoring on four pointwise datasets and achieves the numerically highest end-to-end pairwise accuracy on two preference benchmarks under native tie and abstention policies.

</details>


### [78] [Diagram-MMU: A Multi-Modal Benchmark for Scientific Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12262)
*Weihao Bo, Shan Zhang, Yanpeng Sun, Jie Liu, Yongke Yao, Jinhao Du, Wei He, Kai Zou, Zechao Li, Jingdong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (74.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Diagram-MMU 是一个面向科学图表的多模态评测基准，包含 3.7k 图表和 18.3k 人工验证问题，覆盖六个领域，评估 MLLM 在图表转代码解析、图表转代码编辑和图表问答三项任务上的能力，并包含 agentic 设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 MLLM 在科学写作与协作场景（如 OpenAI Prism 将科学图表转为 LaTeX TikZ 代码）中的应用日益广泛，现有评测缺乏对科学图表解析与理解能力的系统评估，尤其是图表转代码这类任务。

Method: 构建 Diagram-MMU 基准，包含 3.7k 精选图表和 18.3k 人工验证问题，覆盖六个科学领域。设计三项任务：图表转代码解析、图表转代码编辑、图表问答，并为每项任务提供 agentic 设置。对 12 个 MLLM 进行系统评测。

Result: 评测发现图表转代码任务比图表问答更具挑战性：模型能较好地对图表进行推理，但在解析和编辑图表代码方面表现较弱。在 agentic 设置下，多数模型在解析和编辑任务上有所提升但在问答上退化，而 Claude-4.6 Opus 在三项任务上均持续提升。

Conclusion: Diagram-MMU 揭示了当前 MLLM 在科学图表解析与代码生成方面的能力短板，强调了开发增强 MLLM 图表转代码生成能力方法的必要性，为后续研究提供了评测基准。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了 Diagram-MMU 科学图表多模态评测基准，评估 MLLM 的图表解析、编辑与问答能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] multimodal_benchmark 子主题。

Importance reason: A useful but niche benchmark for diagram-to-code parsing, tangential to the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generative world models. Signals: benchmark, scientific diagrams, diagram-to-code, MLLM evaluation, agentic setting

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have been growing the capability for scientific writing and collaboration. For example, OpenAI Prism is a free workspace for scientific writing and collaboration. One important feature in Prism is turning scientific diagrams directly into LaTeX TikZ code. In this paper, we build a benchmark, Diagram-MMU, a multi-modal benchmark designed to assess MLLMs' ability for scientific diagram parsing and understanding. Diagram-MMU features 3.7k curated diagrams and 18.3k human-validated questions across six domains. It evaluates MLLMs on three tasks common in vibe writing workspaces: diagram-to-code parsing, diagram-to-code editing, and diagram question answering, alongside agentic settings per task. The evaluation of 12 MLLMs reveals that diagram-to-code tasks are more challenging than diagram question answering: models can reason well over diagrams but struggle to parse and edit them, underscoring the need for methods to enhance MLLMs' capability in diagram-to-code generation. Under agentic settings, most models improve parsing and editing performance but degrade on question answering, while Claude-4.6 Opus consistently improves across all three tasks. Project Page: .

</details>

### [79] [Generation as Auxiliary Supervision: Enhancing Visual Understanding at Zero Inference Overhead via Decoupled Embedding Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12209)
*Zhongbin Guo, Jiahao Xie, Dongling Xiao, Qianle Wang, Ruiqi Lu, Xiaomin He, Wanxuan Sun, Cheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (89.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GAS 提出一种生成引导的训练框架，将视觉生成重新解释为表征学习的辅助监督，通过解耦的 Mixture-of-Transformers 架构和 Next Embedding Prediction 范式，在不增加推理开销的情况下增强预训练 MLLM 的视觉理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中视觉理解与生成通常被视为相互独立的目标。现有统一框架依赖离散视觉 token 或扩散目标，其生成目标与视觉理解模型使用的连续表征不一致，难以直接迁移到已有预训练 MLLM 上增强其理解能力。

Method: GAS 采用解耦的 Mixture-of-Transformers（MoT）架构，共享底层主干并设置并行上层。通过 Next Embedding Prediction（NEP）作为跨模态生成范式，让生成损失丰富共享视觉通路的空间精度与视觉保持能力，同时屏蔽上层理解层免受生成梯度干扰。此外构造需要深度认知基础的强相关生成任务以最大化协同效应。训练后丢弃辅助生成分支，实现零推理开销。

Result: 在不同模型规模和训练阶段，GAS 提升了聚合多模态理解能力，在感知和空间理解任务上增益最稳定可靠。由于辅助生成分支在训练后被丢弃，这些增益不产生任何推理开销。广泛的受控对比和表征级分析进一步阐明了生成引导训练何时以及为何有益于理解。

Conclusion: GAS 验证了生成引导训练作为增强多模态理解的实用路径的可行性，展示了通过解耦架构将生成作为辅助监督来提升视觉理解的有效性，且不增加推理成本。

Classification reason: 论文提出 GAS 框架，将视觉生成作为辅助监督来增强 MLLM 的视觉理解，采用解耦的 Mixture-of-Transformers 架构和 Next Embedding Prediction 跨模态生成范式，属于理解与生成一体化的统一多模态模型方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] unified_multimodal_model。

Importance reason: Directly targets unified multimodal understanding with generation as auxiliary supervision, aligning with the user's priority on continuous/multimodal representation learning and zero-overhead efficiency. Signals: unified multimodal, mllm, continuous representation, next embedding prediction, zero inference overhead

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress, visual understanding and generation are typically treated as divergent objectives. Existing unified frameworks often rely on discrete visual tokenization or diffusion objectives whose generative targets differ from the continuous representations consumed by visual understanding models, making direct transfer to enhance existing pretrained MLLMs non-trivial. In this work, we present GAS, a generation-guided training framework that reinterprets visual generation as auxiliary supervision for representation learning. Concretely, GAS adapts Next Embedding Prediction (NEP) as a cross-modal generation paradigm within a decoupled Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture. By maintaining a shared lower trunk and parallel upper layers, GAS lets generation losses enrich the shared visual pathway with finer spatial precision and stronger visual retention while shielding the upper understanding layers from direct generation gradients. To maximize this synergy, we further construct highly correlated generation tasks that demand deep cognitive grounding rather than generic synthesis alone. Across model scales and training stages, GAS improves aggregate multimodal understanding, with its most reliable gains on perception and spatial comprehension. Crucially, because the auxiliary generation branch is discarded after training, these gains incur zero inference overhead. Extensive controlled comparisons and representation-level analyses further clarify when and why generation-guided training benefits understanding, and demonstrate the feasibility of generation-guided training as a practical route to stronger multimodal understanding.

</details>

### [80] [AVA-Encoder: Towards Agent-Native Video Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12313)
*Chuyue Li, Jinpeng Yu, Haozhe Wang, Tian Xueyun, Zhijing Zhang, Bingnan Li, Shuqi Gu, Kan Ren, Jiaming Liu, Ruihua Hua*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (80.15) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AVA-Encoder 提出一种面向智能体的视频自编码框架，将视频转换为知识图谱（KG）表示再重建回视频，通过文本梯度优化实现 agent 可理解、可查询、可编辑的视频表示学习，在重建基准上比最强外部基线提升 20.7 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创意智能体缺乏从高质量人类影片中学习的有效方式，难以生成电影级视频。核心挑战在于缺少一种既忠实于影片内容、又可直接用于智能体推理与操作的视频结构化表示。

Method: AVA-Encoder 通过 agentic auto-encoding 将视频转换为知识图谱（KG）表示再重建回视频。KG 的层级与状态节点存储结构化文本，链接的资产层保存生成的图像、音频和视频；类型化边保留文本描述与资产之间的关系，便于 agent 理解、查询和编辑。视频重建差异驱动文本梯度优化框架，将评估反馈表达为自然语言更新方向，用于外层循环的 Data-Independent Encoding Policy Pseudo-Training 和测试时内层循环的可选 Data-Dependent KG Representation Refinement。

Result: AVA-Encoder 相比最强外部基线提升 20.7 个百分点。在受控的仅策略设置下，其伪训练的镜头级 Agentic Video Encoder 策略优于人工精心调优的策略，同时系统提示 token 减少 74.3%。论文还发布了完整的 AVA-Encoder 框架、可靠的 agentic 视频重建基准以及首个高质量电影 KG 表示数据集。

Conclusion: AVA-Encoder 验证了将视频表示为知识图谱并重建回视频的 agentic auto-encoding 范式是可行的，能够学习到既忠实于影片内容又可直接用于智能体推理与操作的视频表示，为创意智能体从高质量影片中学习提供了有效途径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Agentic Video Auto-Encoder (AVA-Encoder)，将视频转换为知识图谱表示并重建回视频，用于智能体（agent）的视频理解与操作。这属于面向智能体的视频表示学习与多模态理解，与 [CANONICAL] vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）方向相关，因为其核心是构建 agent 可直接理解、查询和编辑的视频结构化表示，属于通用多模态理解与表示学习范畴。

Importance reason: Novel agent-native video representation via knowledge graphs, but tangential to the user's core continuous/multimodal generation focus. Signals: video representation learning, knowledge graph, agentic auto-encoding, text-gradient optimization, video reconstruction

Abstract: Creative agents still lack an effective way to learn from high-quality human films, limiting their ability to produce cinematic-grade videos. A key challenge is the absence of a structured video representation that is both faithful to film content and directly usable for agentic reasoning and manipulation. To address the challenge, we propose the Agentic Video Auto-Encoder (AVA-Encoder), a framework for learning agent-native video representations via agentic auto-encoding. AVA-Encoder transforms a video into a knowledge graph (KG) representation and then reconstructs it back into video. Its hierarchy and state nodes store structured text, while a linked asset layer holds generated images, audio, and video. Typed edges preserve the relations between these text descriptions and assets in a form that agents can easily understand, query, and edit. The video reconstruction differences drive a textual-gradient optimization framework, which expresses evaluation feedback as natural-language update directions for Data-Independent Encoding Policy Pseudo-Training in the outer loop and optional Data-Dependent KG Representation Refinement in the test-time inner loop. Extensive experiments show that AVA-Encoder improves by 20.7 percentage points over the strongest external baseline. In the controlled policy-only setting, its pseudo-trained shot-level Agentic Video Encoder policy also outperforms a carefully human-tuned policy while using 74.3% fewer system-prompt tokens. We release the complete AVA-Encoder framework, a reliable agentic video reconstruction benchmark, and the first dataset of high-quality film KG representations.

</details>


### [81] [Click2Poly: A VLM for vector mapping buildings and walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11424)
*Nicolas Girard, Jawher Ben Abdallah, Arno Gobbin, Liuyun Duan, Sacha Lepretre*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 普通相关 (64.37) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Click2Poly 是一个基于 Florence-2 VLM 扩展的人机交互 AI 助手，通过用户点击直接编辑建筑和墙体矢量图层，并以 QGIS 插件形式部署于真实生产环境，加速地理空间制图的矢量编辑流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑和墙体的精确矢量制图对地理空间应用至关重要，但人工标注过程劳动密集。现有深度学习方法虽能自动提取，但为满足制图标准仍需人工质检和修正复杂案例。作者希望加速这一人工编辑步骤。

Method: 扩展 Florence-2 视觉语言模型，使其能够响应用户点击并直接编辑建筑或墙体矢量图层。模型以用户点击位置为输入，输出对应的矢量编辑操作。整个系统实现为 QGIS 插件，集成到真实生产环境中。

Result: Click2Poly 在真实生产环境中加速了建筑和墙体矢量图层的人工编辑流程，作为 QGIS 插件实现了人机交互式矢量编辑。

Conclusion: 将 VLM 与交互式点击编辑结合，可以显著提升地理空间矢量制图中人工编辑环节的效率，展示了 VLM 在专业 GIS 生产工具中的实用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文基于 Florence-2 VLM 扩展，实现人机交互式矢量地图编辑，属于通用视觉语言模型的应用与微调，映射到 [CANONICAL] vlm_mllm_general 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on geospatial vector editing with a VLM, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: VLM for interactive editing, QGIS plugin, human-in-the-loop, geospatial mapping, Florence-2 extension

Abstract: Accurate vector mapping of buildings and walls is critical for geospatial applications but remains a labor-intensive process. While recent deep learning methods have improved automatic extraction, in order to meet cartographic standards they always require a human to perform quality control and fix complex cases in the extraction. We present Click2Poly, a human-in-the-loop AI assistant designed to speed up this manual step. Extending the Florence-2 Vision Language Model (VLM), Click2Poly responds to user clicks by editing the building or wall vector layer directly. Implemented as a QGIS plugin, Click2Poly speeds up the manual editing of building and wall vector layers in a real-world production environment.

</details>


### [82] [VOLA: Improving Open-World Driving by VLM-Based Semantic Attribute Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11777)
*Yuchen Zhang, Yuan Gao, Sebastian Schmidt, Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (79.48) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VOLA 提出将开放世界驾驶场景感知从类别标签转向稠密动作相关属性（可驾驶性与脆弱性），直接读取 Qwen3.5 图像 token 隐状态作为空间语义表征，配合轻量边界感知解码器生成全分辨率属性图，无需自回归文本生成或外部掩码模型，在真实场景与未见障碍物上优于视觉分割基线与提示式 VLM 分割器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界驾驶是开放世界的，车辆可能遇到训练数据之外的物体（如掉落的床垫、鹿等）。仅命名这些物体不够，系统需要知道如何对待每个区域：能否驶过、碰撞严重程度如何。因此需要从类别标签转向稠密、与动作相关的属性感知。

Method: 将场景感知形式化为稠密动作相关属性预测，每个像素标注应如何影响运动而非物体名称。实例化为两个有序属性：7 级可驾驶性和 5 级脆弱性。直接读取 Qwen3.5 图像 token 隐状态作为空间语义表征，用轻量边界感知解码器将粗 token 网格转为全分辨率属性图。整个过程无需自回归文本生成或外部掩码模型（如 SAM）。在 CARLA 构建的稠密属性标签上训练，并测试到真实场景和未见障碍物的迁移。

Result: 模型在熟悉类别上与强视觉分割器相当，在真实开放世界异常迁移上表现更好，达到 69.4% 的平均脆弱性等级召回率，优于最佳视觉基线（57.1%）和最佳提示式 VLM 基线（53.9%）。

Conclusion: VLM 图像 token 为将驾驶属性迁移到训练词汇之外的物体提供了有用的语义线索，验证了利用 VLM 隐状态进行稠密语义属性预测的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文利用 VLM（Qwen3.5）的图像 token 隐状态作为空间语义表征，进行驾驶场景的语义属性预测，属于视觉语言模型的应用与架构设计，映射到 [CANONICAL] vlm_mllm_general。

Importance reason: Uses VLM hidden states for dense spatial prediction, but the driving/autonomous-vehicle domain and attribute-mapping task fall outside your core continuous-multimodal and generation priorities. Signals: VLM hidden states as spatial representation, no autoregressive text generation, open-world driving attributes, transfer to real scenes, Qwen3.5 image tokens

Abstract: Driving in the real world is open-world: a car may encounter a fallen mattress, a deer, or other objects outside its training data. Naming them is not enough. The system must know how to treat each region: can it drive over it, and how severe would a collision be? We therefore shift scene perception from category labels to dense action-relevant attributes, where each pixel is labeled by how it should affect motion rather than by object name. We instantiate this general formulation with two ordered attributes: 7-rank drivability and 5-rank vulnerability. We read Qwen3.5 image-token hidden states directly as a spatial semantic representation. A lightweight boundary-aware decoder then turns this coarse token grid into sharp full-resolution attribute maps. The whole process requires neither autoregressive text generation nor an external mask model such as SAM. We train on dense attribute labels built in CARLA and test transfer to real scenes and to novel obstacles never seen in training. We compare with vision-only segmenters trained on the same attributes and prompted VLM segmenters. Our model matches strong vision-only segmenters on familiar categories and improves transfer to real open-world anomalies, reaching 69.4% mean vulnerability-rank recall versus 57.1% for the best vision-only baseline and 53.9% for the best prompted VLM baseline. These results show that VLM image tokens provide useful semantic cues for transferring driving attributes to objects outside the training vocabulary.

</details>


### [83] [Qwen-MusicAVQA-7B: A Multimodal Model for Music Audio-Visual QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11329)
*Maryam Dehdashti*

Main category: cs.SD, cs.CV, cs.MM

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (79.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Qwen-MusicAVQA-7B 通过冻结的 Whisper 编码器和线性投影将音乐音频接入 Qwen2-VL-7B，实现音乐音视频问答，在 MUSIC-AVQA 上达到 96.0% 准确率，并揭示音频表征保留的局部时序信息是下游准确率的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统为视觉语言模型添加音频能力通常需要训练或适配大型全模态系统，成本高昂。作者希望探索一种轻量级替代方案，仅通过冻结编码器和线性投影即可高效实现音乐音视频问答，并理解音频表征设计对下游性能的影响。

Method: 将冻结的 Whisper 编码器通过两个独立的线性投影分别处理视频音乐轨和 TTS 语音问题，与视觉帧一起输入 Qwen2-VL-7B-Instruct，利用预训练的自注意力机制融合多模态信息，无需任务特定的融合网络。采用两阶段训练，音频特征缓存以降低训练成本。

Result: 在 MUSIC-AVQA 的 7402 题可用视频测试子集上达到 96.0%±3.9% 准确率；在匹配 32 token 预算下，stride-pooled Whisper 帧序列比全局池化 PANNs 表征高 26 个百分点；在 MUSIC-AVQA-R 重述基准上保持 96.5%/95.6% 的高准确率；完整两阶段训练约 5 小时即可在单张 A100 80GB 上完成。

Conclusion: 轻量级适配方案（冻结编码器+线性投影）在音乐音视频问答上高度有效，且下游准确率主要取决于音频表征保留的细粒度局部时序信息，而非表征的全局语义丰富度或投影器规模。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了一个连接冻结 Whisper 音频编码器与 Qwen2-VL 的多模态模型，用于音乐音视频问答（AVQA），属于通用视觉语言模型/MLLM 架构与多模态指令微调范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] vlm_mllm_general。

Importance reason: Lightweight audio adaptation for MLLM is useful but tangential to your core continuous-model, autoregressive generation, and world-model priorities. Signals: multimodal understanding, audio-visual QA, frozen encoder, linear projection, temporal resolution insight

Abstract: A common approach to adding audio to a vision-language model is to train or adapt a large omni-modal system. We show that a lightweight alternative can be highly effective for music audio-visual question answering (AVQA). Qwen-MusicAVQA-7B connects a frozen Whisper encoder to Qwen2-VL-7B-Instruct through learned linear projections. The same frozen encoder processes both the video's music track and a TTS-spoken question through separate projectors, while the language model fuses visual frames, music, and question audio through pretrained self-attention, with no task-specific fusion network. On MUSIC-AVQA, our system reaches 96.0% +/- 3.9% accuracy across three independent training seeds on the 7,402-question available-video test subset. Our central finding is that downstream accuracy tracks how much fine-grained local temporal information the audio representation preserves. In a matched 32-token comparison, a stride-pooled Whisper frame sequence outperforms a globally pooled PANNs representation expanded to the same budget by 26 percentage points, even though PANNs sees at least as much audio and uses a far larger projector. The effect is not simply sequence versus vector: within Whisper alone, reducing temporal resolution at a fixed token budget costs a comparable amount. Under matched data and inputs, fine-tuned Qwen2.5-Omni-7B reaches 80.9%, against 95.9% for our 30 s variant; because the systems differ in backbone and adaptation, this is a system-level comparison. Accuracy remains high on sampled head and tail splits of the rephrased MUSIC-AVQA-R benchmark (96.5% and 95.6%). Because both encoders stay frozen and the music features are cached, the entire adaptation is cheap to train: the complete two-stage AVQA run takes approximately 5 hours on a single A100 80GB, and every run reported here fits on that one GPU.

</details>

### [84] [Gaze Target Estimation Anywhere with Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11367)
*Xu Cao, Houze Yang, Vipin Gunda, Zhongyi Zhou, Tianyu Xu, Adarsh Kowdle, Inki Kim, James M. Rehg*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Promptable Gaze Target Estimation (PGE) 任务，一种端到端、概念驱动的注视分析范式，通过文本或视觉提示（如"穿红衣服的男孩"）指定注视分析对象，并构建 Gaze-Co 数据集（120K 图像对）与 GazeAnywhere 模型，实现主体定位、帧内外判断与注视热图估计的联合求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注视目标估计方法依赖多阶段流水线，需要头部边界框、人体姿态等显式输入，检测误差会级联传播导致失败；且缺乏通过自然语言提示灵活指定注视分析任务的能力。作者希望克服这些局限，提出更灵活、端到端的概念驱动注视分析范式。

Method: 提出 PGE 任务，将注视预测条件化在灵活的用户文本或视觉提示上以识别特定注视分析主体。构建可扩展数据引擎生成 Gaze-Co 数据集（120K 提示标注图像对）。提出 GazeAnywhere 模型，采用基于 transformer 的检测器融合冻结编码器特征，同时求解主体定位、帧内外存在性与注视目标热图估计。

Result: GazeAnywhere 在多个 PGE 基准上取得最先进性能，即使在困难的域外真实临床数据集上也建立了该新问题的强基线。

Conclusion: 概念驱动的提示式注视目标估计是可行且有效的范式，GazeAnywhere 作为首个 PGE 模型为后续研究提供了强基线，其端到端设计避免了多阶段流水线的误差级联问题。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于自然语言/视觉提示的注视目标估计（PGE）任务，属于视觉理解与视觉问答范畴，可映射到 [CANONICAL] 的"视觉问答与文档理解"子主题。

Importance reason: Novel promptable gaze estimation task, but outside the user's core focus on continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: new task formulation, gaze estimation, promptable, dataset contribution, multimodal understanding

Abstract: Estimating human gaze targets from images in-the-wild is an important and formidable task. Existing approaches primarily employ brittle, multi-stage pipelines that require explicit inputs, like head bounding boxes and human pose, in order to identify the subject of gaze analysis. As a result, detection errors can cascade and lead to failure. Moreover, these prior works lack the flexibility of specifying the gaze analysis task via natural language prompting, an approach which has been shown to have significant benefits in convenience and scalability for other image analysis tasks. To overcome these limitations, we introduce the Promptable Gaze Target Estimation (PGE) task, a new end-to-end, concept-driven paradigm for gaze analysis. PGE conditions gaze prediction on flexible user text or visual prompts (e.g., "the boy in the red shirt" or "person in point [0.52, 0.48]") to identify a specific subject for gaze analysis. This approach integrates subject localization with gaze estimation, and eliminates the rigid dependency on intermediate analysis stages. We develop a scalable data engine to generate Gaze-Co (Gaze Estimation with Concepts), a dataset and benchmark of 120K high-quality, prompt-annotated image pairs. We also propose GazeAnywhere, the first model designed for PGE. GazeAnywhere uses a transformer-based detector to fuse features from frozen encoders and simultaneously solves subject localization, in/out-of-frame presence, and gaze target heatmap estimation. GazeAnywhere achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple PGE benchmarks, setting a strong baseline for this new problem even on a difficult out-of-domain, real-world clinical dataset. GazeAnywhere is open-sourced in .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇实例分割（OVIS）和开放集全景分割（OSPS）旨在无需详尽人工标注即可识别预定义和未见过的物体类别。现有方法常受限于噪声伪掩码、视觉-文本对齐不足，以及难以处理同义词和词汇表外（OOV）单词等问题。

Method: 提出一个多模态框架：1）在目标词汇辅助伪标签设置下，利用Grounded SAM、LLaVA和CLIP自动构建伪分割掩码、描述性标题和语义对齐的同义词集，提供无需人工标注的多模态监督；2）通过三种互补训练目标增强视觉-文本对齐：扩展的grounding损失（纳入视觉grounded同义词）、语义一致性损失和生成式标题重建损失。

Result: 在COCO数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在该协议下持续优于先前最先进方法，在OVIS和OSPS基准上均取得显著改进。

Conclusion: 利用多模态伪标签和视觉语言模型可以显著提升开放词汇分割的性能，有效缓解噪声伪掩码和同义词/OOV词处理问题，为无需人工标注的开放词汇分割提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦开放词汇实例分割与开放集全景分割，利用视觉语言模型（Grounded SAM、LLaVA、CLIP）生成伪标签并增强视觉-文本对齐，属于多模态理解与生成方向。虽然核心任务是分割，但方法核心是视觉语言模型驱动的伪标签生成与跨模态对齐，映射到多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉问答与文档理解（multimodal_vqa_doc）子主题，因其涉及视觉语言模型在视觉理解任务中的应用。

Importance reason: Focuses on open-vocabulary segmentation with pseudo-labeling, which is tangential to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: open-vocabulary segmentation, pseudo-labeling, vision-language alignment, COCO benchmark, multimodal supervision

Abstract: This work addresses the challenge of open-vocabulary instance segmentation (OVIS) and open-set panoptic segmentation (OSPS), which aim to recognize both predefined and unseen object categories without exhaustive human annotations. Existing methods often suffer from noisy pseudo-masks, limited visual-textual grounding, and difficulty handling synonyms or out-of-vocabulary (OOV) words. To overcome these challenges, we propose a multimodal framework that leverages pre-trained vision-language models for automatic pseudo-label generation, CLIP-guided synonym filtering, and GPT-based caption reconstruction. In our target-vocabulary-assisted pseudo-labeling setting, the framework first constructs pseudo segmentation masks, descriptive captions, and semantically aligned synonym sets using Grounded SAM, LLaVA, and CLIP, providing multimodal supervision without manual annotation. We then enhance visual-textual alignment through three complementary training objectives: an extended grounding loss that incorporates visually grounded synonyms, a semantic consistency loss, and a generative caption reconstruction loss. Extensive experiments on the COCO dataset demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art approaches under this protocol, achieving substantial improvements on both OVIS and OSPS benchmarks.

</details>
